




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


thairāpadānapāḷi

(dutiyau bhāgau)


43. sakiṃsammaj‍jakavaggau

1. sakiṃsammaj‍jakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘vipassinau  bhagavatau, pāṭaliṃ baudhimuttamaṃ|

disvāva taṃ pādapaggaṃ, tattha cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

2.

 ‘‘sammaj‍janiṃ gahaitvāna, baudhiṃ sammaj‍ji tāvadai|

sammaj‍jitvāna taṃ baudhiṃ, avandiṃ pāṭaliṃ ahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

namassamānau taṃ baudhiṃ, gañchiṃ paṭikuṭiṃ ahaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘tādimaggaina  gacchāmi, sarantau baudhimuttamaṃ|

ajagarau maṃ pīḷaisi, ghaurarūpau mahabbalau||

5.

 ‘‘āsan‍nai  mai kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaina tausayī mamaṃ|

kaḷaivaraṃ mai gilati, daivalaukai ramāmahaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘anāvilaṃ mama cittaṃ, visuddhaṃ paṇḍaraṃ sadā|

saukasal‍laṃ na jānāmi, cittasantāpanaṃ mama||

7.

 ‘‘kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilāsau ca, apamārau vitacchikā|

daddu kaṇḍu ca mai natthi, phalaṃ sammaj‍janāyidaṃ sammaj‍janai idaṃ (sī॰)||

8.

 ‘‘saukau  ca paridaivau ca, hadayai mai na vij‍jati|

abhantaṃ ujukaṃ cittaṃ, phalaṃ sammaj‍janāyidaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘samādhīsu  na maj‍jāmi samādhīsu na saj‍jāmi (sī॰), samādhiṃ puna paj‍jāmi (syā), visadaṃ hauti mānasaṃ|

yaṃ yaṃ samādhimicchāmi, sau sau sampaj‍jatai mamaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘rajanīyai na raj‍jāmi, athau dussaniyaisu dausaniyaisu (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) ca|

mauhanīyai na muyhāmi, phalaṃ sammaj‍janāyidaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau aikanavutai itau (sī॰ syā॰) kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phalaṃ sammaj‍janāyidaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

13.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sakiṃsammaj‍jakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sakiṃsammaj‍jakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aikadussadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

15.

 ‘‘nagarai  haṃsavatiyā, ahausiṃ tiṇahārakau|

tiṇahāraina jīvāmi, taina pausaimi dārakai||

16.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

tamandhakāraṃ nāsaitvā, uppaj‍ji laukanāyakau||

17.

 ‘‘sakai  gharai nisīditvā, aivaṃ cintaisi tāvadai|

‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, daiyyadhammau na vij‍jati||

18.

 ‘‘‘idaṃ  mai sāṭakaṃ aikaṃ, natthi mai kauci dāyakau|

dukkhau nirayasamphassau, raupayissāmi dakkhiṇaṃ’||

19.

 ‘‘aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

aikaṃ dussaṃ gahaitvāna, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘aikaṃ  dussaṃ daditvāna, uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ sampavattayiṃ|

‘yadi buddhau tuvaṃ vīra, tāraihi maṃ mahāmuni’||

21.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mama dānaṃ pakittaintau, akā mai anumaudanaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘‘iminā aikadussaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

kappasatasahassāni, vinipātaṃ na gacchasi||

23.

 ‘‘‘chattiṃsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissasi|

taittiṃsakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī cak‍kavatti (syā॰) bhavissasi||

24.

 ‘‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ asaṅkayaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) aivamuparipi|

daivalaukai manussai vā, saṃsarantau tuvaṃ bhavai||

25.

 ‘‘‘rūpavā guṇasampan‍nau, anavak‍kantadaihavā anuvattanta… (syā)|

akkhaubhaṃ amitaṃ dussaṃ, labhissasi yadicchakaṃ’||

26.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau|

nabhaṃ abbhuggamī vīrau dhīrau (sī॰ syā॰), haṃsarājāva ambarai||

27.

 ‘‘yaṃ  yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, aikadussassidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘paduddhārai  paduddhārai, dussaṃ nibbattatai mamaṃ|

haiṭṭhā dussamhi tiṭṭhāmi, uparicchadanaṃ mama||

29.

 ‘‘cak‍kavāḷaṃ upādāya, sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ|

icchamānau cahaṃ aj‍ja, dussaihacchādayaiyya taṃ||

30.

 ‘‘tainaiva aikadussaina, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇau hutvāna, saṃsarāmi bhavābhavai||

31.

 ‘‘vipākaṃ  viaikadussassa, nājjhagaṃ katthacikkhayaṃ|

ayaṃ mai antimā jāti, vipac‍cati idhāpi mai||

32.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aikadussassidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

34.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikadussadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikadussadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. aikāsanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, gausitau nāma pabbatau|

assamau sukatau mayhaṃ, paṇṇasālā sumāpitā||

37.

 ‘‘nāradau nāma nāmaina, kassapau iti maṃ vidū|

suddhimaggaṃ gavaisantau, vasāmi gausitai tadā||

38.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, agañchi anilañ‍jasā||

39.

 ‘‘vanaggai gacchamānassa, disvā raṃsiṃ mahaisinau|

kaṭṭhamañ‍caṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā, ajinañ‍ca apatthariṃ||

40.

 ‘‘āsanaṃ  pañ‍ñāpaitvāna, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

saumanassaṃ pavaiditvā, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

41.

 ‘‘‘sal‍lakattau mahāvīra, āturānaṃ tikicchakau|

mamaṃ raugaparaitassa rāga… (syā॰), tikicchaṃ daihi nāyaka||

42.

 ‘‘‘kal‍latthikā yai passanti, buddhasaiṭṭha tuvaṃ munai|

dhuvatthasiddhiṃ pappaunti, aitaisaṃ ajarau jaj‍jarau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhavai||

43.

 ‘‘‘na  mai daiyyadhammau atthi, pavattaphalabhaujihaṃ|

idaṃ mai āsanaṃ atthi na mai daiyyaṃ tava atthi (sī॰ syā॰), nisīda kaṭṭhamañ‍cakai’||

44.

 ‘‘nisīdi tattha bhagavā, asambhītauva acchambhitauva (syā॰ ka॰) kaisarī|

muhuttaṃ vītināmaitvā, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

45.

 ‘‘‘visaṭṭhau  vissatthau (sī॰ pī), vissaṭṭhau (syā॰ ka॰) hauhi mā bhāyi, laddhau jautirasau tayā|

yaṃ tuyhaṃ patthitaṃ sabbaṃ, paripūrissatināgatai paripūrissatāsanaṃ (syā॰ ka॰)||

46.

 ‘‘‘na maughaṃ taṃ kataṃ tuyhaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai|

sak‍kā uddharituṃ attā, yassa cittaṃ paṇīhitaṃ sunīhitaṃ (syā॰)||

47.

 ‘‘‘imināsanadānaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

kappasatasahassāni, vinipātaṃ na gacchasi||

48.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍ñāsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissasi|

asītikkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissasi||

49.

 ‘‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ  vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

sabbattha sukhitau hutvā, saṃsārai saṃsarissasi’||

50.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau|

nabhaṃ abbhuggamī vīrau, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

51.

 ‘‘hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, sarathaṃ sandamānikaṃ|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, aikāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘kānanaṃ pavisitvāpi, yadā icchāmi āsanaṃ|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, pal‍laṅkau upatiṭṭhati||

53.

 ‘‘vārimajjhagatau santau, yadā icchāmi āsanaṃ|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, pal‍laṅkau upatiṭṭhati||

54.

 ‘‘yaṃ  yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

pal‍laṅkasatasahassāni, parivārainti maṃ sadā||

55.

 ‘‘duvai  bhavai saṃsarāmi, daivattai atha mānusai|

duvai kulai pajāyāmi, khattiyai atha brāhmaṇai||

56.

 ‘‘aikāsanaṃ daditvāna, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai|

dhammapal‍laṅkamādāya dhammapal‍laṅkamañ‍ñāya (syā॰ ka॰), viharāmi anāsavau||

57.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aikāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

58.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

59.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikāsanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

aikāsanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. sattakadambapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

61.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, kuk‍kuṭau kadambau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) nāma pabbatau|

tamhi pabbatapādamhi, satta buddhā vasiṃsu tai||

62.

 ‘‘kadambaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, paggahaitvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

satta mālā gahaitvāna, puñ‍ñacittaina puṇṇacittaina (ka॰) aukiriṃ||

63.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

66.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

67.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattakadambapupphiyau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sattakadambapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. kauraṇḍapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

68.

 ‘‘vanakammikau purai āsiṃ, pitumātumatainahaṃ pitupitāmahainahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

pasumāraina jīvāmi, kusalaṃ mai na vij‍jati||

69.

 ‘‘mama āsayasāmantā, tissau laukagganāyakau|

padāni tīṇi dassaisi, anukampāya cakkhumā||

70.

 ‘‘ak‍kantai ca padai disvā, tissanāmassa satthunau|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, padai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

71.

 ‘‘kauraṇḍaṃ  pupphitaṃ disvā, pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ|

sakausakaṃ gahaitvāna, padasaiṭṭhamapūjayiṃ||

72.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

kauraṇḍavaṇṇakauyaiva, suppabhāsau bhavāmahaṃ||

74.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, padapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

76.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

77.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kauraṇḍapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kauraṇḍapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ghatamaṇḍadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

78.

 ‘‘sucintitaṃ  bhagavantaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

upaviṭṭhaṃ mahārañ‍ñaṃ, vātābādhaina pīḷitaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘disvā  cittaṃ pasādaitvā, ghatamaṇḍamupānayiṃ|

katattā  ācitattā ca, gaṅgā bhāgīrathī ayaṃ||

80.

 ‘‘mahāsamuddā cattārau, ghataṃ sampaj‍jarai mama|

ayañ‍ca pathavī ghaurā, appamāṇā asaṅkhiyā||

81.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, bhavatai madhusak‍karā|

cātuddīpā catuddisā (syā॰) imai rukkhā, pādapā dharaṇīruhā||

82.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, kapparukkhā bhavanti tai|

pañ‍ñāsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

83.

 ‘‘aikapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

84.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ghatamaṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ||

85.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

86.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ghatamaṇḍadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ghatamaṇḍadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. aikadhammassavaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

88.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

catusac‍caṃ pakāsaintau, santāraisi bahuṃ janaṃ||

89.

 ‘‘ahaṃ  taina samayaina, jaṭilau uggatāpanau|

dhunantau vākacīrāni, gacchāmi ambarai tadā||

90.

 ‘‘buddhasaiṭṭhassa upari, gantuṃ na visahāmahaṃ|

pakkhīva sailamāsaj‍ja, gamanaṃ na labhāmahaṃ||

91.

 ‘‘udakai vauk‍kamitvāna, aivaṃ gacchāmi ambarai|

na mai idaṃ bhūtapubbaṃ, iriyāpathavikaupanaṃ||

92.

 ‘‘handa maitaṃ gavaisissaṃ, appaivatthaṃ labhaiyyahaṃ|

aurauhantau antalikkhā, saddamassausi satthunau||

93.

 ‘‘saraina rajanīyaina, savanīyaina vaggunā|

anic‍cataṃ kathaintassa, tañ‍ñaiva uggahiṃ tadā|

anic‍casañ‍ñamuggayha, agamāsiṃ mamassamaṃ||

94.

 ‘‘yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ|

carimai vattamānamhi, saddhammassavanaṃ sariṃ||

95.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

96.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ|

aikapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

97.

 ‘‘aikavīsatikkhattuñ‍ca aikatiṃsati… (syā॰), cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

98.

 ‘‘anubhaumi sakaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, sukhitauhaṃ bhavābhavai|

anussarāmi taṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai|

na kauṭiṃ paṭivijjhāmi, nibbānaṃ ac‍cutaṃ padaṃ||

99.

 ‘‘pitugaihai nisīditvā, samaṇau bhāvitindriyau|

kathaṃsa kathayaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) paridīpaintau, anic‍catamudāhari||

100.

 ‘‘‘anic‍cā vata saṅkhārā, uppādavayadhamminau|

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau’||

101.

 ‘‘saha  gāthaṃ suṇitvāna, pubbasañ‍ñamanussariṃ|

aikāsanai nisīditvā, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

102.

 ‘‘jātiyā  sattavassaina, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ|

upasampādayī buddhau, dhammassavanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

103.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ dhammamasuṇiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhammassavanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

104.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

105.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

106.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikadhammassavaniyau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikadhammassavaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. sucintitatthairaapadānaṃ

107.

 ‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, ahausiṃ kassakau tadā|

kasikammaina jīvāmi, taina pausaimi dārakai||

108.

 ‘‘susampan‍naṃ  tadā khaittaṃ, dhañ‍ñaṃ mai phalinaṃ phalitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) ahu|

pākakālai ca sampattai, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā||

109.

 ‘‘nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ, jānantassa guṇāguṇaṃ|

yauhaṃ saṅghai adatvāna, aggaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya cai tadā mattanā (syā॰)||

110.

 ‘‘ayaṃ buddhau asamasamau, dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇau|

tatau pabhāvitau saṅghau, puñ‍ñakkhaittau anuttarau||

111.

 ‘‘tattha dassāmahaṃ dānaṃ, navasassaṃ purai purai|

aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, haṭṭhau pīṇitamānasau pītika… (syā॰)||

112.

 ‘‘khaittatau  dhañ‍ñamāhatvā, sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ|

upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

113.

 ‘‘‘navasassañ‍ca sampan‍naṃ, āyāgausi idha hausi (syā॰) ca tvaṃ munai|

anukampamupādāya, adhivāsaihi cakkhuma’||

114.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

115.

 ‘‘‘cattārau ca paṭipan‍nā, cattārau ca phalai ṭhitā|

aisa saṅghau ujubhūtau, pañ‍ñāsīlasamāhitau|

yajantānaṃ manussānaṃ, puñ‍ñapaikkhāna pāṇinaṃ||

116.

 ‘‘‘karautaupadhikaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, saṅghai din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ|

tasmiṃ saṅghaiva saṃghai ca (syā॰ pī॰) dātabbaṃ, tava sassaṃ tathaitaraṃ||

117.

 ‘‘‘saṅghatau uddisitvāna, bhikkhū naitvāna saṃgharaṃ|

paṭiyattaṃ gharai santaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghassa daihi tvaṃ’||

118.

 ‘‘saṅghatau uddisitvāna, bhikkhū naitvāna saṃgharaṃ|

yaṃ gharai paṭiyattaṃ mai, bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ||

119.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

120.

 ‘‘tattha mai sukataṃ byamhaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ sappabhassaraṃ|

saṭṭhiyaujanamubbaidhaṃ, tiṃsayaujanavitthataṃ||

aikūnavīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

121.

 ‘‘ākiṇṇaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ, nārīgaṇasamākulaṃ|

tattha bhutvā pivitvā ca, vasāmi tidasai ahaṃ||

122.

 ‘‘satānaṃ  tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

123.

 ‘‘bhavābhavai  saṃsarantau, labhāmi amitaṃ dhanaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

124.

 ‘‘hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, sivikaṃ sandamānikaṃ|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ sabbamaitampi (ka॰), navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

125.

 ‘‘navavatthaṃ navaphalaṃ, navaggarasabhaujanaṃ|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

126.

 ‘‘kausaiyyakambaliyāni, khaumakappāsikāni ca|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

127.

 ‘‘dāsīgaṇaṃ dāsagaṇaṃ, nāriyau ca alaṅkatā|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

128.

 ‘‘na maṃ sītaṃ vā uṇhaṃ vā, pariḷāhau na vij‍jati|

athau caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, hadayai mai na vij‍jati||

129.

 ‘‘idaṃ khāda idaṃ bhuñ‍ja, imamhi sayanai saya|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

130.

 ‘‘ayaṃ pacchimakau dāni, carimau vattatai bhavau|

aj‍jāpi daiyyadhammau mai, phalaṃ tausaisi sabbadā||

131.

 ‘‘navasassaṃ  daditvāna, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

aṭṭhānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

132.

 ‘‘vaṇṇavā yasavā haumi, mahābhaugau anītikau|

mahāpakkhau mahābhakkhau (syā॰ ka॰) sadā haumi, abhaij‍japarisau sadā||

133.

 ‘‘sabbai maṃ apacāyanti, yai kaici pathavissitā|

daiyyadhammā ca yai kaici, purai purai labhāmahaṃ||

134.

 ‘‘bhikkhusaṅghassa vā majjhai, buddhasaiṭṭhassa sammukhā|

sabbaipi samatik‍kamma, dainti mamaiva dāyakā||

135.

 ‘‘paṭhamaṃ navasassañhi, datvā saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

imānisaṃsai anubhaumi, navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

136.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, navasassassidaṃ phalaṃ||

137.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau|

138.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

139.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sucintitau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sucintitatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sauvaṇṇakiṅkaṇiyatthairaapadānaṃ

140.

 ‘‘saddhāya  abhinikkhamma, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

vākacīradharau āsiṃ, tapaukammamapassitau||

141.

 ‘‘atthadassī tu bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

uppaj‍ji tamhi samayai, tārayantau mahājanaṃ||

142.

 ‘‘balañ‍ca vata mai khīṇaṃ, byādhinā paramaina taṃ|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ saritvāna, pulinai thūpamuttamaṃ||

143.

 ‘‘karitvā haṭṭhacittauhaṃ, sahatthaina pasādaina (ka॰) samaukiriṃ|

sauṇṇakiṅkaṇipupphāni, udaggamanasau ahaṃ||

144.

 ‘‘sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ, thūpaṃ paricariṃ ahaṃ|

taina caitaupasādaina, atthadassissa tādinau||

145.

 ‘‘daivalaukaṃ gatau santau, labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇau tatthāsiṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

146.

 ‘‘asītikauṭiyau mayhaṃ, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

sadā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhanti, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

147.

 ‘‘saṭṭhituriyasahassāni saṭṭhitūriya… (ka॰), bhairiyau paṇavāni ca|

saṅkhā ca ḍiṇḍimā tattha, vaggū vaj‍janti nadanti (sī॰), vadanti (pī॰) dundubhī||

148.

 ‘‘cul‍lāsītisahassāni, hatthinau samalaṅkatā|

tidhāpabhin‍namātaṅgā, kuñ‍jarā saṭṭhihāyanā||

149.

 ‘‘haimajālābhisañchan‍nā, upaṭṭhānaṃ karaunti mai|

balakāyai gajai caiva, ūnatā mai na vij‍jati||

150.

 ‘‘sauṇṇakiṅkaṇipupphānaṃ, vipākaṃ anubhaumahaṃ|

aṭṭhapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

151.

 ‘‘aikasattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ aikasataṃ, mahiyā kārayiṃ ahaṃ||

152.

 ‘‘sau dāni amataṃ pattau, asaṅkhataṃ sududdasaṃ gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ padaṃ (syā॰)|

saṃyaujanaparikkhīṇau, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

153.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai  kappasatai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

154.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

155.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

156.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sauvaṇṇakiṅkaṇiyau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sauvaṇṇakiṅkaṇiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. sauṇṇakauntarikatthairaapadānaṃ

157.

 ‘‘manaubhāvaniyaṃ  buddhaṃ, attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ|

iriyamānaṃ brahmapathai, cittavūpasamai rataṃ||

158.

 ‘‘nittiṇṇaaughaṃ sambuddhaṃ, jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ muniṃ|

upatitthaṃ samāpan‍naṃ, indivaradalappabhaṃ||

159.

 ‘‘alābunaudakaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ upāgamiṃ|

buddhassa pādai dhauvitvā, alābukamadāsahaṃ||

160.

 ‘‘āṇāpaisi  ca sambuddhau, padumuttaranāmakau|

‘iminā dakamāhatvā, pādamūlai ṭhapaihi mai’||

161.

 ‘‘sādhūtihaṃ paṭissutvā, satthugāravatāya ca|

dakaṃ alābunāhatvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ upāgamiṃ||

162.

 ‘‘anumaudi mahāvīrau, cittaṃ nibbāpayaṃ mama|

‘iminālābudānaina, saṅkappau tai samijjhatu’||

163.

 ‘‘pan‍narasaisu kappaisu, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ|

tiṃsatikkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

164.

 ‘‘divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ, caṅkamantassa tiṭṭhatau|

sauvaṇṇaṃ kauntaraṃ gayha, tiṭṭhatai puratau mama||

165.

 ‘‘buddhassa  datvānalābuṃ, labhāmi sauṇṇakauntaraṃ|

appakampi kataṃ kāraṃ, vipulaṃ hauti tādisu||

166.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃlābumadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, alābussa idaṃ phalaṃ||

167.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

168.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

169.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sauṇṇakauntarikau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sauṇṇakauntarikatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

sakiṃsammaj‍jakavaggau taicattālīsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sakiṃsammaj‍jakau  thairau, aikadussī aikāsanī|

kadambakauraṇḍakadau, ghatassavanikaupi ca||

sucintikau kiṅkaṇikau, sauṇṇakauntarikaupi ca|

aikagāthāsatañ‍caittha, aikasattatimaiva ca||


44. aikavihārivaggau

1. aikavihārikatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘imamhi  bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

2.

 ‘‘nippapañ‍cau nirālambau, ākāsasamamānasau|

suñ‍ñatābahulau tādī, animittaratau vasī||

3.

 ‘‘asaṅgacittau nik‍laisau nil‍laipau (syā॰ ka॰), asaṃsaṭṭhau kulai gaṇai|

mahākāruṇikau vīrau, vinayaupāyakauvidau||

4.

 ‘‘uyyuttau parakic‍caisu, vinayantau sadaivakai|

nibbānagamanaṃ maggaṃ, gatiṃ paṅkavisausanaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘amataṃ paramassādaṃ, jarāmac‍cunivāraṇaṃ|

mahāparisamajjhai sau, nisin‍nau laukatārakau||

6.

 ‘‘karavīkarutau karavīkarudau (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) nāthau, brahmaghausau tathāgatau|

uddharantau mahāduggā, vippanaṭṭhai anāyakai||

7.

 ‘‘daisaintau virajaṃ dhammaṃ, diṭṭhau mai laukanāyakau|

tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘pabbajitvā tadāpāhaṃ, cintaintau jinasāsanaṃ|

aikakauva vanai rammai, vasiṃ saṃsaggapīḷitau||

9.

 ‘‘sak‍kāyavūpakāsau  mai, haitubhūtau mamābhavī mamāgamī (syā॰ pī॰)|

manasau vūpakāsassa, saṃsaggabhayadassinau||

10.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

11.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikavihārikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

aikavihārikatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aikasaṅkhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘vipassinau bhagavatau, mahābaudhimahau ahu|

mahājanā samāgamma, pūjainti baudhimuttamaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘na hi taṃ aurakaṃ mañ‍ñai, buddhasaiṭṭhau bhavissati|

yassāyaṃ īdisā baudhi, pūjanīyā īdisau baudhi, pūjanīyau (syā॰) ca satthunau||

15.

 ‘‘tatau saṅkhaṃ gahaitvāna, baudhirukkhamupaṭṭhahiṃ|

dhamantau sabbadivasaṃ, avandiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘āsan‍nakai kataṃ kammaṃ, daivalaukaṃ apāpayī|

kaḷaivaraṃ mai patitaṃ, daivalaukai ramāmahaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘saṭṭhituriyasahassāni, tuṭṭhahaṭṭhā pamauditā|

sadā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhanti, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘aikasattatimai kappai, rājā āsiṃ sudassanau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambumaṇḍassa issarau||

19.

 ‘‘tatau aṅgasatā turiyā tūrā (sī॰ ka॰), parivārainti maṃ sadā|

anubhaumi sakaṃ kammaṃ, upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

mātukucchigatassāpi, vaj‍jarai bhairiyau sadā||

21.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhitvāna sambuddhaṃ, anubhutvāna sampadā|

sivaṃ sukhaimaṃ amataṃ, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

24.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikasaṅkhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

aikasaṅkhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. pāṭihīrasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

vasīsatasahassaihi, nagaraṃ pāvisī tadā||

27.

 ‘‘nagaraṃ pavisantassa, upasantassa tādinau|

ratanāni paj‍jautiṃsu panādiṃsu (pī॰), nigghausau āsi tāvadai||

28.

 ‘‘buddhassa ānubhāvaina, bhairī vaj‍jumaghaṭṭitā|

sayaṃ vīṇā pavaj‍janti, buddhassa pavisatau puraṃ||

29.

 ‘‘buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ namassāmi na passāmi (sī॰), padumuttaramahāmuniṃ|

pāṭihīrañ‍ca passitvā, tattha cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

30.

 ‘‘ahau buddhau ahau dhammau, ahau nau satthusampadā|

acaitanāpi turiyā, sayamaiva pavaj‍jarai||

31.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

33.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā pāṭihīrasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

pāṭihīrasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. ñāṇatthavikatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jalitaṃ, dīparukkhaṃva jautitaṃ|

kañ‍canaṃva viraucantaṃ, addasaṃ dvipaduttamaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘kamaṇḍaluṃ ṭhapaitvāna, vākacīrañ‍ca kuṇḍikaṃ|

aikaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ thaviṃ ahaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘‘tamandhakāraṃ vidhamaṃ, mauhajālasamākulaṃ|

ñāṇālaukaṃ dassaitvāna, nittiṇṇausi mahāmuni||

38.

 ‘‘‘samuddharasimaṃ laukaṃ, sabbāvantamanuttaraṃ|

ñāṇai tai upamā natthi, yāvatājagataugati yāvatā ca gataugati (pī॰ ka॰)||

39.

 ‘‘‘taina ñāṇaina sabbañ‍ñū, iti buddhau pavuc‍cati|

vandāmi taṃ mahāvīraṃ, sabbañ‍ñutamanāvaraṃ’||

40.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ thaviṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ñāṇatthavāyidaṃ phalaṃ|

41.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau|

42.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇatthavikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ñāṇatthavikatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. ucchukhaṇḍikatthairaapadānaṃ

44.

 ‘‘nagarai bandhumatiyā, dvārapālau ahausahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ||

45.

 ‘‘ucchukhaṇḍikamādāya, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vipassissa mahaisinau||

46.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ ucchumadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ucchukhaṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

48.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ucchukhaṇḍikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ucchukhaṇḍikatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kaḷambadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

50.

 ‘‘raumasau  nāma sambuddhau, vasatai pabbatantarai|

kaḷambaṃ tassa pādāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

51.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kaḷambassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

53.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaḷambadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kaḷambadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ambāṭakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

55.

 ‘‘vipinai  buddhaṃ disvāna, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ|

ambāṭakaṃ gahaitvāna, sayambhussa adāsahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

58.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambāṭakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ambāṭakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. harītakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

60.

 ‘‘harītakaṃ āmalakaṃ, ambajambuvibhītakaṃ|

kaulaṃ bhal‍lātakaṃ bil‍laṃ, sayamaiva harāmahaṃ||

61.

 ‘‘disvāna pabbhāragataṃ, jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ muniṃ|

ābādhaina āpīḷaintaṃ, adutīyaṃ mahāmuniṃ||

62.

 ‘‘harītakaṃ gahaitvāna, sayambhussa adāsahaṃ|

khādamattamhi bhaisaj‍jai, byādhi passambhi passaddhi (ka॰) tāvadai||

63.

 ‘‘pahīnadarathau buddhau, anumaudamakāsi mai|

‘bhaisaj‍jadānainiminā, byādhivūpasamaina ca||

64.

 ‘‘‘daivabhūtau manussau vā, jātau vā añ‍ñajātiyā|

sabbattha sukhitau hautu, mā ca tai byādhimāgamā’||

65.

 ‘‘idaṃ  vatvāna sambuddhau, sayambhū aparājitau|

nabhaṃ abbhuggamī dhīrau, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

66.

 ‘‘yatau harītakaṃ din‍naṃ, sayambhussa mahaisinau|

imaṃ jātiṃ upādāya, byādhi mai nupapaj‍jatha||

67.

 ‘‘ayaṃ pacchimakau mayhaṃ, carimau vattatai bhavau|

tissau vij‍jā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, bhaisaj‍jamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhaisaj‍jassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

70.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

71.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā harītakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

harītakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ambapiṇḍiyatthairaapadānaṃ

72.

 ‘‘hatthirājā tadā āsiṃ, īsādantau uruḷhavā|

vicarantau brahārañ‍ñai, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘ambapiṇḍaṃ  gahaitvāna, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ|

paṭiggaṇhi mahāvīrau, siddhatthau laukanāyakau||

74.

 ‘‘mama nijjhāyamānassa, paribhuñ‍ji tadā jinau|

tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘tatau ahaṃ cavitvāna, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

aitainaiva upāyaina, anubhutvāna sampadā||

76.

 ‘‘padhānapahitattauhaṃ, upasantau nirūpadhi|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

77.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

78.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

79.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

80.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambapiṇḍiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ambapiṇḍiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. ambaphaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

81.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

piṇḍāya vicarantassa, dhāratau uttamaṃ yasaṃ||

82.

 ‘‘aggaphalaṃ gahaitvāna, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

dakkhiṇaiyyassa vīrassa, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

83.

 ‘‘taina kammaina dvipadinda dipadinda (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

84.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aggadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

85.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

86.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambaphaliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ambaphaliyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

aikavihārivaggau catucattālīsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

thairau  aikavihārī ca, saṅkhiyau pāṭihīrakau|

thavikau ucchukhaṇḍī ca, kaḷambaambāṭakadau||

harītakambapiṇḍī ca, ambadau dasamau yati|

chaḷasīti ca gāthāyau, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||


45. vibhītakavaggau

1. vibhītakamiñ‍jiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘kakusandhau  mahāvīrau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

gaṇamhā vūpakaṭṭhau sau, agamāsi vanantaraṃ||

2.

 ‘‘bījamiñ‍jaṃ gahaitvāna, latāya āvuṇiṃ ahaṃ|

bhagavā tamhi samayai, jhāyatai pabbatantarai||

3.

 ‘‘disvānahaṃ daivadaivaṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

dakkhiṇaiyyassa vīrassa, bījamiñ‍jamadāsahaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva kappamhi, yaṃ miñ‍jamadadiṃ phalamadadiṃ (sī॰ pī॰), bījamadadiṃ (syā॰) tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bījamiñ‍jassidaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

6.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vibhītakamiñ‍jiyau vibhaidaka… (syā॰ ka॰) thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vibhītakamiñ‍jiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. kauladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

8.

 ‘‘ajinaina  nivatthauhaṃ, vākacīradharau tadā|

khāriyā pūrayitvāna, kaulaṃhāsiṃ mamassamaṃ khāribhāraṃ haritvāna, kaulamāharimassamaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)||

9.

 ‘‘tamhi  kālai sikhī buddhau, aikau adutiyau ahu|

mamassamaṃ upāgacchi, jānantau sabbakālikaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, vanditvāna ca subbataṃ|

ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, kaulaṃ buddhassadāsahaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kauladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

13.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kauladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kauladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. bil‍liyatthairaapadānaṃ

15.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, assamau sukatau mama|

bil‍larukkhaihi bailuvarukkhaihi (syā॰) ākiṇṇau, nānādumanisaivitau||

16.

 ‘‘sugandhaṃ bailuvaṃ disvā, buddhasaiṭṭhamanussariṃ|

khāribhāraṃ pūrayitvā, tuṭṭhau saṃviggamānasau||

17.

 ‘‘kakusandhaṃ upāgamma, bil‍lapak‍kamadāsahaṃ|

puñ‍ñakkhaittassa vīrassa, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

18.

 ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva kappasmiṃ, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

20.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bil‍liyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

bil‍liyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. bhal‍lātadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

22.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ|

vipinaggaina pavanaggaina (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) gacchantaṃ, sālarājaṃva phul‍litaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘tiṇattharaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ ayācahaṃ|

‘anukampatu maṃ buddhau, bhikkhaṃ icchāmi dātavai’||

24.

 ‘‘anukampakau kāruṇikau, atthadassī mahāyasau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, aurūhi mama assamai||

25.

 ‘‘aurauhitvāna sambuddhau, nisīdi paṇṇasantharai|

bhal‍lātakaṃ gahaitvāna, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘mama nijjhāyamānassa, paribhuñ‍ji tadā jinau|

tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, abhivandiṃ tadā jinaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

29.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā bhal‍lātadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

bhal‍lātadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. uttalipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘nigraudhai  haritaubhāsai, saṃviruḷhamhi pādapai|

uttalimālaṃ ummā mālaṃ hi (syā॰) paggayha, baudhiyā abhiraupayiṃ||

32.

 ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva kappamhi, yaṃ baudhimabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

34.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uttalipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

uttalipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ambāṭakiyatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘supupphitaṃ  sālavanaṃ, augayha vaissabhū muni|

nisīdi giriduggaisu, abhijātauva kaisarī||

37.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau  sumanau, ambāṭakamapūjayiṃ|

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ mahāvīraṃ (sī॰ syā॰), pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

38.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggati nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

40.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambāṭakiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ambāṭakiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sīhāsanikatthairaapadānaṃ

42.

 ‘‘padumuttarassa  bhagavatau, sabbabhūtahitaisinau|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sīhāsanamadāsahaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘daivalaukai manussai vā, yattha yattha vasāmahaṃ|

labhāmi vipulaṃ byamhaṃ, sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘sauṇṇamayā rūpimayā, lauhitaṅgamayā lauhitaṅkamayā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bahū|

maṇimayā ca pal‍laṅkā, nibbattanti mamaṃ sadā||

45.

 ‘‘baudhiyā  āsanaṃ katvā, jalajuttamanāminau|

uc‍cai kulai pajāyāmi, ahau dhammasudhammatā||

46.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, sīhāsanamakāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

48.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsanikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sīhāsanikatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. pādapīṭhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

50.

 ‘‘sumaidhau  nāma sambuddhau, aggau kāruṇikau muni|

tārayitvā bahū sattai, nibbutau sau mahāyasau||

51.

 ‘‘sīhāsanassa sāmantā, sumaidhassa mahaisinau|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pādapīṭhamakārayiṃ||

52.

 ‘‘katvāna kusalaṃ kammaṃ, sukhapākaṃ sukhudrayaṃ|

puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttau, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘tattha  mai vasamānassa, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginau|

padāni uddharantassa, sauṇṇapīṭhā bhavanti mai||

54.

 ‘‘lābhā taisaṃ suladdhaṃ vau, yai labhanti upassutiṃ|

nibbutai kāraṃ katvāna, labhanti vipulaṃ sukhaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘mayāpi sukataṃ kammaṃ, vāṇij‍jaṃ suppayaujitaṃ|

pādapīṭhaṃ karitvāna, sauṇṇapīṭhaṃ labhāmahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ disaṃ pak‍kamāmi, kainaci kic‍cayainahaṃ pac‍cayainahaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)|

sauṇṇapīṭhai ak‍kamāmi sauṇṇapīṭhaina kamāmi (ka॰), puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pādapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

58.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

59.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pādapīṭhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

pādapīṭhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaidikārakatthairaapadānaṃ

61.

 ‘‘padumuttarassa  bhagavatau, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

vaidikaṃ sukataṃ katvā, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

62.

 ‘‘atauḷārāni athauḷārāni (sī॰ pī॰), aggauḷārāni (syā॰) bhaṇḍāni, katāni akatāni ca|

antalikkhā pavassanti, vaidikāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘ubhatau byūḷhasaṅgāmai, pakkhandantau bhayānakai|

bhayabhairavaṃ na passāmi, vaidikāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘mama  saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, byamhaṃ nibbattatai subhaṃ|

sayanāni mahagghāni, vaidikāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ vaidikamakārayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vaidikāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

67.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaidikārakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

vaidikārakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. baudhigharadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

69.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, dvipadindassa tādinau|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, baudhigharamakārayiṃ||

70.

 ‘‘tusitaṃ upapan‍naumhi, vasāmi ratanai gharai|

na mai sītaṃ vā uṇhaṃ vā, vātau gattai na samphusai||

71.

 ‘‘pañ‍casaṭṭhimhitau kappai, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

kāsikaṃ nāma nagaraṃ, vissakammaina visukammaina (syā॰ ka॰) māpitaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘dasayaujanaāyāmaṃ, aṭṭhayaujanavitthataṃ|

na tamhi nagarai atthi, kaṭṭhaṃ val‍lī ca mattikā||

73.

 ‘‘tiriyaṃ yaujanaṃ āsi, addhayaujanavitthataṃ|

maṅgalau nāma pāsādau, vissakammaina māpitau||

74.

 ‘‘cul‍lāsītisahassāni, thambhā sauṇṇamayā ahuṃ|

maṇimayā ca niyyūhā, chadanaṃ rūpiyaṃ ahu||

75.

 ‘‘sabbasauṇṇamayaṃ  gharaṃ, vissakammaina māpitaṃ|

ajjhāvutthaṃ mayā aitaṃ, gharadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

76.

 ‘‘tai sabbai anubhautvāna, daivamānusakai bhavai|

ajjhapattaumhi nibbānaṃ, santipadamanuttaraṃ||

77.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, baudhigharamakārayiṃ|

duggatiṃ  nābhijānāmi, gharadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

78.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

79.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

80.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā baudhigharadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

baudhigharadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

vibhītakavaggau pañ‍cacattālīsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

vibhītakī kaulaphalī, bil‍labhal‍lātakappadau|

uttalambaṭakī caiva, āsanī pādapīṭhakau||

vaidikau baudhigharikau, gāthāyau gaṇitāpi ca|

aikūnāsītikā sabbā, asmiṃ vaggai pakittitā||


46. jagatidāyakavaggau

1. jagatidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘dhammadassissa  muninau, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, jagatiṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘daritau pabbatatau vā, rukkhatau patitau ahaṃ|

cutau patiṭṭhaṃ vindāmi, jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘na mai caurā vihaisanti, nātimañ‍ñanti khattiyā pasahanti, nātimañ‍ñati khattiyau (sī॰ pī॰)|

sabbāmittaitik‍kamāmi, jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

sabbattha pūjitau haumi, jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, jagatiṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, jagatidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

7.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā jagatidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

jagatidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. maurahatthiyatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘maurahatthaṃ  gahaitvāna, upaisiṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, maurahatthamadāsahaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘iminā  maurahatthaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

nibbāyiṃsu tayau aggī, labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘ahau buddhau ahau dhammau, ahau nau satthusampadā|

datvānahaṃ maurahatthaṃ, labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘tiyaggī tidhaggī (syā॰ ka॰), tivaggī (pī॰) nibbutā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

13.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, maurahatthassidaṃ phalaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

15.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā maurahatthiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

maurahatthiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sīhāsanabījiyatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘tissassāhaṃ bhagavatau, baudhirukkhamavandiyaṃ|

paggayha bījaniṃ tattha, sīhāsanamabījahaṃ mabījayiṃ (sī॰), mavij‍jahaṃ (syā॰)||

18.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, sīhāsanamabījahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bījanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

20.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsanabījiyau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sīhāsanabījiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. tiṇuk‍kadhāriyatthairaapadānaṃ

22.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, tayau uk‍kai adhārayiṃ||

23.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, sauhaṃ uk‍kamadhārayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, uk‍kadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

25.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇuk‍kadhāriyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

tiṇuk‍kadhāriyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. ak‍kamanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘kakusandhassa muninau, brāhmaṇassa vusīmatau|

divāvihāraṃ vajatau, ak‍kamanamadāsahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva kappamhi, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ak‍kamanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

30.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ak‍kamanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ak‍kamanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vanakauraṇḍiyatthairaapadānaṃ

32.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

vanakauraṇḍamādāya, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

33.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

35.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vanakauraṇḍiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

vanakauraṇḍiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

vīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

7. aikachattiyatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘aṅgārajātā  pathavī, kuk‍kuḷānugatā mahī|

padumuttarau bhagavā, abbhaukāsamhi caṅkami||

38.

 ‘‘paṇḍaraṃ chattamādāya, addhānaṃ paṭipaj‍jahaṃ|

tattha disvāna sambuddhaṃ, vitti mai upapaj‍jatha||

39.

 ‘‘marīciyautthaṭā  maricimauphunā (syā॰), marīcivauphuṭā (pī॰) bhūmi, aṅgārāva mahī ayaṃ|

upahanti upavāyanti (sī॰ pī॰) mahāvātā, sarīrassāsukhaipanā sarīrakāyukhaipanā (syā॰)||

40.

 ‘‘sītaṃ uṇhaṃ vihanantaṃ vihanati (syā॰ ka॰), vātātapanivāraṇaṃ|

paṭiggaṇha imaṃ chattaṃ, phassayissāmi passayissāmi (ka॰) nibbutiṃ||

41.

 ‘‘anukampakau kāruṇikau, padumuttarau mahāyasau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, paṭiggaṇhi tadā jinau||

42.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappāni daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

anubhaumi sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbai sukatamattanau||

44.

 ‘‘ayaṃ  mai pacchimā jāti, carimau vattatai bhavau|

aj‍jāpi saitacchattaṃ mai, sabbakālaṃ dharīyati||

45.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ chattamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, chattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

47.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikachattiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

aikachattiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. jātipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

49.

 ‘‘parinibbutai bhagavati, padumuttarai mahāyasai|

pupphavaṭaṃsakai katvā pupphacaṅkauṭakai gahaitvā (syā॰), sarīramabhiraupayiṃ||

50.

 ‘‘tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, nimmānaṃ agamāsahaṃ|

daivalaukagatau santau, puñ‍ñakammaṃ sarāmahaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘ambarā  pupphavassau mai, sabbakālaṃ pavassati|

saṃsarāmi manussai cai vai (syā॰), rājā haumi mahāyasau||

52.

 ‘‘tahiṃ kusumavassau mai, abhivassati sabbadā|

tassaiva kāyaisu (syā॰), kāyaiva (pī॰) pupphapūjāya, vāhasā sabbadassinau||

53.

 ‘‘ayaṃ pacchimakau mayhaṃ, carimau vattatai bhavau|

aj‍jāpi pupphavassau mai, abhivassati sabbadā||

54.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, daihapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

56.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jātipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

jātipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭṭipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

58.

 ‘‘nīharantai sarīramhi, vaj‍jamānāsu bhairisu|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, paṭṭipupphamapūjayiṃ satti… (syā॰ pī॰)||

59.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, daihapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

61.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṭṭipupphiyau sattipaṇṇiyau (syā॰ pī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

paṭṭipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. gandhapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

63.

 ‘‘citāsu  kurumānāsu cittaisu kayiramānaisu (sī॰), nānāgandhai samāhaṭai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, gandhamuṭṭhimapūjayiṃ||

64.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, citakaṃ yamapūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

66.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

67.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

gandhapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

jagatidāyakavaggau chacattālīsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

jagatī  maurahatthī ca, āsanī uk‍kadhārakau|

ak‍kami vanakauraṇḍi, chattadau jātipūjakau||

paṭṭipupphī ca yau thairau, dasamau gandhapūjakau|

sattasaṭṭhi ca gāthāyau, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||


47. sālakusumiyavaggau

1. sālakusumiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘parinibbutai  bhagavati, jalajuttamanāmakai|

āraupitamhi citakai, sālapupphamapūjayiṃ||

2.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ pupphamabhipujayiṃ (syā॰)|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ buddhapūjāyidaṃ (syā॰) phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

4.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sālakusumiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sālakusumiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. citakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘jhāyamānassa bhagavatau, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

aṭṭha campakapupphāni, citakaṃ abhiraupayiṃ||

7.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ  nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

9.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

citakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

 ‘‘dayhamānai  sarīramhi, vaissabhussa mahaisinau|

5. sumanatālavaṇṭiyatthairaapadānaṃ
