6. avaṭaphaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘sataraṃsī  nāma bhagavā, sayambhū aparājitau|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, gaucarāyābhinikkhami||

27.

 ‘‘phalahatthau ahaṃ disvā, upagacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, adāsiṃ avaṭaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

30.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā avaṭaphaliyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

avaṭaphaliyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. labujaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

32.

 ‘‘nagarai  bandhumatiyā, ārāmikau ahaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

33.

 ‘‘labujaṃ phalamādāya, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

ākāsaiva ṭhitau santau, paṭiggaṇhi mahāyasau||

34.

 ‘‘vittisañ‍jananau mayhaṃ, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahau|

phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

35.

 ‘‘adhigañchiṃ tadā pītiṃ, vipulaṃ sukhamuttamaṃ|

uppaj‍jataiva uppaj‍jatai mai (syā॰) ratanaṃ, nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ||

36.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

38.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā labujaphaladāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

labujaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. pilakkhaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

40.

 ‘‘vanantarai  buddhaṃ disvā vanantai buddhaṃ disvāna (sī॰ pī॰), atthadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pilakkhassa pilakkhussa (pī॰) phalaṃ adā||

41.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

43.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pilakkhaphaladāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pilakkhaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sayaṃpaṭibhāniyatthairaapadānaṃ

45.

 ‘‘kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, kau disvā na pasīdati||

46.

 ‘‘tamandhakāraṃ nāsaitvā, santāraitvā bahuṃ janaṃ|

ñāṇālaukaina jautantaṃ, kau disvā na pasīdati||

47.

 ‘‘vasīsatasahassaihi, nīyantaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

uddharantaṃ bahū sattai, kau disvā na pasīdati||

48.

 ‘‘āhanantaṃ āhanitvā (syā॰ ka॰) dhammabhairiṃ, maddantaṃ titthiyai gaṇai|

sīhanādaṃ vinadantaṃ, kau disvā na pasīdati||

49.

 ‘‘yāvatā brahmalaukatau, āgantvāna sabrahmakā|

pucchanti nipuṇai pañhai, kau disvā na pasīdati||

50.

 ‘‘yassañ‍jaliṃ karitvāna, āyācanti sadaivakā|

taina puñ‍ñaṃ anubhaunti, kau disvā na pasīdati||

51.

 ‘‘sabbai  janā samāgantvā, sampavārainti cakkhumaṃ|

na vikampati ajjhiṭṭhau, kau disvā na pasīdati||

52.

 ‘‘nagaraṃ  pavisatau yassa, ravanti bhairiyau bahū|

vinadanti gajā mattā, kau disvā na pasīdati||

53.

 ‘‘vīthiyā rathiyā (sī॰) gacchatau yassa, sabbābhā jautatai sadā|

abbhun‍natā samā haunti, kau disvā na pasīdati||

54.

 ‘‘byāharantassa  buddhassa, cak‍kavāḷampi suyyati|

sabbai sattai viñ‍ñāpaiti, kau disvā na pasīdati||

55.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

57.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

58.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sayaṃpaṭibhāniyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sayaṃpaṭibhāniyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. nimittabyākaraṇiyatthairaapadānaṃ

59.

 ‘‘ajjhaugāhaitvā himavaṃ, mantai vācai mahaṃ tadā|

catupañ‍ñāsasahassāni, sissā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhahuṃ||

60.

 ‘‘adhitā  vaidagū sabbai, chaḷaṅgai pāramiṃ gatā|

sakavij‍jāhupatthaddhā, himavantai vasanti tai||

61.

 ‘‘cavitvā tusitā kāyā, daivaputtau mahāyasau|

uppaj‍ji mātukucchismiṃ, sampajānau patissatau||

62.

 ‘‘sambuddhai upapaj‍jantai, dasasahassi kampatha|

andhā cakkhuṃ alabhiṃsu, uppaj‍jantamhi nāyakai||

63.

 ‘‘sabbākāraṃ  pakampittha, kaivalā vasudhā ayaṃ|

nigghausasaddaṃ sutvāna, ubbij‍jiṃsu vimhayiṃsu (syā॰ ka॰) mahājanā||

64.

 ‘‘sabbai janā samāgamma, āgacchuṃ mama santikaṃ|

vasudhāyaṃ pakampittha, kiṃ vipākau bhavissati||

65.

 ‘‘avacāsiṃ vidassāmi (syā॰) tadā taisaṃ, mā bhaitha mā rauda (ka॰), mābhāyittha (syā॰) natthi vau bhayaṃ|

visaṭṭhā hautha sabbaipi, uppādauyaṃ suvatthikau sukhatthikau (syā॰)||

66.

 ‘‘aṭṭhahaitūhi  samphussa aṭṭhahaitūhi samphassa (syā॰ pī॰), atthahaitu nisaṃsayaṃ (ka॰), vasudhāyaṃ pakampati|

tathā nimittā dissanti, aubhāsau vipulau mahā||

67.

 ‘‘asaṃsayaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhau, uppaj‍jissati cakkhumā|

sañ‍ñāpaitvāna janataṃ, pañ‍casīlai kathaisahaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘sutvāna  pañ‍ca sīlāni, buddhuppādañ‍ca dul‍labhaṃ|

ubbaigajātā sumanā, tuṭṭhahaṭṭhā ahaṃsu tai||

69.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ nimittaṃ viyākariṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, byākaraṇassidaṃ phalaṃ||

70.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

71.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nimittabyākaraṇiyau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nimittabyākaraṇiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

sālakusumiyavaggau sattacattālīsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sālakusumiyau thairau, pūjā nibbāpakaupi ca|

saitudau tālavaṇṭī ca, avaṭalabujappadau||

pilakkhapaṭibhānī ca, vaiyyākaraṇiyau dijau|

dvaisattati ca gāthāyau, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||


48. naḷamālivaggau

1. naḷamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

vipinaggaina gacchantaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘naḷamālaṃ gahaitvāna, nikkhamantau ca tāvadai|

tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, naḷamālamapūjayiṃ|

dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ mahāvīraṃ, sabbalaukānukampakaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ mālamabhiraupayiṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

6.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

naḷamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. maṇipūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

8.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, gacchatai anilañ‍jasai||

9.

 ‘‘avidūrai himavantassa, mahājātassarau ahu|

tattha mai bhavanaṃ āsi, puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyutaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘bhavanā  abhinikkhamma, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ, ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘vicinaṃ naddasaṃ pupphaṃ, pūjayissanti nāyakaṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, avandiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘mama sīsai maṇiṃ gayha, pūjayiṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

imāya maṇipūjāya, vipākau hautu bhaddakau||

13.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

14.

 ‘sau tai ijjhatu saṅkappau, labhassu vipulaṃ sukhaṃ|

imāya maṇipūjāya, anubhauhi mahāyasaṃ’||

15.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna bhagavā, jalajuttamanāmakau|

agamāsi buddhasaiṭṭhau, yattha cittaṃ paṇīhitaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘saṭṭhikappāni daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

anaikasatakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘pubbakammaṃ  sarantassa, daivabhūtassa mai satau|

maṇi nibbattatai mayhaṃ, ālaukakaraṇau mamaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘chaḷasītisahassāni, nāriyau mai pariggahā|

vicittavatthābharaṇā, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

19.

 ‘‘aḷārapamhā hasulā, susañ‍ñā tanumajjhimā|

parivārainti maṃ nic‍caṃ, maṇipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘sauṇṇamayā maṇimayā, lauhitaṅgamayā tathā|

bhaṇḍā mai sukatā haunti, yadicchasi yadicchāya (sī॰ pī॰) piḷandhanā||

21.

 ‘‘kūṭāgārā  gahārammā, sayanañ‍ca mahārahaṃ|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, nibbattanti yadicchakaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘lābhā taisaṃ suladdhañ‍ca, yai labhanti upassutiṃ|

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ manussānaṃ, ausadhaṃ sabbapāṇinaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘mayhampi  sukataṃ kammaṃ, yauhaṃ adakkhi nāyakaṃ|

vinipātā pamuttaumhi, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

divasañ‍caiva rattiñ‍ca samantā sattaratanā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), ālaukau hauti mai sadā||

25.

 ‘‘tāyaiva maṇipūjāya, anubhautvāna sampadā|

ñāṇālaukau mayā diṭṭhau, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ maṇiṃ abhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, maṇipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

28.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maṇipūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

maṇipūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. uk‍kāsatikatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘kausikau nāma bhagavā, cittakūṭai vasī tadā|

jhāyī jhānaratau buddhau, vivaikābhiratau muni||

31.

 ‘‘ajjhaugāhaitvā ajjhaugahaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) himavantaṃ, nārīgaṇapurakkhatau|

addasaṃ kausikaṃ buddhaṃ, puṇṇamāyaiva candimaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘uk‍kāsatai gahaitvāna, parivāraisahaṃ tadā|

sattarattindivaṃ ṭhatvā buddhau (syā॰ ka॰), aṭṭhamaina agacchahaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘vuṭṭhitaṃ  kausikaṃ buddhaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau vanditvā, aikaṃ bhikkhaṃ adāsahaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘taina kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

uppaj‍jiṃ tusitai kāyai, aikabhikkhāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘divasañ‍caiva  rattiñ‍ca, ālaukau hauti mai sadā|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, aubhāsaina pharāmahaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘pañ‍capañ‍ñāsakappamhi, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambumaṇḍassa jambusaṇḍassa (pī॰) issarau||

37.

 ‘‘tadā mai nagaraṃ āsi, iddhaṃ phītaṃ sunimmitaṃ|

tiṃsayaujanamāyāmaṃ, vitthāraina ca vīsati||

38.

 ‘‘saubhaṇaṃ nāma nagaraṃ, vissakammaina māpitaṃ|

dasasaddāvivittaṃ taṃ, sammatāḷasamāhitaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘na  tamhi nagarai atthi, val‍likaṭṭhañ‍ca mattikā|

sabbasauṇṇamayaṃyaiva, jautatai nic‍cakālikaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘catupākāraparikkhittaṃ, tayau āsuṃ maṇimayā|

vaimajjhai tālapantī ca, vissakammaina māpitā||

41.

 ‘‘dasasahassapaukkharañ‍ñau, padumuppalachāditā|

puṇḍarīkaihi puṇḍarīkādi (syā॰) sañchan‍nā, nānāgandhasamīritā||

42.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ uk‍kaṃ dhārayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, uk‍kadhārassidaṃ phalaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

44.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uk‍kāsatikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

uk‍kāsatikatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. sumanabījaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

46.

 ‘‘vipassinau  bhagavatau, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

sumanau bījaniṃ gayha, abījiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, abījiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bījanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

49.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanabījaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sumanabījaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. kummāsadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘aisanāya  carantassa, vipassissa mahaisinau|

rittakaṃ pattaṃ disvāna, kummāsaṃ pūrayiṃ ahaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ bhikkhaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kummāsassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

54.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kummāsadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kummāsadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kusaṭṭhakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

56.

 ‘‘kassapassa  bhagavatau, brāhmaṇassa vusīmatau|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, kusaṭṭhakamadāsahaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva kappasmiṃ, kusaṭṭhakamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kusaṭṭhakassidaṃ phalaṃ||

58.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

59.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kusaṭṭhakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kusaṭṭhakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. giripun‍nāgiyatthairaapadānaṃ

61.

 ‘‘saubhitau nāma sambuddhau, cittakūṭai vasī tadā|

gahaitvā giripun‍nāgaṃ, sayambhuṃ abhipūjayiṃ||

62.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

64.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā giripun‍nāgiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

giripun‍nāgiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. val‍likāraphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

66.

 ‘‘sumanau  nāma sambuddhau, tak‍karāyaṃ vasī tadā|

val‍likāraphalaṃ gayha, sayambhussa adāsahaṃ||

67.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

69.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

70.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā val‍likāraphaladāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

val‍likāraphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pānadhidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

71.

 ‘‘anaumadassī  bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

divāvihārā nikkhamma, pathamāruhi pītimāruyhi (syā॰) cakkhumā||

72.

 ‘‘pānadhiṃ  sukataṃ gayha, addhānaṃ paṭipaj‍jahaṃ|

tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, pattikaṃ cārudassanaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, nīharitvāna pānadhiṃ|

pādamūlai ṭhapaitvāna, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

74.

 ‘‘‘abhirūha mahāvīra, sugatinda vināyaka|

itau phalaṃ labhissāmi, sau mai atthau samijjhatu’||

75.

 ‘‘anaumadassī bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

pānadhiṃ abhirūhitvā, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

76.

 ‘‘‘yau pānadhiṃ mai adāsi, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau’||

77.

 ‘‘buddhassa  giramañ‍ñāya, sabbai daivā samāgatā|

udaggacittā sumanā, vaidajātā katañ‍jalī||

78.

 ‘‘pānadhīnaṃ padānaina, sukhitauyaṃ bhavissati|

pañ‍capañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

79.

 ‘‘sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

80.

 ‘‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina nāmaina (sabbattha), satthā laukai bhavissati||

81.

 ‘‘tassa  dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

82.

 ‘‘daivalaukai  manussai vā, nibbattissati puñ‍ñavā|

daivayānapaṭibhāgaṃ, yānaṃ paṭilabhissati||

83.

 ‘‘pāsādā sivikā vayhaṃ, hatthinau samalaṅkatā|

rathā vājañ‍ñasaṃyuttā, sadā pātubhavanti mai||

84.

 ‘‘agārā nikkhamantaupi, rathaina nikkhamiṃ ahaṃ|

kaisaisu chij‍jamānaisu, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

85.

 ‘‘lābhā mayhaṃ suladdhaṃ mai, vāṇij‍jaṃ suppayaujitaṃ|

datvāna pānadhiṃ aikaṃ, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

86.

 ‘‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, yaṃ pānadhimadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pānadhissa idaṃ phalaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

88.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

89.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pānadhidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

pānadhidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pulinacaṅkamiyatthairaapadānaṃ

90.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

vātamigaṃ gavaisantau, caṅkamaṃ addasaṃ ahaṃ||

91.

 ‘‘ucchaṅgaina pulinaṃ puḷinaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) gayha, caṅkamai aukiriṃ ahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sugatassa sirīmatau||

92.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, pulinaṃ aukiriṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pulinassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

93.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

94.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pulinacaṅkamiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

pulinacaṅkamiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

naḷamālivaggau aṭṭhacattālīsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

naḷamālī maṇidadau, uk‍kāsatikabījanī|

kummāsau ca kusaṭṭhau ca, giripun‍nāgiyaupi ca||

val‍likārau  pānadhidau, athau pulinacaṅkamau|

gāthāyau pañ‍canavuti, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||


49. paṃsukūlavaggau

1. paṃsukūlasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘tissau  nāmāsi bhagavā, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

paṃsukūlaṃ ṭhapaitvāna, vihāraṃ pāvisī jinau||

2.

 ‘‘vinataṃ saj‍jitaṃ (syā॰), tiyantaṃ (pī॰) dhanumādāya, bhakkhatthāya cariṃ ahaṃ|

maṇḍalaggaṃ gahaitvāna, kānanaṃ pāvisiṃ ahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘tatthaddasaṃ  paṃsukūlaṃ, dumaggai laggitaṃ tadā|

cāpaṃ tatthaiva nikkhippa, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ||

4.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vipulāya ca pītiyā|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ saritvāna, paṃsukūlaṃ avandahaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, paṃsukūlamavandahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

7.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṃsukūlasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

paṃsukūlasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. buddhasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘ajjhāyakau  mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū|

lakkhaṇai itihāsai ca, sanighaṇḍusakaiṭubhai||

10.

 ‘‘nadīsautapaṭibhāgā, sissā āyanti mai tadā|

taisāhaṃ mantai mantaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) vācaimi, rattindivamatanditau||

11.

 ‘‘siddhatthau nāma sambuddhau, laukai uppaj‍ji tāvadai|

tamandhakāraṃ nāsaitvā, ñāṇālaukaṃ pavattayi||

12.

 ‘‘mama añ‍ñatarau sissau, sissānaṃ sau kathaisi mai|

sutvāna tai aitamatthaṃ, āraucaisuṃ mamaṃ tadā||

13.

 ‘‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakau|

tassānuvattati janau, lābhau amhaṃ na vij‍jati na haissati (sī॰ pī॰)||

14.

 ‘‘adhic‍cuppattikā buddhā, cakkhumantau mahāyasā|

yaṃnūnāhaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ, passaiyyaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘ajinaṃ  mai gahaitvāna, vākacīraṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ|

assamā abhinikkhamma, sissai āmantayiṃ ahaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘audumbarikapupphaṃva, candamhi sasakaṃ yathā|

vāyasānaṃ yathā khīraṃ, dul‍labhau laukanāyakau dul‍labhā laukanāyakā (sī॰), dul‍labhaṃ laukanāyakaṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)||

17.

 ‘‘buddhau laukamhi uppan‍nau, manussattampi dul‍labhaṃ|

ubhausu vij‍jamānaisu, savanañ‍ca sudul‍labhaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, cakkhuṃ lacchāma nau bhavaṃ|

aitha sabbai gamissāma, sammāsambuddhasantikaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘kamaṇḍaludharā  sabbai, kharājinanivāsinau|

tai jaṭā bhārabharitā, nikkhamuṃ vipinā tadā||

20.

 ‘‘yugamattaṃ paikkhamānā, uttamatthaṃ gavaisinau|

āsattidausarahitā, asambhītāva kaisarī||

21.

 ‘‘appakic‍cā alauluppā, nipakā santavuttinau|

uñchāya caramānā tai, buddhasaiṭṭhamupāgamuṃ||

22.

 ‘‘diyaḍḍhayaujanai  saisai, byādhi mai upapaj‍jatha|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ saritvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

25.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

buddhasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. bhisadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘augayha yaṃ augayhāhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) paukkharaṇiṃ, nānākuñ‍jarasaivitaṃ|

uddharāmi bhisaṃ tattha, ghāsahaitu asanahaitu (syā॰) ahaṃ tadā||

28.

 ‘‘bhagavā tamhi samayai, padumuttarasavhayau|

rattambaradharau rattakambaladharau (syā॰) buddhau, gacchati anilañ‍jasai||

29.

 ‘‘dhunantau paṃsukūlāni, saddamassausahaṃ tadā|

uddhaṃ nijjhāyamānauhaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘tatthaiva ṭhitakau santau, āyāciṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

madhuṃ bhisaihi savati, khīraṃ sappiṃ muḷālibhi||

31.

 ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai buddhau, anukampāya cakkhumā|

tatau kāruṇikau satthā, auruhitvā mahāyasau||

32.

 ‘‘paṭiggaṇhi mamaṃ bhikkhaṃ, anukampāya cakkhumā|

paṭiggahaitvā sambuddhau, akā mai anumaudanaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘‘sukhī  hautu hauhi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) mahāpuñ‍ña, gati tuyhaṃ samijjhatu|

iminā bhisadānaina, labhassu vipulaṃ sukhaṃ’||

34.

 ‘‘idaṃ  vatvāna sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau|

bhikkhamādāya sambuddhau, ambaraināgamā jinau||

35.

 ‘‘tatau  bhisaṃ gahaitvāna, āgacchiṃ mama assamaṃ|

bhisaṃ rukkhai lagaitvāna laggitvāna (syā॰ ka॰), mama dānamanussariṃ||

36.

 ‘‘mahāvātau vuṭṭhahitvā, sañ‍cālaisi vanaṃ tadā|

ākāsau abhinādittha, asaniyā phalantiyā||

37.

 ‘‘tatau mai asanipātau, matthakai nipatī tadā|

sauhaṃ nisin‍nakau santau, tattha kālaṅkatau ahuṃ||

38.

 ‘‘puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttau, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ|

kaḷaivaraṃ mai patitaṃ, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘chaḷasītisahassāni, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

sāyapātaṃ sāyaṃ pātaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) upaṭṭhanti, bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘manussayaunimāgantvā, sukhitau haumahaṃ sadā|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘anukampitakau taina, daivadaivaina tādinā|

sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

42.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ bhisaṃ bhikkhaṃ (sabbattha) adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

44.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhisadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

bhisadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. ñāṇathavikatthairaapadānaṃ

46.

 ‘‘dakkhiṇai  himavantassa, sukatau assamau mama|

uttamatthaṃ gavaisantau, vasāmi vipinai tadā||

47.

 ‘‘lābhālābhaina  santuṭṭhau, mūlaina ca phalaina ca|

anvaisantau ācariyaṃ, vasāmi aikakau ahaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘sumaidhau nāma sambuddhau, laukai uppaj‍ji tāvadai|

catusac‍caṃ pakāsaiti, uddharantau mahājanaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ suṇaumi sambuddhaṃ, napi mai kauci saṃsati bhāsati (sī॰), sāsati (syā॰ pī॰)|

aṭṭhavassai atik‍kantai, assausiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘aggidāruṃ  nīharitvā, sammaj‍jitvāna assamaṃ|

khāribhāraṃ gahaitvāna, nikkhamiṃ vipinā ahaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘aikarattiṃ  vasantauhaṃ, gāmaisu nigamaisu ca|

anupubbaina candavatiṃ, tadāhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ||

52.

 ‘‘bhagavā tamhi samayai, sumaidhau laukanāyakau|

uddharantau bahū sattai, daisaiti amataṃ padaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘janakāyamatik‍kamma, vanditvā jinasāgaraṃ|

aikaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘‘tuvaṃ satthā ca kaitu ca, dhajau yūpau ca pāṇinaṃ|

parāyanau parāyaṇau (sī॰ pī॰) patiṭṭhā ca, dīpau ca dvipaduttamau||

aikavīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

55.

 ‘‘‘naipuñ‍ñau dassanai vīrau, tāraisi janataṃ tuvaṃ|

natthañ‍ñau tārakau laukai, tavuttaritarau munai||

56.

 ‘‘‘sak‍kā thaivai havai (sī॰ pī॰) bhavai (syā॰ ka॰) kusaggaina, pamaituṃ sāgaruttamai sāgaruttamau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

natvaiva tava sabbañ‍ñu, ñāṇaṃ sak‍kā pamaitavai||

57.

 ‘‘‘tuladaṇḍai  tulamaṇḍalai (sī॰ pī॰) ṭhapaitvāna, mahiṃ mahī (syā॰ pī॰) sak‍kā dharaitavai|

natvaiva tava pañ‍ñāya, pamāṇamatthi cakkhuma||

58.

 ‘‘‘ākāsau minituṃ sak‍kā, raj‍juyā aṅgulaina vā|

natvaiva tava sabbañ‍ñu, sīlaṃ sak‍kā pamaitavai||

59.

 ‘‘‘mahāsamuddai  udakaṃ, ākāsau ca vasundharā|

parimaiyyāni aitāni, appamaiyyausi cakkhuma’||

60.

 ‘‘chahi  gāthāhi sabbañ‍ñuṃ, kittayitvā mahāyasaṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, tuṇhī aṭṭhāsahaṃ tadā||

61.

 ‘‘yaṃ vadanti sumaidhauti, bhūripañ‍ñaṃ sumaidhasaṃ|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

62.

 ‘‘‘yau mai ñāṇaṃ pakittaisi, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

63.

 ‘‘‘sattasattati kappāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

sahassakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

64.

 ‘‘‘anaikasatakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘‘daivabhūtau  manussau vā, puñ‍ñakammasamāhitau|

anūnamanasaṅkappau, tikkhapañ‍ñau bhavissati’||

66.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

67.

 ‘‘agārā abhinikkhamma, pabbajissati kiñ‍canau|

jātiyā sattavassaina, arahattaṃ phusissati||

68.

 ‘‘yatau sarāmi attānaṃ, yatau pattausmi sāsanaṃ|

aitthantarai na jānāmi, caitanaṃ amanauramaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘saṃsaritvā bhavai sabbai, sampattānubhaviṃ ahaṃ|

bhaugai  mai ūnatā natthi, phalaṃ ñāṇassa thaumanai||

70.

 ‘‘tiyaggī nibbutā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

71.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ ñāṇamathaviṃ ahaṃ mabhithaumayiṃ (sī॰ pī॰), mabhithaumahaṃ (syā॰)|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phalaṃ ñāṇassa thaumanai||

72.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

73.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

74.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇathavikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ñāṇathavikatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. candanamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

75.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai hitvā, piyarūpai manauramai|

asītikauṭiyau hitvā, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

76.

 ‘‘pabbajitvāna kāyaina, pāpakammaṃ vivaj‍jayiṃ|

vacīduc‍caritaṃ hitvā, nadīkūlai vasāmahaṃ||

77.

 ‘‘aikakaṃ  maṃ viharantaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhau upāgami|

nāhaṃ jānāmi buddhauti, akāsiṃ paṭisantharaṃ paṭisandhāraṃ (ka॰)||

78.

 ‘‘karitvā paṭisanthāraṃ, nāmagauttamapucchahaṃ|

‘daivatānusi gandhabbau, adu sak‍kau purindadau||

79.

 ‘‘‘kau vā tvaṃ kassa vā puttau, mahābrahmā idhāgatau|

viraucaisi disā sabbā, udayaṃ sūriyau yathā||

80.

 ‘‘‘sahassārāni  cak‍kāni, pādai dissanti mārisa|

kau vā tvaṃ kassa vā puttau, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ mayaṃ|

nāmagauttaṃ pavaidaihi, saṃsayaṃ apanaihi mai’||

81.

 ‘‘‘namhi daivau na gandhabbau, namhi nāpi (sī॰) sak‍kau purindadau|

brahmabhāvau ca mai natthi, aitaisaṃ uttamau ahaṃ||

82.

 ‘‘‘atītau visayaṃ taisaṃ, dālayiṃ kāmabandhanaṃ|

sabbai kilaisai jhāpaitvā, pattau sambaudhimuttamaṃ’||

83.

 ‘‘tassa  vācaṃ suṇitvāhaṃ, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ|

‘yadi buddhauti sabbañ‍ñū, nisīda tvaṃ mahāmunai||

84.

 ‘tamahaṃ pūjayissāmi, dukkhassantakarau tuvaṃ’|

‘‘pattharitvā jinacammaṃ, adāsi satthunau ahaṃ||

85.

 ‘‘nisīdi tattha bhagavā, sīhauva girigabbharai|

khippaṃ pabbatamāruyha, ambassa phalamaggahiṃ||

86.

 ‘‘sālakalyāṇikaṃ pupphaṃ, candanañ‍ca mahārahaṃ|

khippaṃ  paggayha taṃ sabbaṃ, upaitvā laukanāyakaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna, sālapupphamapūjayiṃ|

candanaṃ anulimpitvā, avandiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

88.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vipulāya ca pītiyā|

ajinamhi nisīditvā, sumaidhau laukanāyakau||

89.

 ‘‘mama kammaṃ pakittaisi, hāsayantau mamaṃ tadā|

‘iminā phaladānaina, gandhamālaihi cūbhayaṃ||

90.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍cavīsai kappasatai, daivalaukai ramissati|

anūnamanasaṅkappau, vasavattī bhavissati||

91.

 ‘‘‘chabbīsatikappasatai, manussattaṃ gamissati|

bhavissati cak‍kavattī, cāturantau mahiddhikau||

92.

 ‘‘‘vaibhāraṃ nāma nagaraṃ, vissakammaina māpitaṃ|

haissati sabbasauvaṇṇaṃ, nānāratanabhūsitaṃ||

93.

 ‘‘‘aitainaiva upāyaina, saṃsarissati sau bhavai yaunisau (syā॰ pī॰)|

sabbattha pūjitau hutvā, daivattai atha mānusai||

94.

 ‘‘‘pacchimai bhavai sampattai, brahmabandhu bhavissati|

agārā abhinikkhamma, anagārī bhavissati|

abhiñ‍ñāpāragū hutvā, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

95.

 ‘‘idaṃ  vatvāna sambuddhau, sumaidhau laukanāyakau|

mama nijjhāyamānassa, pak‍kāmi anilañ‍jasai||

96.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

97.

 ‘‘tusitatau  cavitvāna, nibbattiṃ mātukucchiyaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, yamhi gabbhai vasāmahaṃ||

98.

 ‘‘mātukucchigatai mayi, an‍napānañ‍ca bhaujanaṃ|

mātuyā mama chandaina, nibbattati yadicchakaṃ||

99.

 ‘‘jātiyā pañ‍cavassaina, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

auraupitamhi kaisamhi, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

100.

 ‘‘pubbakammaṃ gavaisantau, auraina nāddasaṃ ahaṃ|

tiṃsakappasahassamhi, mama kammamanussariṃ||

101.

 ‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

tava sāsanamāgamma, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

102.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

103.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

104.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

105.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā candanamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

candanamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dhātupūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

106.

 ‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, siddhatthai laukanāyakai|

mama ñātī samānaitvā, dhātupūjaṃ akāsahaṃ||

107.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dhātumabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhātupūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

108.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

109.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

110.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhātupūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

dhātupūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pulinuppādakatthairaapadānaṃ

111.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, daivalau nāma tāpasau|

tattha mai caṅkamau āsi, amanussaihi māpitau||

112.

 ‘‘jaṭābhāraina  jaṭābhārassa (syā॰ ka॰) bharitau, kamaṇḍaludharau sadā|

uttamatthaṃ gavaisantau, vipinā nikkhamiṃ tadā||

113.

 ‘‘cul‍lāsītisahassāni, sissā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhahuṃ|

sakakammābhipasutā, vasanti vipinai tadā||

114.

 ‘‘assamā abhinikkhamma, akaṃ pulinacaitiyaṃ|

nānāpupphaṃ samānaitvā, taṃ caitiyamapūjayiṃ||

115.

 ‘‘tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, assamaṃ pavisāmahaṃ|

sabbai sissā samāgantvā, aitamatthaṃ pucchiṃsu maṃ aitamatthamapucchu maṃ (sī॰), aitamattaṃ apucchiṃsu (syā॰ ka॰)||

116.

 ‘‘‘pulinaina katau thūpau katau thūpai (sī॰), yaṃ tvaṃ daiva daivaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) massati|

mayampi ñātumicchāma, puṭṭhau ācikkha nau tuvaṃ’||

117.

 ‘‘‘niddiṭṭhā nu niddiṭṭhā nau (sī॰ pī॰), diṭṭhānau vau (syā॰) mantapadai, cakkhumantau mahāyasā|

tai khau ahaṃ namassāmi, buddhasaiṭṭhai mahāyasai’||

118.

 ‘‘‘kīdisā tai mahāvīrā, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakā|

kathaṃvaṇṇā kathaṃsīlā, kīdisā tai mahāyasā’||

119.

 ‘‘‘bāttiṃsalakkhaṇā  buddhā, cattālīsadijāpi ca|

naittā gaupakhumā taisaṃ, jiñ‍jukā phalasan‍nibhā||

120.

 ‘‘‘gacchamānā ca tai buddhā, yugamattañ‍ca paikkharai|

na taisaṃ jāṇu nadati, sandhisaddau na suyyati||

121.

 ‘‘‘gacchamānā ca sugatā, uddharantāva gaccharai|

paṭhamaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ pādaṃ, buddhānaṃ aisa dhammatā||

122.

 ‘‘‘asambhītā  ca tai buddhā, migarājāva kaisarī|

naivuk‍kaṃsainti attānaṃ, nau ca vambhainti pāṇinaṃ||

123.

 ‘‘‘mānāvamānatau muttā, samā sabbaisu pāṇisu|

anattuk‍kaṃsakā buddhā, buddhānaṃ aisa dhammatā||

124.

 ‘‘‘uppaj‍jantā  ca sambuddhā, ālaukaṃ dassayanti tai|

chappakāraṃ pakampainti, kaivalaṃ vasudhaṃ imaṃ||

125.

 ‘‘‘passanti  nirayañ‍caitai, nibbāti nirayau tadā|

pavassati mahāmaighau, buddhānaṃ aisa dhammatā||

126.

 ‘‘‘īdisā tai mahānāgā, atulā ca tai (syā॰ ka॰) mahāyasā|

vaṇṇatau anatik‍kantā, appamaiyyā tathāgatā’||

127.

 ‘‘‘anumaudiṃsu mai vākyaṃ, sabbai sissā sagāravā|

tathā ca paṭipaj‍jiṃsu, yathāsatti yathābalaṃ’||

128.

 ‘‘paṭipūjainti pulinaṃ, sakakammābhilāsinau|

saddahantā mama vākyaṃ, buddhasak‍katamānasā buddhattagatamānasā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

129.

 ‘‘tadā cavitvā tusitā, daivaputtau mahāyasau|

uppaj‍ji mātukucchimhi, dasasahassi kampatha||

130.

 ‘‘assamassāvidūramhi, caṅkamamhi ṭhitau ahaṃ|

sabbai sissā samāgantvā, āgacchuṃ mama santikai||

131.

 ‘‘usabhauva mahī nadati, migarājāva kūjati|

susumārauva suṃsumārauva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) saḷati, kiṃ vipākau bhavissati||

132.

 ‘‘yaṃ  pakittaimi sambuddhaṃ, sikatāthūpasantikai|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, mātukucchimupāgami||

133.

 ‘‘taisaṃ dhammakathaṃ vatvā, kittayitvā mahāmuniṃ|

uyyaujaitvā sakai sissai, pal‍laṅkamābhujiṃ ahaṃ||

134.

 ‘‘balañ‍ca vata mai khīṇaṃ, byādhinā byādhitau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) paramaina taṃ|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ saritvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau kālakatau (sī॰ pī॰) ahaṃ||

135.

 ‘‘sabbai sissā samāgantvā, akaṃsu citakaṃ tadā|

kaḷaivarañ‍ca mai gayha, citakaṃ abhiraupayuṃ||

136.

 ‘‘citakaṃ parivāraitvā, sīsai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

saukasal‍laparaitā tai, vik‍kandiṃsu samāgatā||

137.

 ‘‘taisaṃ lālappamānānaṃ, agamaṃ citakaṃ tadā|

‘ahaṃ ācariyau tumhaṃ, mā saucittha sumaidhasā||

138.

 ‘‘‘sadatthai  vāyamaiyyātha, rattindivamatanditā|

mā vau pamattā ahuttha ahuvattha (sī॰), khaṇau vau paṭipāditau’||

139.

 ‘‘sakai sissainusāsitvā, daivalaukaṃ punāgamiṃ|

aṭṭhārasa ca kappāni, daivalaukai ramāmahaṃ||

140.

 ‘‘satānaṃ  pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

anaikasatakkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

141.

 ‘‘avasaisaisu kappaisu, vaukiṇṇau vaukiṇṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) saṃsariṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, uppādassa idaṃ phalaṃ pulinapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ (sī॰)||

142.

 ‘‘yathā  kaumudikai māsai, bahū pupphanti pādapā|

tathaivāhampi samayai, pupphitaumhi mahaisinā||

143.

 ‘‘vīriyaṃ mai dhuradhaurayhaṃ, yaugakkhaimādhivāhanaṃ|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

144.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

145.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

146.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

147.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pulinuppādakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

pulinuppādakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. taraṇiyatthairaapadānaṃ

148.

 ‘‘atthadassī tu bhagavā, sayambhū laukanāyakau|

vinatā nadiyā tīraṃ tīrai (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), upāgacchi tathāgatau||

149.

 ‘‘udakā abhinikkhamma, kacchapau vārigaucarau|

buddhaṃ tāraitukāmauhaṃ, upaisiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

150.

 ‘‘‘abhirūhatu  maṃ buddhau, atthadassī mahāmuni|

ahaṃ taṃ tārayissāmi, dukkhassantakarau tuvaṃ’||

151.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, atthadassī mahāyasau|

abhirūhitvā mai piṭṭhiṃ, aṭṭhāsi laukanāyakau||

152.

 ‘‘yatau sarāmi attānaṃ, yatau pattausmi viñ‍ñutaṃ|

sukhaṃ mai tādisaṃ natthi, phuṭṭhai pādatalai yathā||

153.

 ‘‘uttaritvāna sambuddhau, atthadassī mahāyasau|

nadītīramhi ṭhatvāna, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

154.

 ‘‘‘yāvatā  vattatai cittaṃ, gaṅgāsautaṃ tarāmahaṃ|

ayañ‍ca kacchapau rājā, tāraisi mama pañ‍ñavā||

155.

 ‘‘‘iminā buddhataraṇaina, maittacittavatāya ca|

aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, daivalaukai ramissati||

156.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukā idhāgantvā, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

aikāsanai nisīditvā, kaṅkhāsautaṃ tarissati||

157.

 ‘‘‘yathāpi  bhaddakai khaittai, bījaṃ appampi raupitaṃ|

sammādhārai pavacchantai, phalaṃ tausaiti kassakaṃ kassakai (syā॰)||

158.

 ‘‘‘tathaividaṃ buddhakhaittaṃ, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ|

sammādhārai pavacchantai, phalaṃ maṃ tausayissati’||

159.

 ‘‘padhānapahitattaumhi, upasantau nirūpadhi|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

160.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai  kappasatai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, taraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

161.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

162.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

163.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

taraṇiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhammaruciyatthairaapadānaṃ

164.

 ‘‘yadā  dīpaṅkarau buddhau, sumaidhaṃ byākarī jinau|

‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, ayaṃ buddhau bhavissati||

165.

 ‘‘‘imassa janikā mātā, māyā nāma bhavissati|

pitā suddhaudanau nāma, ayaṃ haissati gautamau||

166.

 ‘‘‘padhānaṃ padahitvāna, katvā duk‍karakārikaṃ|

assatthamūlai sambuddhau, bujjhissati mahāyasau||

167.

 ‘‘‘upatissau kaulitau ca, aggā haissanti sāvakā|

ānandau nāma nāmaina ānandau nāmupaṭṭhākau (syā॰), upaṭṭhissatimaṃ jinaṃ||

168.

 ‘‘‘khaimā  uppalavaṇṇā ca, aggā haissanti sāvikā|

cittau āḷavakau caiva, aggā haissantupāsakā||

169.

 ‘‘‘khuj‍juttarā  nandamātā, aggā haissantupāsikā|

baudhi imassa vīrassa, assatthauti pavuc‍cati’||

170.

 ‘‘idaṃ  sutvāna vacanaṃ, asamassa mahaisinau|

āmauditā naramarū, namassanti katañ‍jalī||

171.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ māṇavau āsiṃ, maighau nāma susikkhitau|

sutvā byākaraṇaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, sumaidhassa mahāmunai||

172.

 ‘‘saṃvisaṭṭhau bhavitvāna, sumaidhai karuṇāsayai karuṇālayai (syā॰)|

pabbajantañ‍ca taṃ vīraṃ, sahāva anupabbajiṃ||

173.

 ‘‘saṃvutau pātimaukkhasmiṃ, indriyaisu ca pañ‍casu|

suddhājīvau satau vīrau, jinasāsanakārakau||

174.

 ‘‘aivaṃ viharamānauhaṃ, pāpamittaina kainaci|

niyaujitau anācārai, sumaggā paridhaṃsitau||

175.

 ‘‘vitak‍kavasikau hutvā, sāsanatau apak‍kamiṃ|

pacchā taina kumittaina, payuttau mātughātanaṃ||

176.

 ‘‘akariṃ ānantariyaṃ akariṃ nantariyañ‍ca (syā॰ ka॰), ghātayiṃ duṭṭhamānasau|

tatau cutau mahāvīciṃ, upapan‍nau sudāruṇaṃ||

177.

 ‘‘vinipātagatau  santau, saṃsariṃ dukkhitau ciraṃ|

na punau addasaṃ vīraṃ, sumaidhaṃ narapuṅgavaṃ||

178.

 ‘‘asmiṃ  kappai samuddamhi, macchau āsiṃ timiṅgalau|

disvāhaṃ sāgarai nāvaṃ, gaucaratthamupāgamiṃ||

179.

 ‘‘disvā maṃ vāṇijā bhītā, buddhasaiṭṭhamanussaruṃ|

gautamauti mahāghausaṃ, sutvā taihi udīritaṃ||

180.

 ‘‘pubbasañ‍ñaṃ saritvāna, tatau kālaṅkatau ahaṃ|

sāvatthiyaṃ kulai iddhai, jātau brāhmaṇajātiyaṃ||

181.

 ‘‘āsiṃ dhammaruci nāma, sabbapāpajigucchakau|

disvāhaṃ laukapaj‍jautaṃ, jātiyā sattavassikau||

182.

 ‘‘mahājaitavanaṃ  gantvā, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

upaimi buddhaṃ tikkhattuṃ, rattiyā divasassa ca||

183.

 ‘‘tadā disvā muni āha, ciraṃ dhammarucīti maṃ|

tatauhaṃ avacaṃ buddhaṃ, pubbakammapabhāvitaṃ||

184.

 ‘‘suciraṃ satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇaṃ, patipubbaina visuddhapac‍cayaṃ|

ahamaj‍jasupaikkhanaṃ  vata, tava passāmi nirupamaṃ viggahaṃ nirūpamaggahaṃ (sī॰)||

185.

 ‘‘suciraṃ vihatattamau mayā, sucirakkhaina nadī visausitā|

suciraṃ amalaṃ visaudhitaṃ, nayanaṃ ñāṇamayaṃ mahāmunai||

186.

 ‘‘cirakālasamaṅgitau  cirakālaṃ samāgatau (pī॰) tayā, avinaṭṭhau punarantaraṃ ciraṃ|

punaraj‍jasamāgatau tayā, na hi nassanti katāni gautama||

187.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

188.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

189.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammaruciyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

dhammaruciyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. sālamaṇḍapiyatthairaapadānaṃ

190.

 ‘‘ajjhaugāhaitvā  sālavanaṃ, sukatau assamau mama|

sālapupphaihi sañchan‍nau, vasāmi vipinai tadā||

191.

 ‘‘piyadassī ca bhagavā, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, sālavanamupāgami||

192.

 ‘‘assamā abhinikkhamma, pavanaṃ agamāsahaṃ|

mūlaphalaṃ gavaisantau, āhiṇḍāmi vanai tadā||

193.

 ‘‘tatthaddasāsiṃ  sambuddhaṃ, piyadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ|

sunisin‍naṃ samāpan‍naṃ, viraucantaṃ mahāvanai||

194.

 ‘‘catudaṇḍai  ṭhapaitvāna, buddhassa uparī ahaṃ|

maṇḍapaṃ sukataṃ katvā, sālapupphaihi chādayiṃ||

195.

 ‘‘sattāhaṃ dhārayitvāna, maṇḍapaṃ sālachāditaṃ|

tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, buddhasaiṭṭhamavandahaṃ||

196.

 ‘‘bhagavā  tamhi samayai, vuṭṭhahitvā samādhitau|

yugamattaṃ paikkhamānau, nisīdi purisuttamau||

197.

 ‘‘sāvakau varuṇau nāma, piyadassissa satthunau|

vasīsatasahassaihi, upagacchi vināyakaṃ||

198.

 ‘‘piyadassī ca bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, sitaṃ pātukarī jinau||

199.

 ‘‘anuruddhau upaṭṭhākau, piyadassissa satthunau|

aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā, apucchittha mahāmuniṃ||

200.

 ‘‘‘kau nu khau bhagavā haitu, sitakammassa satthunau|

kāraṇai vij‍jamānamhi, satthā pātukarai sitaṃ’||

201.

 ‘‘‘sattāhaṃ sālacchadanaṃ pupphachadanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), yau mai dhāraisi māṇavau|

tassa kammaṃ saritvāna, sitaṃ pātukariṃ ahaṃ||

202.

 ‘‘‘anaukāsaṃ na passāmi, yattha yaṃ taṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) puñ‍ñaṃ vipac‍cati|

daivalaukai  manussai vā, aukāsauva na sammati||

203.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukai  vasantassa, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginau|

yāvatā parisā tassa, sālacchan‍nā bhavissati||

204.

 ‘‘‘tattha dibbaihi nac‍caihi, gītaihi vāditaihi ca|

ramissati sadā santau, puñ‍ñakammasamāhitau||

205.

 ‘‘‘yāvatā parisā tassa, gandhagandhī bhavissati|

sālassa pupphavassau ca, pavassissati tāvadai||

206.

 ‘‘‘tatau cutauyaṃ manujau, mānusaṃ āgamissati|

idhāpi sālacchadanaṃ, sabbakālaṃ dharissati dhariyati (sī॰ pī॰)||

207.

 ‘‘‘idha  nac‍cañ‍ca gītañ‍ca, sammatāḷasamāhitaṃ|

parivāraissanti maṃ nic‍caṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

208.

 ‘‘‘uggacchantai ca sūriyai, sālavassaṃ pavassati|

puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttaṃ, vassatai sabbakālikaṃ||

209.

 ‘‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

210.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

211.

 ‘‘‘dhammaṃ abhisamaintassa, sālacchan‍naṃ bhavissati|

citakai jhāyamānassa, chadanaṃ tattha haissati’||

212.

 ‘‘vipākaṃ kittayitvāna, piyadassī mahāmuni|

parisāya dhammaṃ daisaisi, tappaintau dhammavuṭṭhiyā||

213.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappāni  daivaisu, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

saṭṭhi ca sattakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

214.

 ‘‘daivalaukā idhāgantvā, labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ|

idhāpi sālacchadanaṃ, maṇḍapassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

215.

 ‘‘ayaṃ pacchimakau mayhaṃ, carimau vattatai bhavau|

idhāpi sālacchadanaṃ, haissati sabbakālikaṃ||

216.

 ‘‘mahāmuniṃ tausayitvā, gautamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

217.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai  kappasatai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

218.

 kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

219.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

220.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā sālamaṇḍapiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sālamaṇḍapiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

paṃsukūlavaggau aikūnapañ‍ñāsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

paṃsukūlaṃ  buddhasañ‍ñī, bhisadau ñāṇakittakau|

candanī dhātupūjī ca, pulinuppādakaupi ca||

taraṇau dhammarucikau, sālamaṇḍapiyau tathā|

satāni dvai haunti gāthā, ūnavīsatimaiva ca||


50. kiṅkaṇipupphavaggau

1. tikiṅkaṇipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, vipassiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘tīṇi kiṅkaṇipupphāni, paggayha abhiraupayiṃ|

sambuddhamabhipūjaitvā, gacchāmi dakkhiṇāmukhau||

3.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

6.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikiṅkaṇipupphiyau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tikiṅkaṇipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṃsukūlapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

8.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, udaṅgaṇau nāma pabbatau|

tatthaddasaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ, dumaggamhi vilambitaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘tīṇi kiṅkaṇipupphāni, aucinitvānahaṃ tadā|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, paṃsukūlamapūjayiṃ||

10.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pūjitvā arahaddhajaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

13.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṃsukūlapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

paṃsukūlapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. kauraṇḍapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

15.

 ‘‘vanakammikau  purai āsiṃ, pitumātumatainahaṃ pitupaitāmahainahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

pasumāraina jīvāmi, kusalaṃ mai na vij‍jati||

16.

 ‘‘mama āsayasāmantā, tissau laukagganāyakau|

padāni tīṇi dassaisi, anukampāya cakkhumā||

17.

 ‘‘ak‍kantai ca padai disvā, tissanāmassa satthunau|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, padai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

18.

 ‘‘kauraṇḍaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ|

sakausakaṃ gahaitvāna, padasaiṭṭhamapūjayiṃ padasaiṭṭhai apūjayiṃ (sī॰ pī॰)||

19.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

kauraṇḍakachavi haumi, suppabhāsau sapabhāsau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhavāmahaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, padapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

23.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kauraṇḍapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kauraṇḍapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. kiṃsukapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

25.

 ‘‘kiṃsukaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, paggahaitvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ saritvāna, ākāsai abhipūjayiṃ||

26.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

29.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kiṃsukapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kiṃsukapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. upaḍḍhadussadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘padumuttarabhagavatau, sujātau nāma sāvakau|

paṃsukūlaṃ gavaisantau, saṅkārai caratai caratī (sī॰ ka॰) tadā||

32.

 ‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, paraisaṃ bhatakau ahaṃ|

upaḍḍhadussaṃ datvāna, sirasā abhivādayiṃ||

33.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘taittiṃsakkhattuṃ  daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

sattasattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

upaḍḍhadussadānaina, maudāmi akutaubhayau||

36.

 ‘‘icchamānau cahaṃ aj‍ja, sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ|

khaumadussaihi chādaiyyaṃ, aḍḍhadussassidaṃ phalaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aḍḍhadussassidaṃ phalaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

39.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upaḍḍhadussadāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

upaḍḍhadussadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ghatamaṇḍadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

41.

 ‘‘sucintitaṃ bhagavantaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

upaviṭṭhaṃ mahārañ‍ñaṃ, vātābādhaina pīḷitaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘disvā  cittaṃ pasādaitvā, ghatamaṇḍamupānayiṃ|

katattā ācitattā upacitattā (syā॰ ka॰) ca, gaṅgā bhāgīrathī ayaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘mahāsamuddā cattārau, ghataṃ sampaj‍jarai mama|

ayañ‍ca pathavī ghaurā, appamāṇā asaṅkhiyā||

44.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, bhavatai madhusak‍karā madhusakkharā (syā॰ ka॰)|

cātuddīpā imai rukkhā, pādapā dharaṇīruhā||

45.

 ‘‘mama  saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, kapparukkhā bhavanti tai|

pañ‍ñāsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

46.

 ‘‘aikapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau chanavutai itau (sī॰) kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ghatamaṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

49.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ghatamaṇḍadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ghatamaṇḍadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. udakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, bhikkhusaṅghai anuttarai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pānīghaṭamapūrayiṃ||

52.

 ‘‘pabbataggai dumaggai vā, ākāsai vātha bhūmiyaṃ|

yadā pānīyamicchāmi, khippaṃ nibbattatai mama||

53.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dakadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

55.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

udakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. pulinathūpiyatthairaapadānaṃ

57.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, yamakau nāma pabbatau|

assamau sukatau mayhaṃ, paṇṇasālā sumāpitā||

58.

 ‘‘nāradau nāma nāmaina, jaṭilau uggatāpanau|

catuddasasahassāni, sissā paricaranti maṃ||

59.

 ‘‘paṭisal‍līnakau santau, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā|

‘sabbau janau maṃ pūjaiti, nāhaṃ pūjaimi kiñ‍canaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘‘na mai auvādakau atthi, vattā kauci na vij‍jati|

anācariyupajjhāyau, vanai vāsaṃ upaimahaṃ||

61.

 ‘‘‘upāsamānau yamahaṃ, garucittaṃ upaṭṭhahai|

sau mai ācariyau natthi, vanavāsau niratthakau||

62.

 ‘‘‘āyāgaṃ mai gavaisissaṃ, garuṃ bhāvaniyaṃ tathā|

sāvassayau vasissāmi, na kauci garahissati’||

63.

 ‘‘uttānakūlā nadikā, supatitthā manauramā|

saṃsuddhapulinākiṇṇā, avidūrai mamassamaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘nadiṃ  amarikaṃ nāma, upagantvānahaṃ tadā|

saṃvaḍḍhayitvā pulinaṃ, akaṃ pulinacaitiyaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘yai  tai ahaisuṃ sambuddhā, bhavantakaraṇā munī|

taisaṃ aitādisau thūpau, taṃ nimittaṃ karaumahaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘karitvā  pulinaṃ puḷinai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) thūpaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ māpayiṃ ahaṃ|

sauṇṇakiṅkaṇipupphāni, sahassai tīṇi pūjayiṃ||

67.

 ‘‘sāyapātaṃ namassāmi, vaidajātau katañ‍jalī|

sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ, vandiṃ pulinacaitiyaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘yadā kilaisā jāyanti, vitak‍kā gaihanissitā|

sarāmi sukataṃ thūpaṃ, pac‍cavaikkhāmi tāvadai||

69.

 ‘‘upanissāya viharaṃ, satthavāhaṃ vināyakaṃ|

kilaisai saṃvasaiyyāsi, na yuttaṃ tava mārisa||

70.

 ‘‘saha  āvaj‍jitai thūpai, gāravaṃ hauti mai tadā|

kuvitak‍kai vinaudaisiṃ, nāgau tuttaṭṭitau yathā||

71.

 ‘‘aivaṃ viharamānaṃ maṃ, mac‍curājābhimaddatha|

tattha kālaṅkatau santau, brahmalaukamagacchahaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna, tidivai tidasai (sī॰ pī॰) upapaj‍jahaṃ|

asītikkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

73.

 ‘‘satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

74.

 ‘‘sauṇṇakiṅkaṇipupphānaṃ  taisaṃ kiṅkaṇipupphānaṃ (sī॰), vipākaṃ anubhaumahaṃ|

dhātīsatasahassāni, parivārainti maṃ mai (ka॰) bhavai||

75.

 ‘‘thūpassa pariciṇṇattā, rajaujal‍laṃ na limpati|

gattai saidā na muc‍canti, suppabhāsau bhavāmahaṃ||

76.

 ‘‘ahau mai sukatau thūpau, sudiṭṭhāmarikā nadī|

thūpaṃ katvāna pulinaṃ, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

77.

 ‘‘kusalaṃ kattukāmaina, jantunā sāragāhinā|

natthi khaittaṃ akhaittaṃ vā, paṭipattīva sādhakā sārikā (pī॰), sārakā (syā॰), sāratā (ka॰)||

78.

 ‘‘yathāpi balavā pausau, aṇṇavaṃ taritussahai|

parittaṃ kaṭṭhamādāya, pakkhandaiyya mahāsaraṃ||

79.

 ‘‘imāhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ nissāya, tarissāmi mahaudadhiṃ|

ussāhaina vīriyaina, taraiyya udadhiṃ narau||

80.

 ‘‘tathaiva  mai kataṃ kammaṃ, parittaṃ thaukakañ‍ca yaṃ|

taṃ kammaṃ upanissāya, saṃsāraṃ samatik‍kamiṃ||

81.

 ‘‘pacchimai bhavai sampattai, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sāvatthiyaṃ purai jātau, mahāsālai suaḍḍhakai||

82.

 ‘‘saddhā mātā pitā mayhaṃ, buddhassa saraṇaṃ gatā|

ubhau diṭṭhapadā aitai, anuvattanti sāsanaṃ||

83.

 ‘‘baudhipapaṭikaṃ  gayha, sauṇṇathūpamakārayuṃ|

sāyapātaṃ sāyaṃ pātaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) namassanti, sakyaputtassa sammukhā||

84.

 ‘‘upausathamhi  divasai, sauṇṇathūpaṃ vinīharuṃ|

buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ kittaintā, tiyāmaṃ vītināmayuṃ||

85.

 ‘‘saha disvānahaṃ pasādaitvānahaṃ (ka॰) thūpaṃ, sariṃ pulinacaitiyaṃ|

aikāsanai nisīditvā, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

dvāvīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

86.

 ‘‘gavaisamānau taṃ vīraṃ, dhammasaināpatiddasaṃ|

agārā nikkhamitvāna, pabbajiṃ tassa santikai||

87.

 ‘‘jātiyā sattavassaina, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ|

upasampādayī buddhau, guṇamañ‍ñāya cakkhumā||

88.

 ‘‘dārakainaiva santaina, kiriyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ mayā|

kataṃ mai karaṇīyaj‍ja, sakyaputtassa sāsanai||

89.

 ‘‘sabbavairabhayātītau, sabbasaṅgātigau sabbasaṅkātitau (ka॰) isi|

sāvakau tai mahāvīra, sauṇṇathūpassidaṃ phalaṃ||

90.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

91.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

92.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā pulinathūpiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

pulinathūpiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. naḷakuṭidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

93.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, hāritau nāma pabbatau|

sayambhū nāradau nāma, rukkhamūlai vasī tadā||

94.

 ‘‘naḷāgāraṃ  karitvāna, tiṇaina chādayiṃ ahaṃ|

caṅkamaṃ saudhayitvāna, sayambhussa adāsahaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

96.

 ‘‘tattha mai sukataṃ byamhaṃ, naḷakuṭikanimmitaṃ|

saṭṭhiyaujanamubbaidhaṃ, tiṃsayaujanavitthataṃ||

97.

 ‘‘catuddasaisu kappaisu, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ|

aikasattatikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

98.

 ‘‘catutiṃsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

99.

 ‘‘dhammapāsādamāruyha, sabbākāravarūpamaṃ|

yadicchakāhaṃ viharai, sakyaputtassa sāsanai||

100.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, naḷakuṭiyidaṃ phalaṃ||

101.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

102.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

103.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷakuṭidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

naḷakuṭidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. piyālaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

104.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, vipinai vicaraṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ||

105.

 ‘‘piyālaphalamādāya, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

puñ‍ñakkhaittassa vīrassa, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

106.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

107.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

108.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

109.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piyālaphaladāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

piyālaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

kiṅkaṇipupphavaggau pañ‍ñāsamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kiṅkaṇī paṃsukūlañ‍ca, kauraṇḍamatha kiṃsukaṃ|

upaḍḍhadussī ghatadau, udakaṃ thūpakārakau||

naḷakārī ca navamau, piyālaphaladāyakau|

satamaikañ‍ca gāthānaṃ, navakañ‍ca taduttari||

atha vagguddānaṃ –

maittaiyyavaggau bhaddāli, sakiṃsammaj‍jakaupi ca|

aikavihārī vibhītakī, jagatī sālapupphiyau||

naḷāgāraṃ paṃsukūlaṃ, kiṅkaṇipupphiyau tathā|

asīti dvai ca gāthāyau, catuddasasatāni ca||

maittaiyyavaggadasakaṃ|


pañ‍camasatakaṃ samattaṃ|


51. kaṇikāravaggau

1. tikaṇikārapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘sumaidhau  nāma sambuddhau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, himavantamupāgamiṃ||

2.

 ‘‘ajjhaugayha himavantaṃ, aggau kāruṇikau muni|

pal‍laṅkamābhujitvāna, nisīdi purisuttamau||

3.

 ‘‘vij‍jādharau tadā āsiṃ, antalikkhacarau ahaṃ|

tisūlaṃ sukataṃ gayha, gacchāmi ambarai tadā||

4.

 ‘‘pabbataggai yathā aggi, puṇṇamāyaiva candimā|

vanai aubhāsatai buddhau, sālarājāva phul‍litau||

5.

 ‘‘vanaggā  nikkhamitvāna, buddharaṃsībhidhāvarai|

naḷaggivaṇṇasaṅkāsā, disvā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

6.

 ‘‘vicinaṃ addasaṃ pupphaṃ, kaṇikāraṃ daivagandhikaṃ|

tīṇi pupphāni ādāya, buddhasaiṭṭhamapūjayiṃ||

7.

 ‘‘buddhassa ānubhāvaina, tīṇi pupphāni mai tadā|

uddhaṃvaṇṭā adhaupattā, chāyaṃ kubbanti satthunau||

8.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘tattha  mai sukataṃ byamhaṃ, kaṇikārīti ñāyati|

saṭṭhiyaujanamubbaidhaṃ, tiṃsayaujanavitthataṃ||

10.

 ‘‘sahassakaṇḍaṃ satabhaiṇḍu satagaiṇḍu (syā॰), dhajāluharitāmayaṃ|

satasahassaniyyūhā, byamhai pātubhaviṃsu mai||

11.

 ‘‘sauṇṇamayā maṇimayā, lauhitaṅgamayāpi ca|

phalikāpi ca pal‍laṅkā, yainicchakā yadicchakā yainicchayā yadicchakaṃ (syā॰), yadicchakāyadicchakā (ka॰)||

12.

 ‘‘mahārahañ‍ca  sayanaṃ, tūlikāvikatīyutaṃ|

uddhalaumikaaikantaṃ, bimbauhanasamāyutaṃ bibbauhanasamāyutaṃ… (syā॰ ka॰)||

13.

 ‘‘bhavanā nikkhamitvāna, carantau daivacārikaṃ|

yadā icchāmi gamanaṃ, daivasaṅghapurakkhatau||

14.

 ‘‘pupphassa haiṭṭhā tiṭṭhāmi, uparicchadanaṃ mama|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, kaṇikāraihi chāditaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘saṭṭhituriyasahassāni, sāyapātamupaṭṭhahuṃ|

parivārainti maṃ nic‍caṃ, rattindivamatanditā||

16.

 ‘‘tattha nac‍caihi gītaihi, tāḷaihi vāditaihi ca|

ramāmi khiḍḍāratiyā, maudāmi kāmakāmihaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘tattha bhutvā pivitvā ca, maudāmi tidasai tadā|

nārīgaṇaihi sahitau, maudāmi byamhamuttamai||

18.

 ‘‘satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ  vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘bhavābhavai saṃsarantau, mahābhaugaṃ labhāmahaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘duvai  bhavai saṃsarāmi, daivattai atha mānusai|

añ‍ñaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘duvai kulai pajāyāmi, khattiyai cāpi brāhmaṇai|

nīcai kulai na jāyāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, sivikaṃ sandamānikaṃ|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘dāsīgaṇaṃ dāsagaṇaṃ, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘kausaiyyakambaliyāni, khaumakappāsikāni ca|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘navavatthaṃ  navaphalaṃ, navaggarasabhaujanaṃ|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘imaṃ khāda imaṃ bhuñ‍ja, imamhi sayanai saya|

labhāmi sabbamaivaitaṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘sabbattha pūjitau haumi, yasau abbhuggatau mama|

mahāpakkhau sadā haumi, abhaij‍japarisau sadā|

ñātīnaṃ uttamau haumi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘sītaṃ  uṇhaṃ na jānāmi, pariḷāhau na vij‍jati|

athau caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, hadayai mai na vij‍jati||

29.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau hutvāna, saṃsarāmi bhavābhavai|

vaivaṇṇiyaṃ na jānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sāvatthiyaṃ purai jātau, mahāsālai suaḍḍhakai||

31.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai hitvā, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

32.

 ‘‘upasampādayī buddhau, guṇamañ‍ñāya cakkhumā|

taruṇau pūjanīyauhaṃ, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘dibbacakkhu visuddhaṃ mai, samādhikusalau ahaṃ|

abhiñ‍ñāpāramippattau, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā anuppattau, iddhipādaisu kauvidau|

dhammaisu pāramippattau, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

37.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikaṇikārapupphiyau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tikaṇikārapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aikapattadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, kumbhakārau ahausahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘sukataṃ mattikāpattaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

pattaṃ datvā bhagavatau, ujubhūtassa tādinau||

41.

 ‘‘bhavai nibbattamānauhaṃ, sauṇṇathālai labhāmahaṃ|

rūpimayai ca sauvaṇṇai, taṭṭikai ca maṇīmayai||

42.

 ‘‘pātiyau paribhuñ‍jāmi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ|

yasānañ‍ca dhanānañ‍ca yasasāva janānañ‍ca (syā॰), aggabhūtau pattabhūtau (sī॰ pī॰) ca haumahaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘yathāpi bhaddakai khaittai, bījaṃ appampi raupitaṃ|

sammādhāraṃ pavacchantai, phalaṃ tausaiti kassakaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘tathaividaṃ pattadānaṃ, buddhakhaittamhi raupitaṃ|

pītidhārai pavassantai, phalaṃ maṃ tausayissati||

45.

 ‘‘yāvatā khaittā vij‍janti, saṅghāpi ca gaṇāpi ca|

buddhakhaittasamau natthi, sukhadau sabbapāṇinaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘namau  tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

aikapattaṃ daditvāna, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pattamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

49.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikapattadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

aikapattadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. kāsumāraphaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

kāsumārikamādāya, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

55.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kāsumāraphaliyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kāsumāraphaliyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. avaṭaphaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

57.

 ‘‘sahassaraṃsī bhagavā, sayambhū aparājitau|

vivaikā uṭṭhahitvāna, gaucarāyābhinikkhami||

58.

 ‘‘phalahatthau  ahaṃ disvā, upagacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, avaṭaṃ adadiṃ phalaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

61.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā avaṭaphaliyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

avaṭaphaliyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. pādaphaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

63.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, pādaphalaṃ vāraphalaṃ (sī॰), cāraphalaṃ (syā॰), pāraphalaṃ (pī॰) adāsahaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

6. mātuluṅgaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

68.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jalitaṃ, puṇṇamāyaiva candimaṃ|

jalantaṃ dīparukkhaṃva, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘mātuluṅgaphalaṃ  gayha, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ|

dakkhiṇaiyyassa vīrassa dhīrassa (sī॰), pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

70.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mātuluṅgaphaladāyakau thairau imā

mātuluṅgaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ajailiphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

74.

 ‘‘aj‍junau ajinau (syā॰) nāma sambuddhau, himavantai vasī tadā|

caraṇaina ca sampan‍nau, samādhikusalau muni||

75.

 ‘‘kumbhamattaṃ  gahaitvāna, ajailiṃ añ‍jaliṃ (syā॰), ajailaṃ (pī॰) jīvajīvakaṃ|

chattapaṇṇaṃ gahaitvāna, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

76.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

10. nāḷikairaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

91.

 ‘‘nagarai  bandhumatiyā, ārāmikau ahaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

92.

 ‘‘nāḷikairaphalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

ākāsai ṭhitakau santau, paṭiggaṇhi mahāyasau||

93.

 ‘‘vittisañ‍jananau  mayhaṃ, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahau|

phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

94.

 ‘‘adhigacchiṃ tadā pītiṃ, vipulañ‍ca sukhuttamaṃ|

uppaj‍jataiva ratanaṃ, nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ||

95.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

96.

 ‘‘dibbacakkhu visuddhaṃ mai, samādhikusalau ahaṃ|

abhiñ‍ñāpāramippattau, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

97.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

98.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰…  kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

99.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāḷikairaphaladāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nāḷikairaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

kaṇikāravaggau aikapañ‍ñāsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kaṇikāraikapattā ca, kāsumārī tathāvaṭā|

pādañ‍ca mātuluṅgañ‍ca, ajailīmaudamaiva ca||

tālaṃ tathā nāḷikairaṃ, gāthāyau gaṇitā viha|

aikaṃ gāthāsataṃ hauti, ūnādhikavivaj‍jitaṃ||


52. phaladāyakavaggau

1. kurañ‍ciyaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, vipinai vicaraṃ ahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ||

2.

 ‘‘kurañ‍ciyaphalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

puñ‍ñakkhaittassa tādinau, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

3.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

5.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, buddhasaiṭṭhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kurañ‍ciyaphaladāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kurañ‍ciyaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

6. aj‍junapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

28.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, ahausiṃ kin‍narau tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vaidajātau katañ‍jalī|

gahaitvā aj‍junaṃ pupphaṃ, sayambhuṃ abhipūjayiṃ||

30.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā kin‍naraṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘chattiṃsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

dasakkhattuṃ cak‍kavattī, mahāraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

32.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

sukhaittai vappitaṃ bījaṃ, sayambhumhi ahau mama ahausi mai (syā॰)||

33.

 ‘‘kusalaṃ vij‍jatai mayhaṃ, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

pūjārahau ahaṃ aj‍ja, sakyaputtassa sāsanai||

34.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

35.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā aj‍junapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

aj‍junapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kuṭajapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, vasalau cāvalau (sī॰ pī॰), ac‍cayau (syā॰) nāma pabbatau|

buddhau sudassanau nāma, vasatai pabbatantarai||

38.

 ‘‘pupphaṃ haimavantaṃ gayha, vaihāsaṃ agamāsahaṃ|

tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘pupphaṃ  kuṭajamādāya, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ katvānahaṃ tadā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sayambhussa mahaisinau||

40.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

42.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭajapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kuṭajapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ghausasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

44.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai vipinai ahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, daivasaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

45.

 ‘‘catusac‍caṃ pakāsaintaṃ, daisaintaṃ amataṃ padaṃ|

assausiṃ madhuraṃ dhammaṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

46.

 ‘‘ghausai cittaṃ pasādaisiṃ, asamappaṭipuggalai|

tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, uttariṃ atariṃ (sī॰ pī॰) duttaraṃ bhavaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ghausasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

49.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ghausasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

ghausasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sabbaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘varuṇau  nāma nāmaina, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

chaḍḍaitvā dasaputtāni, vanamajjhaugahiṃ tadā||

52.

 ‘‘assamaṃ  sukataṃ katvā, suvibhattaṃ manauramaṃ|

paṇṇasālaṃ karitvāna, vasāmi vipinai ahaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi mama assamaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘yāvatā vanasaṇḍamhi, aubhāsau vipulau ahu|

buddhassa ānubhāvaina, paj‍jalī vipinaṃ tadā||

55.

 ‘‘disvāna taṃ pāṭihīraṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

pattapuṭaṃ gahaitvāna, phalaina pūjayiṃ ahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ, sahakhārimadāsahaṃ|

anukampāya mai buddhau, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

57.

 ‘khāribhāraṃ gahaitvāna, pacchatau aihi mai tuvaṃ|

paribhuttai ca saṅghamhi, puñ‍ñaṃ tava bhavissati’||

58.

 ‘‘puṭakantaṃ gahaitvāna, bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ|

tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘tattha dibbaihi nac‍caihi, gītaihi vāditaihi ca|

puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttaṃ, anubhaumi sadā sukhaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘yaṃ  yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

61.

 ‘‘yāvatā caturau dīpā, sasamuddā sapabbatā|

phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna, issaraṃ kārayāmahaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘yāvatā mai pakkhigaṇā, ākāsai uppatanti cai|

taipi maṃ vasamanvainti, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘yāvatā vanasaṇḍamhi, yakkhā bhūtā ca rakkhasā|

kumbhaṇḍā garuḷā cāpi, pāricariyaṃ upainti mai||

64.

 ‘‘kumbhā  sauṇā madhukārā, ḍaṃsā ca makasā ubhau|

taipi maṃ vasamanvainti, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘supaṇṇā  nāma sakuṇā, pakkhijātā mahabbalā|

taipi maṃ saraṇaṃ yanti, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘yaipi dīghāyukā nāgā, iddhimantau mahāyasā|

taipi maṃ vasamanvainti, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

67.

 ‘‘sīhā byagghā ca dīpī ca, acchakaukataracchakā|

taipi maṃ vasamanvainti, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘ausadhītiṇavāsī ca, yai ca ākāsavāsinau|

sabbai maṃ saraṇaṃ yanti, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘sududdasaṃ sunipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ suppakāsitaṃ|

phassayitvā phusayitvā (ka॰) viharāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

70.

 ‘‘vimaukkhai  aṭṭha phusitvā, viharāmi anāsavau|

ātāpī nipakau cāhaṃ, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

71.

 ‘‘yai phalaṭṭhā buddhaputtā, khīṇadausā mahāyasā|

ahamañ‍ñatarau taisaṃ, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘abhiñ‍ñāpāramiṃ gantvā, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

73.

 ‘‘taivij‍jā iddhipattā ca, buddhaputtā mahāyasā|

dibbasautasamāpan‍nā, taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau ahaṃ||

74.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

76.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

77.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sabbaphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sabbaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. padumadhārikatthairaapadānaṃ

78.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, raumasau nāma pabbatau|

buddhaupi sambhavau nāma, abbhaukāsai vasī tadā||

79.

 ‘‘bhavanā nikkhamitvāna, padumaṃ dhārayiṃ ahaṃ|

aikāhaṃ dhārayitvāna, bhavanaṃ punarāgamiṃ||

80.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

81.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

82.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

83.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumadhārikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

padumadhārikatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

phaladāyakavaggau dvaipañ‍ñāsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kurañ‍ciyaṃ kapitthañ‍ca, kausambamatha kaitakaṃ|

nāgapupphaj‍junañ‍caiva, kuṭajī ghausasañ‍ñakau||

thairau  ca sabbaphaladau, tathā padumadhārikau|

asīti caittha gāthāyau, tissau gāthā taduttari||


53. tiṇadāyakavaggau

1. tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, lambakau nāma pabbatau|

tatthaiva tissau tatthaupatissau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) sambuddhau, abbhaukāsamhi caṅkami||

2.

 ‘‘migaluddau purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

disvāna taṃ daivadaivaṃ, tiṇamuṭṭhimadāsahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘nisīdanatthaṃ buddhassa, datvā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, pak‍kāmiṃ pak‍kamiṃ (ka॰) uttarāmukhau||

4.

 ‘‘aciraṃ gatamattassa gatamattaṃ maṃ (sī॰ syā॰), migarājā apauthayi ahaiṭhayi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

sīhaina pauthitau pātitau (sī॰ pī॰), ghāṭitau (syā॰) santau, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘āsan‍nai mai kataṃ kammaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhai anāsavai|

sumuttau saravaigauva, daivalaukamagacchahaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘yūpau tattha subhau āsi, puñ‍ñakammābhinimmitau|

sahassakaṇḍau satabhaiṇḍu, dhajālu haritāmayau||

7.

 ‘‘pabhā  niddhāvatai tassa, sataraṃsīva uggatau|

ākiṇṇau daivakañ‍ñāhi, āmaudiṃ kāmakāmihaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘daivalaukā  cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

āgantvāna manussattaṃ, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, nisīdanamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tiṇamuṭṭhai idaṃ phalaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

11.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. mañ‍cadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘vipassinau bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

aikamañ‍caṃ aikaṃ vaic‍caṃ (syā॰), aikapacchaṃ (pī॰) mayā din‍naṃ, pasan‍naina sapāṇinā||

14.

 ‘‘hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, dibbayānaṃ samajjhagaṃ|

taina mañ‍cakadānaina, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ mañ‍camadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, mañ‍cadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

17.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mañ‍cadāyakau vaic‍cakadāyakau (syā॰), saddasañ‍ñikavaggaipi idaṃ§apadānaṃ dissati thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

mañ‍cadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. saraṇagamaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

19.

 ‘‘āruhimha tadā nāvaṃ, bhikkhu cājīvikau cahaṃ|

nāvāya bhij‍jamānāya, bhikkhu mai saraṇaṃ adā||

20.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ sau mai saraṇaṃ adā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saraṇagamanai phalaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

22.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇagamaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

saraṇagamaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. abbhañ‍janadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘nagarai  bandhumatiyā, rājuyyānai vasāmahaṃ|

cammavāsī tadā āsiṃ, kamaṇḍaludharau ahaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘addasaṃ vimalaṃ buddhaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ|

padhānaṃ pahitattaṃ taṃ, jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ vasiṃ isiṃ (syā॰)||

26.

 ‘‘sabbakāmasamiddhiñ‍ca, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ|

disvā pasan‍nau sumanau, abbhañ‍janamadāsahaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, abbhañ‍janassidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

29.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā abbhañ‍janadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

abbhañ‍janadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. supaṭadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘divāvihārā  nikkhantau, vipassī laukanāyakau|

lahuṃ supaṭakaṃ supaṭikaṃ (syā॰), pūpapavaṃ (pī॰) datvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, supaṭakamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, supaṭassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

34.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā supaṭadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

supaṭadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. daṇḍadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘kānanaṃ vanamaugayha, vaiḷuṃ chaitvānahaṃ tadā|

ālambaṇaṃ karitvāna, saṅghassa adadiṃ bahuṃ ahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

37.

 ‘‘taina  cittappasādaina, subbatai abhivādiya|

ālambadaṇḍaṃ datvāna, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

38.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ daṇḍamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, daṇḍadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

40.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā daṇḍadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

daṇḍadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

taivīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

7. girinailapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

42.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, vipinai vicaraṃ ahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ||

43.

 ‘‘tasmiṃ mahākāruṇikai, sabbasattahitai ratai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, nailapupphamapūjayiṃ||

44.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

46.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā girinailapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

girinailapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. baudhisammaj‍jakatthairaapadānaṃ

48.

 ‘‘ahaṃ purai baudhipattaṃ, ujjhitaṃ caitiyaṅgaṇai|

taṃ gahaitvāna chaḍḍaisiṃ, alabhiṃ vīsatīguṇai||

49.

 ‘‘tassa kammassa taijaina, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai|

duvai bhavai saṃsarāmi, daivattai cāpi mānusai||

50.

 ‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, āgantvā mānusaṃ bhavaṃ|

duvai kulai pajāyāmi, khattiyai cāpi brāhmaṇai||

51.

 ‘‘aṅgapac‍caṅgasampan‍nau, ārauhapariṇāhavā|

abhirūpau suci haumi, sampuṇṇaṅgau anūnakau||

52.

 ‘‘daivalaukai  manussai vā, jātau vā yattha katthaci|

bhavai suvaṇṇavaṇṇau ca, uttattakanakūpamau||

53.

 ‘‘mudukā  maddavā sniddhā mudu maddavā siniddhā (syā॰), sukhumā sukumārikā|

chavi mai sabbadā hauti, baudhipattai suchaḍḍitai suchaḍḍinai (sī॰)||

54.

 ‘‘yatau kutauci gatīsu, sarīrai samudāgatai|

na limpati rajaujal‍laṃ, vipākau pattachaḍḍitai||

55.

 ‘‘uṇhai vātātapai tassa, aggitāpaina vā pana|

gattai saidā na muc‍canti, vipākau pattachaḍḍitai||

56.

 ‘‘kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilāsau ca, tilakā piḷakā tathā|

na haunti kāyai daddu ca, vipākau pattachaḍḍitai||

57.

 ‘‘aparampi guṇaṃ tassa, nibbattati bhavābhavai|

raugā na haunti kāyasmiṃ, vipākau pattachaḍḍitai||

58.

 ‘‘aparampi guṇaṃ tassa, nibbattati bhavābhavai|

na hauti cittajā pīḷā, vipākau pattachaḍḍitai||

59.

 ‘‘aparampi guṇaṃ tassa, nibbattati bhavābhavai|

amittā na bhavantassa, vipākau pattachaḍḍitai||

60.

 ‘‘aparampi guṇaṃ tassa, nibbattati bhavābhavai|

anūnabhaugau bhavati, vipākau pattachaḍḍitai||

61.

 ‘‘aparampi guṇaṃ tassa, nibbattati bhavābhavai|

aggirājūhi cauraihi, na hauti udakai bhayaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘aparampi  guṇaṃ tassa, nibbattati bhavābhavai|

dāsidāsā anucarā, haunti cittānuvattakā||

63.

 ‘‘yamhi āyuppamāṇamhi, jāyatai mānusai bhavai|

tatau na hāyatai āyu, tiṭṭhatai yāvatāyukaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘abbhantarā ca bāhirā bahicarā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), naigamā ca saraṭṭhakā|

nuyuttā haunti sabbaipi, vuddhikāmā sukhicchakā||

65.

 ‘‘bhaugavā yasavā haumi, sirimā ñātipakkhavā|

apaitabhayasantāsau, bhavaihaṃ sabbatau bhavai||

66.

 ‘‘daivā  manussā asurā, gandhabbā yakkharakkhasā|

sabbai tai parirakkhanti, bhavai saṃsaratau sadā||

67.

 ‘‘daivalaukai manussai ca, anubhautvā ubhau yasai|

avasānai ca nibbānaṃ, sivaṃ pattau anuttaraṃ||

68.

 ‘‘sambuddhamuddisitvāna, baudhiṃ vā tassa satthunau|

yau puñ‍ñaṃ pasavai pausau, tassa kiṃ nāma dul‍labhaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘maggai  phalai āgamai ca, jhānābhiñ‍ñāguṇaisu ca|

añ‍ñaisaṃ adhikau hutvā, nibbāyāmi anāsavau||

70.

 ‘‘puraihaṃ baudhiyā pattaṃ, chaḍḍaitvā haṭṭhamānasau|

imaihi vīsataṅgaihi, samaṅgī haumi sabbadā||

71.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

72.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā baudhisammaj‍jakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

baudhisammaj‍jakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. āmaṇḍaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

74.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā, caṅkamī laukanāyakau||

75.

 ‘‘khāribhāraṃ gahaitvāna, āharantau phalaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, caṅkamantaṃ mahāmuniṃ||

76.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, āmaṇḍamadadiṃ phalaṃ||

77.
