 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, āmaṇḍassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

78.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

79.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

80.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āmaṇḍaphaladāyakau thairau imā

gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

āmaṇḍaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. sugandhatthairaapadānaṃ

81.

 ‘‘imamhi bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina nāmaina (sabbattha), uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

82.

 ‘‘anubyañ‍janasampan‍nau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau|

byāmappabhāparivutau, raṃsijālasamautthaṭau raṃsijālasamausaṭau (sī॰ pī॰)||

83.

 ‘‘assāsaitā yathā candau, sūriyauva pabhaṅkarau|

nibbāpaitā yathā maighau, sāgarauva guṇākarau||

84.

 ‘‘dharaṇīriva  sīlaina, himavāva samādhinā|

ākāsau viya pañ‍ñāya, asaṅgau anilau yathā||

85.

 ‘‘sa kadāci mahāvīrau, parisāsu visāradau|

sac‍cāni sampakāsaiti, uddharantau mahājanaṃ||

86.

 ‘‘tadā hi bārāṇasiyaṃ, saiṭṭhiputtau mahāyasau|

āsahaṃ dhanadhañ‍ñassa anantadhanadhañ‍ñassa (ka॰), pahūtassa bahū tadā||

87.

 ‘‘jaṅghāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ, migadāyamupaic‍cahaṃ mupaisahaṃ (ka॰)|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, daisaintaṃ amataṃ padaṃ||

88.

 ‘‘visaṭṭhakantavacanaṃ, karavīkasamassaraṃ|

haṃsarutaihi haṃsadundubhi (syā॰ pī॰) nigghausaṃ, viñ‍ñāpaintaṃ mahājanaṃ||

89.

 ‘‘disvā  daivātidaivaṃ taṃ, sutvāva madhuraṃ giraṃ|

pahāyanappakai bhaugai, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

90.

 ‘‘aivaṃ  pabbajitau cāhaṃ, na ciraina bahussutau|

ahausiṃ dhammakathikau, vicittapaṭibhāṇavā||

91.

 ‘‘mahāparisamajjhaihaṃ, haṭṭhacittau punappunaṃ|

vaṇṇayiṃ haimavaṇṇassa, vaṇṇaṃ vaṇṇavisāradau||

92.

 ‘‘aisa khīṇāsavau buddhau, anīghau chin‍nasaṃsayau|

sabbakammakkhayaṃ pattau, vimuttaupadhisaṅkhayai||

93.

 ‘‘aisa sau bhagavā buddhau, aisa sīhau anuttarau|

sadaivakassa laukassa, brahmacak‍kappavattakau||

94.

 ‘‘dantau damaitā santau ca, samaitā nibbutau isi|

nibbāpaitā ca assatthau, assāsaitā mahājanaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘vīrau sūrau ca vik‍kantau dhīrau ca (sī॰ pī॰), pañ‍ñau kāruṇikau vasī|

vijitāvī ca sa jinau, appagabbau anālayau||

96.

 ‘‘anaiñ‍jau acalau dhīmā, amauhau asamau muni|

dhaurayhau usabhau nāgau, sīhau sak‍kau garūsupi||

97.

 ‘‘virāgau vimalau brahmā, vādī sūrau raṇañ‍jahau|

akhilau ca visal‍lau ca, asamau saṃyatau vusabhau (syā॰), payatau (pī॰) suci||

98.

 ‘‘brāhmaṇau  samaṇau nāthau, bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattakau|

yaudhau buddhau sutāsutau sutau sutau (sī॰ pī॰), acalau muditau sitau ditau (sī॰)||

99.

 ‘‘dhātā dhatā ca santi ca, kattā naitā pakāsitā|

sampahaṃsitā bhaittā ca, chaittā sautā pasaṃsitā||

100.

 ‘‘akhilau ca visal‍lau ca, anīghau akathaṃkathī|

anaijau virajau kattā, gandhā vattā pasaṃsitā||

101.

 ‘‘tāraitā atthakāraitā, kāraitā sampadāritā|

pāpaitā sahitā kantā, hantā ātāpī tāpasau hantā, tāpitā ca visausitā (syā॰)||

102.

 ‘‘samacittau  sac‍caṭṭhitau (syā॰) samasamau, asahāyau dayālayau dayāsayau (sī॰)|

acchairasattau acchairamantau (syā॰) akuhau, katāvī isisattamau||

103.

 ‘‘nittiṇṇakaṅkhau nimmānau, appamaiyyau anūpamau|

sabbavākyapathātītau, sac‍ca naiyyantagū sabbanaiyyantikau (syā॰) jinau||

104.

 ‘‘sattasāravarai  sataraṃsīvarai (syā॰) tasmiṃ, pasādau amatāvahau|

tasmā buddhai ca dhammai ca, saṅghai saddhā mahatthikā mahiddhikā (sī॰ ka॰)||

105.

 ‘‘guṇaihi aivamādīhi, tilaukasaraṇuttamaṃ|

vaṇṇaintau parisāmajjhai, akaṃ kathiṃ (syā॰) dhammakathaṃ ahaṃ||

106.

 ‘‘tatau cutāhaṃ tusitai, anubhautvā mahāsukhaṃ|

tatau cutau manussaisu, jātau haumi sugandhikau||

107.

 ‘‘nissāsau mukhagandhau ca, daihagandhau tathaiva mai|

saidagandhau ca satataṃ, sabbagandhauva hauti mai sabbagandhautisaiti mai (sī॰ pī॰)||

108.

 ‘‘mukhagandhau  sadā mayhaṃ, padumuppalacampakau|

parisantau ādisantau (sī॰), atikantau (syā॰), atisantau (pī॰) sadā vāti, sarīrau ca tathaiva mai||

109.

 ‘‘guṇatthavassa sabbantaṃ, phalaṃ tu phalantaṃ (syā॰) paramabbhutaṃ|

aikaggamanasā sabbai, vaṇṇayissaṃ bhāsitassa (syā॰) suṇātha mai||

110.

 ‘‘guṇaṃ  buddhassa vatvāna, hitāya ca na sadisaṃ hitāya janasandhisu (sī॰ pī॰), hitāya naṃ sukhāvahaṃ (syā॰)|

sukhitau sucittau (syā॰) haumi sabbattha, saṅghau vīrasamāyutau saraddhanisamāyutau (sī॰)||

111.

 ‘‘yasassī sukhitau kantau, jutimā piyadassanau|

vattā aparibhūtau ca, niddausau pañ‍ñavā tathā||

112.

 ‘‘khīṇai āyusi pāsusi (syā॰) nibbānaṃ, sulabhaṃ buddhabhattinau|

taisaṃ haituṃ pavakkhāmi, taṃ suṇātha yathātathaṃ||

113.

 ‘‘santaṃ  yasaṃ bhagavatau, vidhinā abhivādayaṃ|

tattha tatthūpapan‍naupi yattha tatthūpapan‍naupi (sī॰ pī॰), yasassī taina haumahaṃ||

114.

 ‘‘dukkhassantakaraṃ buddhaṃ, dhammaṃ santamasaṅkhataṃ|

vaṇṇayaṃ sukhadau āsiṃ, sattānaṃ sukhitau tatau||

115.

 ‘‘guṇaṃ vadantau buddhassa, buddhapītisamāyutau|

sakantiṃ parakantiñ‍ca, janayiṃ taina kantimā||

116.

 ‘‘jinau tai titthikākiṇṇai janaughai titthakākiṇṇai (sī॰ pī॰), jinau yau titthikātiṇṇau (syā॰), abhibhuyya kutitthiyai|

guṇaṃ vadantau jautaisiṃ thaumaisiṃ (syā॰), nāyakaṃ jutimā tatau||

117.

 ‘‘piyakārī janassāpi, sambuddhassa guṇaṃ vadaṃ|

saradauva sasaṅkauhaṃ, taināsiṃ piyadassanau||

118.

 ‘‘yathāsattivasaināhaṃ, sabbavācāhi santhaviṃ|

sugataṃ taina vāgisau, vicittapaṭibhānavā||

119.

 ‘‘yai bālā vimatiṃ pattā, paribhaunti mahāmuniṃ|

niggahiṃ tai saddhammaina, paribhūtau na tainahaṃ paribhūtaina tainahaṃ (syā॰)||

120.

 ‘‘buddhavaṇṇaina sattānaṃ, kilaisai apanaisahaṃ|

nik‍kilaisamanau haumi, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

121.

 ‘‘sautūnaṃ  vuddhimajaniṃ buddhimajaniṃ (sī॰ pī॰), buddhānussatidaisakau|

taināhamāsiṃ taināpi cāsiṃ (syā॰) sappañ‍ñau, nipuṇatthavipassakau||

122.

 ‘‘sabbāsavaparikkhīṇau, tiṇṇasaṃsārasāgarau|

sikhīva anupādānau, pāpuṇissāmi nibbutiṃ||

123.

 ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva kappasmiṃ, yamahaṃ santhaviṃ jinaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhavaṇṇassidaṃ phalaṃ||

124.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

125.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

126.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sugandhau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sugandhatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

tiṇadāyakavaggau taipañ‍ñāsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

tiṇadau  mañ‍cadau caiva, saraṇabbhañ‍janappadau|

supaṭau daṇḍadāyī ca, nailapūjī tathaiva ca||

baudhisammaj‍jakau maṇḍau, sugandhau dasamauti ca|

gāthāsataṃ sataivīsaṃ, gaṇitañ‍caittha sabbasau||


54. kac‍cāyanavaggau

1. mahākac‍cāyanatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, anaijau ajitaṃ jayau|

satasahassai kappānaṃ, itau uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

2.

 ‘‘vīrau kamalapattakkhau, sasaṅkavimalānanau|

kanakācalasaṅkāsau kañ‍canatacasaṅkāsau (syā॰), ravidittisamappabhau||

3.

 ‘‘sattanaittamanauhārī, varalakkhaṇabhūsitau|

sabbavākyapathātītau, manujāmarasak‍katau||

4.

 ‘‘sambuddhau baudhayaṃ sattai, vāgīsau madhurassarau|

karuṇānibandhasantānau, parisāsu visāradau||

5.

 ‘‘daisaiti  madhuraṃ dhammaṃ, catusac‍cūpasaṃhitaṃ|

nimuggai mauhapaṅkamhi, samuddharati pāṇinai||

6.

 ‘‘tadā aikacarau hutvā, tāpasau himavālayau|

nabhasā mānusaṃ laukaṃ, gacchantau jinamaddasaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘upaic‍ca santikaṃ tassa, assausiṃ dhammadaisanaṃ|

vaṇṇayantassa vīrassa, sāvakassa mahāguṇaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘saṅkhittaina mayā vuttaṃ, vitthāraina pakāsayaṃ|

parisaṃ mañ‍ca tausaiti, yathā kac‍cāyanau ayaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘‘nāhaṃ  aivamidhaikac‍caṃ aivaṃvidhaṃ kañ‍ci (sī॰ pī॰), añ‍ñaṃ passāmi sāvakaṃ|

tasmātadaggai tasmaitadaggai (sī॰) aisaggau, aivaṃ dhāraitha bhikkhavau’||

10.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ vimhitau hutvā, sutvā vākyaṃ manauramaṃ|

himavantaṃ gamitvāna, āhitvā āhatvā (sī॰ pī॰) pupphasañ‍cayaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘pūjaitvā laukasaraṇaṃ, taṃ ṭhānamabhipatthayiṃ|

tadā mamāsayaṃ ñatvā, byākāsi sa raṇañ‍jahau||

12.

 ‘‘‘passathaitaṃ  isivaraṃ, niddhantakanakattacaṃ|

uddhaggalaumaṃ pīṇaṃsaṃ, acalaṃ pañ‍jaliṃ ṭhitaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘‘hāsaṃ  supuṇṇanayanaṃ, buddhavaṇṇagatāsayaṃ|

dhammajaṃ uggahadayaṃ dhammaṃva viggahavaraṃ (sī॰), dhammapaṭiggahavaraṃ (pī॰), amatāsittasan‍nibhaṃ’||

14.

 ‘‘kac‍cānassa guṇaṃ sutvā, taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayaṃ ṭhitau|

anāgatamhi addhānai, gautamassa mahāmunai||

15.

 ‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kac‍cānau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

16.

 ‘‘bahussutau mahāñāṇī, adhippāyavidū munai|

pāpuṇissati taṃ ṭhānaṃ, yathāyaṃ byākatau mayā||

17.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘duvai bhavai saṃsarāmi, daivattai atha mānusai|

añ‍ñaṃ gatiṃ na gacchāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘duvai  kulai pajāyāmi, khattiyai atha brāhmaṇai|

nīcai kulai na jāyāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘pacchimai  ca bhavai dāni, jātau uj‍jainiyaṃ purai jātau, uj‍jainiyaṃ purai ramai (syā॰)|

paj‍jautassa ca caṇḍassa, purauhitadijādhinau purauhitadijātinau (sī॰ pī॰)||

21.

 ‘‘puttau tiriṭivacchassa tiriṭavacchassa (sī॰), tipitivacchassa (syā॰), nipuṇau vaidapāragū|

mātā ca candimā nāma, kac‍cānauhaṃ varattacau||

22.

 ‘‘vīmaṃsanatthaṃ buddhassa, bhūmipālaina paisitau|

disvā maukkhapuradvāraṃ, nāyakaṃ guṇasañ‍cayaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘sutvā ca vimalaṃ vākyaṃ, gatipaṅkavisausanaṃ|

pāpuṇiṃ amataṃ santaṃ, saisaihi saha sattahi||

24.

 ‘‘adhippāyavidū jātau, sugatassa mahāmatai|

ṭhapitau aitadaggai ca, susamiddhamanaurathau||

25.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

26.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai| tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

mahākac‍cāyanatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. vak‍kalitthairaapadānaṃ

28.

 ‘‘itau  satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau|

anaumanāmau amitau, nāmaina padumuttarau||

29.

 ‘‘padumākāravadanau, padumāmalasucchavī|

laukainānupalittauva tauyaina padumaṃ yathā||

30.

 ‘‘vīrau padumapattakkhau, kantau ca padumaṃ yathā|

padumuttaragandhauva, tasmā sau padumuttarau||

31.

 ‘‘laukajaiṭṭhau ca nimmānau, andhānaṃ nayanūpamau|

santavaisau guṇanidhi, karuṇāmatisāgarau||

32.

 ‘‘sa kadāci mahāvīrau, brahmāsurasurac‍citau|

sadaivamanujākiṇṇai, janamajjhai jinuttamau januttamau (syā॰ pī॰), anuttamau (ka॰) vaṅgīsatthairāpadānaipi||

33.

 ‘‘vadanaina sugandhaina, madhuraina rutaina ca|

rañ‍jayaṃ parisaṃ sabbaṃ, santhavī sāvakaṃ sakaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘saddhādhimuttau  sumati, mama dassanalālasau dassanasālayau (syā॰)|

natthi aitādisau añ‍ñau, yathāyaṃ bhikkhu vak‍kali||

35.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, nagarai brāhmaṇatrajau|

hutvā sutvā ca taṃ vākyaṃ, taṃ ṭhānamabhiraucayiṃ||

36.

 ‘‘sasāvakaṃ  taṃ vimalaṃ, nimantaitvā tathāgataṃ|

sattāhaṃ bhaujayitvāna, dussaihacchādayiṃ tadā||

37.

 ‘‘nipac‍ca  sirasā tassa, anantaguṇasāgarai|

nimuggau pītisampuṇṇau, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

38.

 ‘‘‘yau sau tayā santhavitau, itau sattamakai muni idha saddhādhimuttau isi (syā॰), itau sattamakaihani (sī॰ pī॰)|

bhikkhu saddhāvataṃ aggau, tādisau haumahaṃ munai’||

39.

 ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai mahāvīrau, anāvaraṇadassanau|

imaṃ vākyaṃ udīraisi, parisāya mahāmuni||

40.

 ‘‘‘passathaitaṃ māṇavakaṃ, pītamaṭṭhanivāsanaṃ|

haimayañ‍ñaupacitaṅgaṃ haimayañ‍ñaupavītaṅgaṃ (sī॰), jananaittamanauharaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘‘aisau anāgataddhānai, gautamassa mahaisinau|

aggau saddhādhimuttānaṃ, sāvakauyaṃ bhavissati||

42.

 ‘‘‘daivabhūtau manussau vā, sabbasantāpavaj‍jitau|

sabbabhaugaparibyūḷhau, sukhitau saṃsarissati||

43.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

44.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

vak‍kali nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

45.

 ‘‘taina kammavisaisaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘sabbattha sukhitau hutvā, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai|

sāvatthiyaṃ purai jātau, kulai añ‍ñatarai ahaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘naunītasukhumālaṃ  maṃ, jātapal‍lavakaumalaṃ|

mandaṃ uttānasayanaṃ, pisācabhayataj‍jitā||

48.

 ‘‘pādamūlai mahaisissa, sāyaisuṃ dīnamānasā|

imaṃ dadāma tai nātha, saraṇaṃ hauhi nāyaka||

49.

 ‘‘tadā  paṭiggahi sau maṃ, bhītānaṃ saraṇau muni|

jālinā cak‍kaṅkitaina saṅkalaṅkaina (sī॰), mudukaumalapāṇinā||

50.

 ‘‘tadā pabhuti taināhaṃ, arakkhaiyyaina rakkhitau|

sabbavairavinimuttau sabbabyādhivinimuttau (syā॰), sabbūpadhivinimuttau (pī॰), sukhaina parivuddhitau||

51.

 ‘‘sugataina  vinā bhūtau, uk‍kaṇṭhāmi muhuttakaṃ|

jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘sabbapāramisambhūtaṃ, nīlakkhinayanaṃ laṅkinīlayanaṃ (sī॰) varaṃ|

rūpaṃ sabbasubhākiṇṇaṃ, atittau viharāmahaṃ vihayāmahaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)||

53.

 ‘‘buddharūparatiṃ buddhau rūparatiṃ (sī॰) ñatvā, tadā auvadi maṃ jinau|

‘alaṃ vak‍kali kiṃ rūpai, ramasai bālananditai||

54.

 ‘‘‘yau hi passati saddhammaṃ, sau maṃ passati paṇḍitau|

apassamānau saddhammaṃ, maṃ passampi na passati||

55.

 ‘‘‘anantādīnavau kāyau, visarukkhasamūpamau|

āvāsau sabbaraugānaṃ, puñ‍jau dukkhassa kaivalau||

56.

 ‘‘‘nibbindiya tatau rūpai, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ|

passa upak‍kilaisānaṃ, sukhainantaṃ gamissati’||

57.

 ‘‘aivaṃ  tainānusiṭṭhauhaṃ, nāyakaina hitaisinā|

gijjhakūṭaṃ samāruyha, jhāyāmi girikandarai||

58.

 ‘‘ṭhitau pabbatapādamhi, assāsayi mamāhasau (sī॰) mahāmuni|

vak‍kalīti jinau vācaṃ, taṃ sutvā muditau ahaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘pakkhandiṃ sailapabbhārai, anaikasatapaurisai|

tadā buddhānubhāvaina, sukhainaiva mahiṃ gatau||

60.

 ‘‘punaupi punāpi (syā॰), muni maṃ (ka॰) dhammaṃ daisaiti, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ|

tamahaṃ dhammamañ‍ñāya, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

61.

 ‘‘sumahāparisamajjhai, tadā maṃ caraṇantagau|

aggaṃ saddhādhimuttānaṃ, pañ‍ñapaisi mahāmati||

62.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

64.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vak‍kalitthairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

vak‍kalitthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. mahākappinatthairaapadānaṃ

66.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

uditau ajaṭākāsai jagadākāsai (sī॰), jaladākāsai (pī॰), ravīva saradambarai||

67.

 ‘‘vacanābhāya baudhaiti, vainaiyyapadumāni sau|

kilaisapaṅkaṃ sausaiti, matiraṃsīhi nāyakau||

68.

 ‘‘titthiyānaṃ yasai yasau (sī॰ pī॰) hanti, khaj‍jautābhā yathā ravi|

sac‍catthābhaṃ pakāsaiti, ratanaṃva divākarau||

69.

 ‘‘guṇānaṃ āyatibhūtau, ratanānaṃva sāgarau|

paj‍jun‍nauriva bhūtāni, dhammamaighaina vassati||

70.

 ‘‘akkhadassau tadā āsiṃ, nagarai haṃsasavhayai|

upaic‍ca dhammamassausiṃ, jalajuttamanāminau||

71.

 ‘‘auvādakassa bhikkhūnaṃ, sāvakassa katāvinau|

guṇaṃ pakāsayantassa, tappayantassa tausayantassa (sī॰), hāsayantassa (syā॰), vāsayantassa (pī॰) mai manaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘sutvā patītau sumanau, nimantaitvā tathāgataṃ|

sasissaṃ bhaujayitvāna, taṃ ṭhānamabhipatthayiṃ||

73.

 ‘‘tadā  haṃsasamabhāgau, haṃsadundubhinissanau haṃsadundubhisussarau (sī॰)|

passathaitaṃ mahāmattaṃ, vinicchayavisāradaṃ||

74.

 ‘‘patitaṃ pādamūlai mai, samuggatatanūruhaṃ|

jīmūtavaṇṇaṃ pīṇaṃsaṃ, pasan‍nanayanānanaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘parivāraina  mahatā, rājayuttaṃ mahāyasaṃ|

aisau katāvinau ṭhānaṃ, patthaiti muditāsayau||

76.

 ‘‘‘iminā  paṇipātaina, cāgaina paṇidhīhi ca piṇḍapātaina, caitanā paṇidhīhi ca (sī॰)|

kappasatasahassāni, nupapaj‍jati duggatiṃ||

77.

 ‘‘‘daivaisu daivasaubhaggaṃ, manussaisu mahantataṃ|

anubhautvāna saisaina abhutvāva saisaina (sī॰), anubhautvāva saisaina (syā॰), nibbānaṃ pāpuṇissati||

78.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

79.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kappinau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

80.

 ‘‘tatauhaṃ sukataṃ kāraṃ, katvāna jinasāsanai|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tusitaṃ agamāsahaṃ||

81.

 ‘‘daivamānusaraj‍jāni, satasau anusāsiya|

bārāṇasiyamāsan‍nai, jātau kainiyajātiyaṃ||

82.

 ‘‘sahassaparivāraina satasahassaparivārau (syā॰), sapajāpatikau ahaṃ|

pañ‍ca pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ, satāni samupaṭṭhahiṃ||

83.

 ‘‘taimāsaṃ bhaujayitvāna, pacchādamha ticīvaraṃ|

tatau cutā mayaṃ sabbai, ahumha tidasūpagā||

84.

 ‘‘punau sabbai manussattaṃ, agamimha tatau cutā|

kuk‍kuṭamhi purai jātā, himavantassa passatau||

85.

 ‘‘kappinau  nāmahaṃ āsiṃ, rājaputtau mahāyasau|

saisāmac‍cakulai jātā, mamaiva parivārayuṃ||

86.

 ‘‘mahāraj‍jasukhaṃ pattau, sabbakāmasamiddhimā|

vāṇijaihi samakkhātaṃ, buddhuppādamahaṃ suṇiṃ||

87.

 ‘‘‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, asamau aikapuggalau|

sau pakāsaiti saddhammaṃ, amataṃ sukhamuttamaṃ||

88.

 ‘‘‘suyuttā tassa sissā ca, sumuttā ca anāsavā’|

‘‘sutvā naisaṃ suvacanaṃ, sak‍karitvāna vāṇijai||

89.

 ‘‘pahāya raj‍jaṃ sāmac‍cau, nikkhamiṃ buddhamāmakau|

nadiṃ disvā mahācandaṃ, pūritaṃ samatittikaṃ||

90.

 ‘‘appatiṭṭhaṃ  anālambaṃ, duttaraṃ sīghavāhiniṃ|

guṇaṃ saritvā buddhassa, sautthinā samatik‍kamiṃ||

91.

 ‘‘‘bhavasautaṃ sacai buddhau, tiṇṇau laukantagū vidū vibhū (ka॰)|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, gamanaṃ mai samijjhatu||

92.

 ‘‘‘yadi  santigamau maggau, maukkhau cac‍cantikaṃ maukkhadaṃ santikaṃ (syā॰) sukhaṃ|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, gamanaṃ mai samijjhatu||

93.

 ‘‘‘saṅghau cai tiṇṇakantārau, puñ‍ñakkhaittau anuttarau|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, gamanaṃ mai samijjhatu’||

94.

 ‘‘saha katai sac‍cavarai, maggā apagataṃ jalaṃ|

tatau sukhaina uttiṇṇau, nadītīrai manauramai||

95.

 ‘‘nisin‍naṃ  addasaṃ buddhaṃ, udaintaṃva pabhaṅkaraṃ|

jalantaṃ haimasailaṃva, dīparukkhaṃva jautitaṃ||

96.

 ‘‘sasiṃva tārāsahitaṃ, sāvakaihi purakkhataṃ|

vāsavaṃ viya vassantaṃ, daisanājaladantaraṃ daivaina jalanandanaṃ (syā॰ pī॰)||

97.

 ‘‘vanditvāna sahāmac‍cau, aikamantamupāvisiṃ|

tatau nau āsayaṃ tatau ajjhāsayaṃ (syā॰) ñatvā, buddhau dhammamadaisayi||

98.

 ‘‘sutvāna  dhammaṃ vimalaṃ, avaucumha mayaṃ jinaṃ|

‘pabbājaihi mahāvīra, nibbindāmha nibbin‍nāmha (sī॰ pī॰), autiṇṇamha (syā॰) mayaṃ bhavai’||

99.

 ‘‘‘svakkhātau bhikkhavai dhammau, dukkhantakaraṇāya vau|

caratha brahmacariyaṃ’, ic‍cāha munisattamau||

100.

 ‘‘saha vācāya sabbaipi, bhikkhuvaisadharā mayaṃ|

ahumha upasampan‍nā, sautāpan‍nā ca sāsanai||

101.

 ‘‘tatau jaitavanaṃ gantvā, anusāsi vināyakau|

anusiṭṭhau jinaināhaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

102.

 ‘‘tatau bhikkhusahassāni bhikkhusahassaṃ taṃ (sī॰ pī॰), anusāsimahaṃ tadā|

mamānusāsanakarā, taipi āsuṃ anāsavā||

103.

 ‘‘jinau tasmiṃ guṇai tuṭṭhau, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ|

bhikkhuauvādakānaggau, kappinauti mahājanai||

104.

 ‘‘satasahassai  kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

pamuttau saravaigauva, kilaisai jhāpayiṃ jhāpayī (sī॰) mama||

105.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

106.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

107.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākappinau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

mahākappinatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. dabbamal‍laputtatthairaapadānaṃ

108.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbalaukavidū muni|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji cakkhumā||

109.

 ‘‘auvādakau viñ‍ñāpakau, tārakau sabbapāṇinaṃ|

daisanākusalau buddhau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

110.

 ‘‘anukampakau  kāruṇikau, hitaisī sabbapāṇinaṃ|

sampattai titthiyai sabbai, pañ‍casīlai patiṭṭhapi patiṭṭhahi (syā॰ ka॰)||

111.

 ‘‘aivaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi, suñ‍ñataṃ suñ‍ñakaṃ (sī॰) aivamuparipi titthiyaihi ca|

vicittaṃ arahantaihi, vasībhūtaihi tādibhi||

112.

 ‘‘ratanānaṭṭhapañ‍ñāsaṃ, uggatau sau mahāmuni|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau||

113.

 ‘‘vassasatasahassāni, āyu vij‍jati tāvadai|

tāvatā tiṭṭhamānau sau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

114.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, saiṭṭhiputtau mahāyasau|

upaitvā laukapaj‍jautaṃ, assausiṃ dhammadaisanaṃ||

115.

 ‘‘saināsanāni bhikkhūnaṃ, pañ‍ñāpaintaṃ sasāvakaṃ|

kittayantassa vacanaṃ, suṇitvā muditau ahaṃ||

116.

 ‘‘adhikāraṃ  sasaṅghassa, katvā tassa mahaisinau|

nipac‍ca sirasā pādai, taṃ ṭhānamabhipatthayiṃ||

117.

 ‘‘tadāha sa mahāvīrau, mama kammaṃ pakittayaṃ|

‘yau sasaṅghamabhaujaisi, sattāhaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

118.

 ‘‘‘sauyaṃ kamalapattakkhau, sīhaṃsau kanakattacau|

mama pādamūlai nipati patitau (pī॰), patthayaṃ ṭhānamuttamaṃ||

119.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

120.

 ‘‘‘sāvakau tassa buddhassa, dabbau nāmaina vissutau|

saināsanapañ‍ñāpakau, aggau haissatiyaṃ tadā’||

121.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

122.

 ‘‘satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

123.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ  vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

sabbattha sukhitau āsiṃ, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

124.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, vipassī nāma nāyakau|

uppaj‍ji cārudassanau cārunayanau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), sabbadhammavipassakau||

125.

 ‘‘duṭṭhacittau upavadiṃ, sāvakaṃ tassa tādinau|

sabbāsavaparikkhīṇaṃ, suddhauti ca vijāniya||

126.

 ‘‘tassaiva naravīrassa, sāvakānaṃ mahaisinaṃ|

salākañ‍ca gahaitvāna salākaṃ paggahaitvāna (sī॰ pī॰), khīraudanamadāsahaṃ||

127.

 ‘‘imamhi bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

128.

 ‘‘sāsanaṃ jautayitvāna, abhibhuyya kutitthiyai|

vinaiyyai vinayitvāva, nibbutau sau sasāvakau||

129.

 ‘‘sasissai nibbutai nāthai, atthamaintamhi sāsanai|

daivā kandiṃsu saṃviggā, muttakaisā rudammukhā||

130.

 ‘‘nibbāyissati  dhammakkhau, na passissāma subbatai|

na suṇissāma saddhammaṃ, ahau nau appapuñ‍ñatā||

131.

 ‘‘tadāyaṃ pathavī sabbā, acalā sā calācalā calācalī (sī॰), pulāpulī (syā॰)|

sāgarau ca sasaukauva, vinadī karuṇaṃ giraṃ||

132.

 ‘‘catuddisā dundubhiyau, nādayiṃsu amānusā|

samantatau asaniyau, phaliṃsu ca bhayāvahā||

133.

 ‘‘uk‍kā  patiṃsu nabhasā, dhūmakaitu ca dissati|

sadhūmā jālavaṭṭā ca sabbathalajasattā ca (sī॰), raviṃsu karuṇaṃ migā||

134.

 ‘‘uppādai dāruṇai disvā, sāsanatthaṅgasūcakai|

saṃviggā bhikkhavau satta, cintayimha mayaṃ tadā||

135.

 ‘‘sāsanaina vināmhākaṃ, jīvitaina alaṃ mayaṃ|

pavisitvā mahārañ‍ñaṃ, yuñ‍jāma jinasāsanaṃ||

136.

 ‘‘addasamha  tadārañ‍ñai, ubbiddhaṃ sailamuttamaṃ|

nissaiṇiyā tamāruyha, nissaiṇiṃ pātayimhasai||

137.

 ‘‘tadā  auvadi nau thairau, buddhuppādau sudul‍labhau|

saddhātidul‍labhā laddhā, thaukaṃ saisañ‍ca sāsanaṃ||

138.

 ‘‘nipatanti khaṇātītā, anantai dukkhasāgarai|

tasmā payaugau kattabbau, yāva ṭhāti munai mataṃ yāva tiṭṭhati sāsanaṃ (syā॰)||

139.

 ‘‘arahā āsi sau thairau, anāgāmī tadānugau|

susīlā itarai yuttā, daivalaukaṃ agamhasai||

140.

 ‘‘nibbutau tiṇṇasaṃsārau, suddhāvāsai ca aikakau|

ahañ‍ca pak‍kusāti ca, sabhiyau bāhiyau tathā||

141.

 ‘‘kumārakassapau caiva, tattha tatthūpagā mayaṃ|

saṃsārabandhanā muttā, gautamainānukampitā||

142.

 ‘‘mal‍laisu kusinārāyaṃ, jātau gabbhaiva mai satau|

mātā matā citāruḷhā, tatau nippatitau ahaṃ||

143.

 ‘‘patitau  dabbapuñ‍jamhi, tatau dabbauti vissutau|

brahmacārībalaināhaṃ, vimuttau sattavassikau||

144.

 ‘‘khīraudanabalaināhaṃ, pañ‍cahaṅgaihupāgatau|

khīṇāsavaupavādaina, pāpaihi bahucauditau||

145.

 ‘‘ubhau puñ‍ñañ‍ca pāpañ‍ca, vītivattaumhi dānihaṃ|

patvāna paramaṃ santiṃ, viharāmi anāsavau||

146.

 ‘‘saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpayiṃ, hāsayitvāna subbatai|

jinau tasmiṃ guṇai tuṭṭhau, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

147.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

148.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

149.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dabbamal‍laputtau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

dabbamal‍laputtatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. kumārakassapatthairaapadānaṃ

150.

 ‘‘itau  satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau|

sabbalaukahitau vīrau, padumuttaranāmakau||

151.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ  brāhmaṇau hutvā, vissutau vaidapāragū|

divāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

152.

 ‘‘catusac‍caṃ  pakāsaintaṃ, baudhayantaṃ sadaivakaṃ|

vicittakathikānaggaṃ, vaṇṇayantaṃ mahājanai||

153.

 ‘‘tadā muditacittauhaṃ, nimantaitvā tathāgataṃ|

nānārattaihi vatthaihi, alaṅkaritvāna maṇḍapaṃ||

154.

 ‘‘nānāratanapaj‍jautaṃ, sasaṅghaṃ bhaujayiṃ tahiṃ|

bhaujayitvāna sattāhaṃ, nānaggarasabhaujanaṃ||

155.

 ‘‘nānācittaihi nānāvaṇṇaihi (sī॰) pupphaihi, pūjayitvā sasāvakaṃ mahāvīraṃ (ka॰)|

nipac‍ca pādamūlamhi, taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ ahaṃ||

156.

 ‘‘tadā  munivarau āha, karuṇaikarasāsayau karuṇau karuṇālayau (syā॰)|

‘passathaitaṃ dijavaraṃ, padumānanalaucanaṃ||

157.

 ‘‘‘pītipāmauj‍jabahulaṃ, samuggatatanūruhaṃ|

hāsamhitavisālakkhaṃ, mama sāsanalālasaṃ||

158.

 ‘‘‘patitaṃ pādamūlai mai, aikāvatthasumānasaṃ aikavatthaṃ sumānasaṃ (syā॰ ka॰)|

aisa patthaiti taṃ ṭhānaṃ, vicittakathikattanaṃ vicittakathikattadaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)||

159.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

160.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kumārakassapau nāma, haissati satthu sāvakau||

161.

 ‘‘‘vicittapupphadussānaṃ, ratanānañ‍ca vāhasā|

vicittakathikānaṃ sau, aggataṃ pāpuṇissati’||

162.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

163.

 ‘‘paribbhamaṃ bhavābhavai bhavākāsai (sī॰ pī॰), raṅgamajjhai yathā naṭau|

sākhamigatrajau hutvā, migiyā kucchimauk‍kamiṃ||

164.

 ‘‘tadā mayi kucchigatai, vajjhavārau upaṭṭhitau|

sākhaina cattā mai mātā, nigraudhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā||

165.

 ‘‘taina sā migarājaina, maraṇā parimaucitā|

paric‍cajitvā sapāṇaṃ saṃpāṇaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), mamaivaṃ auvadī tadā||

166.

 ‘‘‘nigraudhamaiva saivaiyya, na sākhamupasaṃvasai|

nigraudhasmiṃ mataṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai sākhamhi jīvitaṃ’||

167.

 ‘‘tainānusiṭṭhā migayūthapaina, ahañ‍ca mātā ca tathaitarai ca citarai ca (syā॰), tassauvādaina (pī॰), citarai ca tassauvādaṃ (ka॰)|

āgamma  rammaṃ tusitādhivāsaṃ, gatā pavāsaṃ sagharaṃ yathaiva||

168.

 ‘‘punau kassapavīrassa, atthamaintamhi sāsanai|

āruyha sailasikharaṃ, yuñ‍jitvā jinasāsanaṃ||

169.

 ‘‘idānāhaṃ rājagahai, jātau saiṭṭhikulai ahuṃ|

āpan‍nasattā mai mātā, pabbaji anagāriyaṃ||

170.

 ‘‘sagabbhaṃ  taṃ viditvāna, daivadattamupānayuṃ|

sau avauca ‘vināsaitha, pāpikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ imaṃ’||

171.

 ‘‘idānipi munindaina, jinaina anukampitā|

sukhinī ajanī mayhaṃ, mātā bhikkhunupassayai||

172.

 ‘‘taṃ viditvā mahīpālau, kausalau maṃ apausayi|

kumāraparihāraina, nāmaināhañ‍ca kassapau||

173.

 ‘‘mahākassapamāgamma, ahaṃ kumārakassapau|

vammikasadisaṃ kāyaṃ, sutvā buddhaina daisitaṃ||

174.

 ‘‘tatau  cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai, anupādāya sabbasau|

pāyāsiṃ damayitvāhaṃ, aitadaggamapāpuṇiṃ||

175.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

176.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

177.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kumārakassapau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kumārakassapatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

catuvīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

6. bāhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

178.

 ‘‘itau  satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau|

mahappabhau tilaukaggau, nāmaina padumuttarau||

179.

 ‘‘khippābhiñ‍ñassa bhikkhussa, guṇaṃ kittayatau munai|

sutvā udaggacittauhaṃ, kāraṃ katvā mahaisinau||

180.

 ‘‘datvā sattāhikaṃ dānaṃ, sasissassa munai ahaṃ|

abhivādiya sambuddhaṃ, taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ tadā||

181.

 ‘‘tatau maṃ byākari buddhau, ‘aitaṃ passatha brāhmaṇaṃ|

patitaṃ pādamūlai mai, cariyaṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pasan‍nanayanānanaṃ (sī॰), pīnasampan‍navaikkhaṇaṃ (syā॰), pīṇaṃsaṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ (pī॰)||

182.

 ‘‘‘haimayañ‍ñaupacitaṅgaṃ, avadātatanuttacaṃ|

palambabimbatambauṭṭhaṃ, saitatiṇhasamaṃ dijaṃ||

183.

 ‘‘‘guṇathāmabahutaraṃ, samuggatatanūruhaṃ|

guṇaughāyatanībhūtaṃ, pītisamphul‍litānanaṃ||

184.

 ‘‘‘aisau patthayatai ṭhānaṃ, khippābhiñ‍ñassa bhikkhunau|

anāgatai mahāvīrau, gautamau nāma haissati||

185.

 ‘‘‘tassa  dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

bāhiyau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

186.

 ‘‘tadā hi tuṭṭhau vuṭṭhāya, yāvajīvaṃ mahāmunai|

kāraṃ katvā cutau saggaṃ, agaṃ sabhavanaṃ yathā||

187.

 ‘‘daivabhūtau  manussau vā, sukhitau tassa kammunau|

vāhasā saṃsaritvāna, sampattimanubhaumahaṃ||

188.

 ‘‘puna kassapavīrassa, atthamaintamhi atthaṅgatamhi (syā॰) sāsanai|

āruyha sailasikharaṃ, yuñ‍jitvā jinasāsanaṃ||

189.

 ‘‘visuddhasīlau sappañ‍ñau, jinasāsanakārakau|

tatau cutā pañ‍ca janā, daivalaukaṃ agamhasai||

190.

 ‘‘tatauhaṃ bāhiyau jātau, bhārukacchai puruttamai|

tatau nāvāya pakkhandau pakkhantau (sī॰), pak‍kantau (pī॰), sāgaraṃ appasiddhiyaṃ atthasiddhiyaṃ (ka॰)||

191.

 ‘‘tatau nāvā abhij‍jittha, gantvāna katipāhakaṃ|

tadā bhīsanakai ghaurai, patitau makarākarai||

192.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ vāyamitvāna, santaritvā mahaudadhiṃ|

suppādapaṭṭanavaraṃ suppārapaṭṭanavaraṃ (sī॰ pī॰), sampattau mandavaidhitau mandamaidhikau (sī॰), mandavaiditau (syā॰), maddavairataṃ (ka॰)||

193.

 ‘‘dārucīraṃ nivāsaitvā, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ|

tadāha sau janau tuṭṭhau, arahāyamidhāgatau||

194.

 ‘‘imaṃ an‍naina pānaina, vatthaina sayanaina ca|

bhaisaj‍jaina ca sak‍katvā, haissāma sukhitā mayaṃ||

195.

 ‘‘pac‍cayānaṃ tadā lābhī, taihi sak‍katapūjitau|

arahāhanti saṅkappaṃ, uppādaisiṃ ayaunisau||

196.

 ‘‘tatau  mai cittamañ‍ñāya, caudayī pubbadaivatā|

‘na tvaṃ upāyamaggañ‍ñū, kutau tvaṃ arahā bhavai’||

197.

 ‘‘cauditau  tāya saṃviggau, tadāhaṃ paripucchi taṃ|

‘kai vā aitai kuhiṃ laukai, arahantau naruttamā||

198.

 ‘‘‘sāvatthiyaṃ  kausalamandirai jinau, pahūtapañ‍ñau varabhūrimaidhasau|

sau sakyaputtau arahā anāsavau, daisaiti dhammaṃ arahattapattiyā||

199.

 ‘‘‘tadassa sutvā vacanaṃ supīṇitau pīṇitvā (ka॰), nidhiṃva laddhā kapaṇauti vimhitau|

udaggacittau arahattamuttamaṃ, sudassanaṃ daṭṭhumanantagaucaraṃ||

200.

 ‘‘‘tadā tatau nikkhamitvāna satthunau nikkhamituna satthuvaraṃ (sī॰), sadā jinaṃ passāmi vimalānanaṃ parājinaṃ passāmi kamalānanaṃ (ka॰)|

upaic‍ca rammaṃ vijitavhayaṃ vanaṃ, dijai apucchiṃ kuhiṃ laukanandanau||

201.

 ‘‘‘tatau avaucuṃ naradaivavanditau, puraṃ paviṭṭhau asanaisanāya sau|

sasauva pac‍caihi (sī॰ syā॰) khippaṃ munidassanussukau, upaic‍ca vandāhi tamaggapuggalaṃ’||

202.

 ‘‘tatauhaṃ tuvaṭaṃ gantvā, sāvatthiṃ puramuttamaṃ|

vicarantaṃ tamaddakkhiṃ, piṇḍatthaṃ apihāgidhaṃ||

203.

 ‘‘pattapāṇiṃ  alaulakkhaṃ, pācayantaṃ pītākaraṃ bhājayantaṃ viyāmataṃ (sī॰), jautayantaṃ idhāmataṃ (syā॰), bhājayantaṃ idaṃmataṃ (pī॰)|

sirīnilayasaṅkāsaṃ, ravidittiharānanaṃ||

204.

 ‘‘taṃ samaic‍ca nipac‍cāhaṃ, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ|

‘kupathai vippanaṭṭhassa, saraṇaṃ hauhi gautama||

205.

 ‘‘‘pāṇasantāraṇatthāya, piṇḍāya vicarāmahaṃ|

na tai dhammakathākālau, ic‍cāha munisattamau’||

206.

 ‘‘tadā punappunaṃ buddhaṃ, āyāciṃ dhammalālasau|

yau mai dhammamadaisaisi, gambhīraṃ suñ‍ñataṃ padaṃ||

207.

 ‘‘tassa  dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, pāpuṇiṃ āsavakkhayaṃ|

parikkhīṇāyukau santau, ahau satthānukampakau||

208.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

209.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

210.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

211.

 ‘‘aivaṃ thairau viyākāsi, bāhiyau dārucīriyau|

saṅkārakūṭai patitau, bhūtāviṭṭhāya gāviyā||

212.

 ‘‘attanau  pubbacariyaṃ, kittayitvā mahāmati|

parinibbāyi sau thairau vīrau (sī॰), dhīrau (syā॰), sāvatthiyaṃ puruttamai||

213.

 ‘‘nagarā  nikkhamantau taṃ, disvāna isisattamau|

dārucīradharaṃ dhīraṃ, bāhiyaṃ bāhitāgamaṃ||

214.

 ‘‘bhūmiyaṃ patitaṃ dantaṃ, indakaitūva pātitaṃ|

gatāyuṃ sukkhakilaisaṃ gatāyu saṃgatak‍laisaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), tadāyu saṅkatālaisaṃ (ka॰), jinasāsanakārakaṃ||

215.

 ‘‘tatau āmantayī satthā, sāvakai sāsanai ratai|

‘gaṇhatha naitvā hutvā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) jhāpaitha, tanuṃ sabrahmacārinau||

216.

 ‘‘‘thūpaṃ karautha pūjaitha, nibbutau sau mahāmati|

khippābhiñ‍ñānamaisaggau, sāvakau mai vacaukarau||

217.

 ‘‘‘sahassamapi cai gāthā, anatthapadasañhitā|

aikaṃ gāthāpadaṃ saiyyau, yaṃ sutvā upasammati||

218.

 ‘‘‘yattha āpau ca pathavī, taijau vāyau na gādhati|

na tattha suk‍kā jautanti, ādic‍cau na pakāsati||

219.

 ‘‘‘na tattha candimā bhāti, tamau tattha na vij‍jati|

yadā ca attanā vaidi, munimaunaina brāhmaṇau||

220.

 ‘‘‘atha  rūpā arūpā ca, sukhadukkhā vimuc‍cati’|

ic‍caivaṃ abhaṇī nāthau, tilaukasaraṇau muni’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bāhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

bāhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahākauṭṭhikatthairaapadānaṃ

221.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbalaukavidū muni|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji cakkhumā||

222.

 ‘‘auvādakau viñ‍ñāpakau, tārakau sabbapāṇinaṃ|

daisanākusalau buddhau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

223.

 ‘‘anukampakau  kāruṇikau, hitaisī sabbapāṇinaṃ|

sampattai titthiyai sabbai, pañ‍casīlai patiṭṭhapi||

224.

 ‘‘aivaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi, suñ‍ñataṃ titthiyaihi ca|

vicittaṃ arahantaihi, vasībhūtaihi tādibhi||

225.

 ‘‘ratanānaṭṭhapañ‍ñāsaṃ, uggatau sau mahāmuni|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau||

226.

 ‘‘vassasatasahassāni, āyu vij‍jati tāvadai|

tāvatā tiṭṭhamānau sau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

227.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, brāhmaṇau vaidapāragū|

upaic‍ca sabbalaukaggaṃ sabbasāraggaṃ (sī॰), sattapāragaṃ (pī॰), assausiṃ dhammadaisanaṃ||

228.

 ‘‘tadā sau sāvakaṃ vīrau, pabhin‍namatigaucaraṃ|

atthai dhammai niruttai ca, paṭibhānai ca kauvidaṃ||

229.

 ‘‘ṭhapaisi aitadaggamhi, taṃ sutvā muditau ahaṃ|

sasāvakaṃ jinavaraṃ, sattāhaṃ bhaujayiṃ tadā||

230.

 ‘‘dussaihacchādayitvāna, sasissaṃ buddhisāgaraṃ buddhasāgaraṃ (ka॰)|

nipac‍ca pādamūlamhi, taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ ahaṃ||

231.

 ‘‘tatau  avauca laukaggau, ‘passathaitaṃ dijuttamaṃ|

vinataṃ pādamūlai mai, kamalaudarasappabhaṃ||

232.

 ‘‘‘buddhasaiṭṭhassa saiṭṭhaṃ buddhassa (syā॰ ka॰) bhikkhussa, ṭhānaṃ patthayatai ayaṃ|

tāya saddhāya cāgaina, saddhammassavanaina taina dhammassavaina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) ca||

233.

 ‘‘‘sabbattha sukhitau hutvā, saṃsaritvā bhavābhavai|

anāgatamhi addhānai, lacchasai taṃ manaurathaṃ||

234.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

235.

 ‘‘‘tassa  dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kauṭṭhikau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

236.

 ‘‘taṃ sutvā muditau hutvā, yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ|

maittacittau paricariṃ, satau pañ‍ñāsamāhitau||

237.

 ‘‘taina  kammavipākaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

238.

 ‘‘satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

239.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

sabbattha sukhitau āsiṃ, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

240.

 ‘‘duvai  bhavai saṃsarāmi, daivattai atha mānusai|

añ‍ñaṃ gatiṃ na gacchāmi, suciṇṇassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

241.

 ‘‘duvai kulai pajāyāmi, khattiyai atha brāhmaṇai|

‘‘nīcai kulai na jāyāmi, suciṇṇassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

242.

 ‘‘pacchimai bhavai sampattai, brahmabandhu ahausahaṃ|

sāvatthiyaṃ vippakulai, pac‍cājātau mahaddhanai||

243.

 ‘‘mātā candavatī nāma, pitā mai assalāyanau|

yadā mai pitaraṃ buddhau, vinayī sabbasuddhiyā||

244.

 ‘‘tadā  pasan‍nau sugatai, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

mauggal‍lānau ācariyau, upajjhā sārisambhavau||

245.

 ‘‘kaisaisu chij‍jamānaisu, diṭṭhi chin‍nā samūlikā|

nivāsaintau ca kāsāvaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

246.

 ‘‘atthadhammaniruttīsu, paṭibhānai ca mai mati|

pabhin‍nā taina laukaggau, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

247.

 ‘‘asandiṭṭhaṃ viyākāsiṃ, upatissaina pucchitau|

paṭisambhidāsu taināhaṃ, aggau sambuddhasāsanai||

248.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

249.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

250.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

mahākauṭṭhikatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. uruvaiḷakassapatthairaapadānaṃ

251.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbalaukavidū muni|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji cakkhumā||

252.

 ‘‘auvādakau viñ‍ñāpakau, tārakau sabbapāṇinaṃ|

daisanākusalau buddhau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

253.

 ‘‘anukampakau kāruṇikau, hitaisī sabbapāṇinaṃ|

sampattai titthiyai sabbai, pañ‍casīlai patiṭṭhapi||

254.

 ‘‘aivaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi, suñ‍ñataṃ titthiyaihi ca|

vicittaṃ arahantaihi, vasībhūtaihi tādibhi||

255.

 ‘‘ratanānaṭṭhapañ‍ñāsaṃ, uggatau sau mahāmuni|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau||

256.

 ‘‘vassasatasahassāni, āyu vij‍jati tāvadai|

tāvatā tiṭṭhamānau sau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

257.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyā, brāhmaṇau sādhusammatau|

upaic‍ca laukapaj‍jautaṃ, assausiṃ dhammadaisanaṃ||

258.

 ‘‘tadā  mahāparisatiṃ, mahāparisasāvakaṃ|

ṭhapaintaṃ aitadaggamhi, sutvāna muditau ahaṃ||

259.

 ‘‘mahatā parivāraina, nimantaitvā mahājinaṃ|

brāhmaṇānaṃ sahassaina, sahadānamadāsahaṃ||

260.

 ‘‘mahādānaṃ daditvāna, abhivādiya nāyakaṃ|

aikamantaṃ ṭhitau haṭṭhau, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

261.

 ‘‘‘tayi saddhāya mai vīra, adhikāraguṇaina ca|

parisā mahatī hautu, nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ’||

262.

 ‘‘tadā avauca parisaṃ, gajagaj‍jitasussarau|

karavīkarutau satthā, ‘aitaṃ passatha brāhmaṇaṃ||

263.

 ‘‘‘haimavaṇṇaṃ mahābāhuṃ, kamalānanalaucanaṃ|

udaggatanujaṃ haṭṭhaṃ, saddhavantaṃ guṇai mama||

264.

 ‘‘‘aisa  patthayatai ṭhānaṃ patthayi taṃ ṭhānaṃ (syā॰), sīhaghausassa bhikkhunau|

anāgatamhi addhānai, lacchasai taṃ manaurathaṃ||

265.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

266.

 ‘‘‘tassa  dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

267.

 ‘‘itau dvainavutai kappai, ahu satthā anuttarau|

anūpamau asadisau, phussau laukagganāyakau||

268.

 ‘‘sau  ca sabbaṃ tamaṃ hantvā, vijaṭaitvā mahājaṭaṃ|

vassatai amataṃ vuṭṭhiṃ, tappayantau sadaivakaṃ||

269.

 ‘‘tadā  hi bārāṇasiyaṃ, rājā pac‍cā ahumhasai|

bhātaraumha tayau sabbai, saṃvisaṭṭhāva rājinau||

270.

 ‘‘vīraṅgarūpā balinau, saṅgāmai aparājitā|

tadā kupitapac‍cantau kuppati pac‍cantau (ka॰), amhai āha mahīpati||

271.

 ‘‘‘aitha gantvāna pac‍cantaṃ, saudhaitvā aṭṭavībalaṃ|

khaimaṃ vijiritaṃ katvā, puna daithāti bhāsatha’||

272.

 ‘‘tatau mayaṃ avaucumha, yadi daiyyāsi nāyakaṃ|

upaṭṭhānāya amhākaṃ, sādhayissāma vau tatau||

273.

 ‘‘tatau mayaṃ laddhavarā, bhūmipālaina paisitā|

nikkhittasatthaṃ pac‍cantaṃ, katvā punarupac‍ca taṃ||

274.

 ‘‘yācitvā satthupaṭṭhānaṃ, rājānaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

munivīraṃ labhitvāna, yāvajīvaṃ yajimha taṃ||

275.

 ‘‘mahagghāni ca vatthāni, paṇītāni rasāni ca|

saināsanāni rammāni, bhaisaj‍jāni hitāni ca||

276.

 ‘‘datvā sasaṅghamuninau sasaṃghassa munai (sī॰ pī॰), dhammainuppāditāni nau|

sīlavantau kāruṇikā, bhāvanāyuttamānasā||

277.

 ‘‘saddhā paricaritvāna, maittacittaina nāyakaṃ|

nibbutai tamhi laukaggai, pūjaṃ katvā yathābalaṃ||

278.

 ‘‘tatau  cutā santusitaṃ tāvatiṃsaṃ (syā॰), gatā tattha mahāsukhaṃ|

anubhūtā mayaṃ sabbai, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

279.

 ‘‘māyākārau  yathā raṅgai laddhau (syā॰ pī॰), dassaisi vikatiṃ bahuṃ|

tathā bhavai bhamantauhaṃ gamaintauhaṃ (ka॰), bhavantauhaṃ (syā॰), vidaihādhipatī ahuṃ||

280.

 ‘‘guṇācaiḷassa vākyaina, micchādiṭṭhigatāsayau|

narakaṃ maggamārūḷhau, rucāya mama dhītuyā||

281.

 ‘‘auvādaṃ nādiyitvāna, brahmunā nāradainahaṃ|

bahudhā saṃsitau santau, diṭṭhiṃ hitvāna pāpikaṃ||

282.

 ‘‘pūrayitvā  visaisaina, dasa kammapathānihaṃ|

hitvāna daihamagamiṃ, saggaṃ sabhavanaṃ yathā||

283.

 ‘‘pacchimai bhavai sampattai, brahmabandhu ahausahaṃ|

bārāṇasiyaṃ phītāyaṃ, jātau vippamahākulai||

284.

 ‘‘mac‍cubyādhijarā bhītau, augāhaitvā mahāvanaṃ jahitvāna mahādhanaṃ (sī॰), jahitvā ca mahādhanaṃ (pī॰)|

nibbānaṃ padamaisantau, jaṭilaisu paribbajiṃ||

285.

 ‘‘tadā dvai bhātarau mayhaṃ, pabbajiṃsu mayā saha|

uruvaiḷāyaṃ māpaitvā, assamaṃ nivasiṃ ahaṃ||

286.

 ‘‘kassapau nāma gauttaina, uruvaiḷanivāsikau uruvaiḷāya nivasiṃ (syā॰)|

tatau mai āsi pañ‍ñatti, uruvaiḷakassapau iti||

287.

 ‘‘nadīsakāsai bhātā mai, nadīkassapasavhayau|

āsī sakāsanāmaina, gayāyaṃ gayākassapau||

288.

 ‘‘dvai  satāni kaniṭṭhassa, tīṇi majjhassa bhātunau|

mama pañ‍ca satānūnā, sissā sabbai mamānugā||

289.

 ‘‘tadā upaic‍ca maṃ buddhau, katvāna vividhāni mai katvā nānāvidhāni mai (sī॰)|

pāṭihīrāni laukaggau, vinaisi narasārathi||

290.

 ‘‘sahassaparivāraina, ahausiṃ aihibhikkhukau|

taihaiva saha sabbaihi, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

291.

 ‘‘tai  caivañ‍ñai ca bahavau, sissā maṃ parivārayuṃ|

sāsituñ‍ca samatthauhaṃ, tatau maṃ isisattamau||

292.

 ‘‘mahāparisabhāvasmiṃ, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ|

ahau buddhai kataṃ kāraṃ, saphalaṃ mai ajāyatha||

293.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

294.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

295.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uruvaiḷakassapau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

uruvaiḷakassapatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. rādhatthairaapadānaṃ

296.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbalaukavidū muni|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji cakkhumā||

297.

 ‘‘auvādakau viñ‍ñāpakau, tārakau sabbapāṇinaṃ|

daisanākusalau buddhau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

298.

 ‘‘anukampakau kāruṇikau, hitaisī sabbapāṇinaṃ|

sampattai titthiyai sabbai, pañ‍casīlai patiṭṭhapi||

299.

 ‘‘aivaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi, suñ‍ñataṃ titthiyaihi ca|

vicittaṃ arahantaihi, vasībhūtaihi tādibhi||

300.

 ‘‘ratanānaṭṭhapañ‍ñāsaṃ, uggatau sau mahāmuni|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau||

301.

 ‘‘vassasatasahassāni, āyu vij‍jati tāvadai|

tāvatā tiṭṭhamānau sau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

302.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

upaic‍ca taṃ naravaraṃ, assausiṃ dhammadaisanaṃ||

303.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñapaintaṃ mahāvīraṃ, parisāsu visāradaṃ|

paṭibhānaiyyakaṃ bhikkhuṃ, aitadaggai vināyakaṃ||

304.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ kāraṃ katvāna, sasaṅghai laukanāyakai|

nipac‍ca sirasā pādai, taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhipatthayiṃ||

305.

 ‘‘tatau  maṃ bhagavā āha, siṅgīnikkhasamappabhau|

saraina rajanīyaina, kilaisamalahārinā||

306.

 ‘‘‘sukhī  bhavassu dīghāyu, sijjhatu paṇidhī tava|

sasaṅghai mai kataṃ kāraṃ, atīva vipulaṃ tayā||

307.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

308.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

rādhauti nāmadhaiyyaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

309.

 ‘‘‘sa tai haituguṇai tuṭṭhau, sakyaputtau narāsabhau idaṃ pādadvayaṃ syāmamūlai natthī|

paṭibhānaiyyakānaggaṃ, pañ‍ñapaissati nāyakau’||

310.

 ‘‘taṃ sutvā muditau hutvā, yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ|

maittacittau paricariṃ, satau pañ‍ñāsamāhitau||

311.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

312.

 ‘‘satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

313.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

sabbattha sukhitau āsiṃ, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

314.

 ‘‘pacchimai bhavai sampattai, giribbajapuruttamai|

jātau vippakulai niddhai, vikalacchādanāsanai||

315.

 ‘‘kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ pādāsiṃ, sāriputtassa tādinau|

yadā  jiṇṇau ca vuddhau ca, tadārāmamupāgamiṃ||

316.

 ‘‘pabbajati na maṃ kauci pabbājainti na maṃ kaici (sī॰ syā pī॰), jiṇṇadubbalathāmakaṃ|

taina dīnau vivaṇṇaṅgau vivaṇṇakau (ka॰), saukau cāsiṃ tadā ahaṃ||

317.

 ‘‘disvā mahākāruṇikau, mamamāha mamāha sau (sī॰), mamāha ca (pī॰) mahāmuni|

‘kimatthaṃ puttasaukaṭṭau, brūhi tai cittajaṃ rujaṃ’||

318.

 ‘‘‘pabbaj‍jaṃ na labhai vīra, svākkhātai tava sāsanai|

taina saukaina dīnausmi, saraṇaṃ hauhi nāyaka’||

319.

 ‘‘tadā  bhikkhū samānaitvā, apucchi munisattamau|

‘imassa adhikāraṃ yai, saranti byāharantu tai’||

320.

 ‘‘sāriputtau  tadāvauca, ‘kāramassa sarāmahaṃ|

kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ dāpaisi, piṇḍāya caratau mama’||

321.

 ‘‘‘sādhu sādhu katañ‍ñūsi, sāriputta imaṃ tuvaṃ|

pabbājaihi dijaṃ vuḍḍhaṃ, haissatājāniyau ayaṃ’||

322.

 ‘‘tatau  alatthaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, kammavācaupasampadaṃ|

na cirainaiva kālaina, pāpuṇiṃ āsavakkhayaṃ||

323.

 ‘‘sak‍kac‍caṃ muninau vākyaṃ, suṇāmi muditau yatau|

paṭibhānaiyyakānaggaṃ, tatau maṃ ṭhapayī jinau||

324.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

325.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

326.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rādhau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

rādhatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. maugharājatthairaapadānaṃ

327.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbalaukavidū muni|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji cakkhumā||

328.

 ‘‘auvādakau viñ‍ñāpakau, tārakau sabbapāṇinaṃ|

daisanākusalau buddhau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

329.

 ‘‘anukampakau kāruṇikau, hitaisī sabbapāṇinaṃ|

sampattai titthiyai sabbai, pañ‍casīlai patiṭṭhapi||

330.

 ‘‘aivaṃ nirākulaṃ āsi, suñ‍ñataṃ titthiyaihi ca|

vicittaṃ arahantaihi, vasībhūtaihi tādibhi||

331.

 ‘‘ratanānaṭṭhapañ‍ñāsaṃ, uggatau sau mahāmuni|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau||

332.

 ‘‘vassasatasahassāni, āyu vij‍jati tāvadai|

tāvatā tiṭṭhamānau sau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

333.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ  haṃsavatiyaṃ, kulai añ‍ñatarai ahuṃ|

parakammāyanai yuttau, natthi mai kiñ‍ci saṃdhanaṃ||

334.

 ‘‘paṭik‍kamanasālāyaṃ, vasantau katabhūmiyaṃ|

aggiṃ uj‍jālayiṃ tattha, daḷhaṃ kaṇhāsi sā kaṇhā siyā (sī॰ pī॰), ḍayhakaṇhā silā (syā॰) hī||

335.

 ‘‘tadā parisatiṃ nāthau, catusac‍capakāsakau|

sāvakaṃ sampakittaisi, lūkhacīvaradhārakaṃ||

336.

 ‘‘tassa tamhi guṇai tuṭṭhau, paṇipac‍ca patipaj‍ja (syā॰) tathāgataṃ|

lūkhacīvaradhāraggaṃ, patthayiṃ ṭhānamuttamaṃ||

337.

 ‘‘tadā avauca bhagavā, sāvakai padumuttarau|

‘passathaitaṃ purisakaṃ, kucailaṃ tanudaihakaṃ||

338.

 ‘‘‘pītippasan‍navadanaṃ, saddhādhanasamanvitaṃ saddhāsnaihasamanvataṃ (ka॰)|

udaggatanujaṃ haṭṭhaṃ, acalaṃ sālapiṇḍitaṃ||

339.

 ‘‘‘aisau patthaiti taṃ ṭhānaṃ, sac‍casainassa bhikkhunau|

lūkhacīvaradhārissa, tassa vaṇṇasitāsayau vaṇṇagatāsayau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

340.

 ‘‘taṃ sutvā muditau hutvā, nipac‍ca sirasā jinaṃ|

yāvajīvaṃ subhaṃ kammaṃ, karitvā jinasāsanai||

341.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsūpagau ahaṃ||

342.

 ‘‘paṭik‍kamanasālāyaṃ, bhūmidāhakakammunā|

samasahassaṃ nirayai, adayhiṃ vaidanāṭṭitau||

343.

 ‘‘taina  kammāvasaisaina, pañ‍ca jātisatānihaṃ|

manussau kulajau hutvā, jātiyā lakkhaṇaṅkitau||

344.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca  jātisatānaiva, kuṭṭharaugasamappitau|

mahādukkhaṃ anubhaviṃ, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

345.

 ‘‘imasmiṃ bhaddakai kappai, upariṭṭhaṃ yasassinaṃ|

piṇḍapātaina tappaisiṃ, pasan‍namānasau ahaṃ||

346.

 ‘‘taina  kammavisaisaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

347.

 ‘‘pacchimai bhavai sampattai, ajāyiṃ khattiyai kulai|

pitunau ac‍cayaināhaṃ, mahāraj‍jasamappitau||

348.

 ‘‘kuṭṭharaugādhibhūtauhaṃ, na ratiṃ na sukhaṃ labhai|

maughaṃ raj‍jaṃ sukhaṃ yasmā, maugharājā tatau ahaṃ||

349.

 ‘‘kāyassa dausaṃ disvāna, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

bāvarissa dijaggassa, sissattaṃ ajjhupāgamiṃ||

350.

 ‘‘mahatā parivāraina, upaic‍ca naranāyakaṃ|

apucchiṃ nipuṇaṃ pañhaṃ, taṃ vīraṃ vādisūdanaṃ||

351.

 ‘‘‘ayaṃ laukau parau laukau, brahmalaukau sadaivakau|

diṭṭhiṃ nau diṭṭhā nau (sī॰), diṭṭhaṃ nau (pī॰), diṭṭhiṃ tai (syā॰) nābhijānāmi, gautamassa yasassinau||

352.

 ‘‘‘aivābhik‍kantadassāviṃ, atthi pañhaina āgamaṃ|

kathaṃ laukaṃ avaikkhantaṃ, mac‍curājā na passati’||

353.

 ‘‘‘suñ‍ñatau  laukaṃ avaikkhassu, maugharāja sadā satau|

attānudiṭṭhiṃ uhac‍ca, aivaṃ mac‍cutarau siyā||

354.

 ‘‘‘aivaṃ laukaṃ avaikkhantaṃ, mac‍curājā na passati’|

iti maṃ abhaṇi buddhau, sabbaraugatikicchakau||

355.

 ‘‘saha gāthāvasānaina, kaisamassuvivaj‍jitau|

kāsāvavatthavasanau, āsiṃ bhikkhu tathārahā||

356.

 ‘‘saṅghikaisu vihāraisu, na vasiṃ raugapīḷitau|

mā vihārau padussīti, vātaraugaihi pīḷitau vācāyābhisupīḷitau (syā॰ pī॰), vātaraugī supīḷitau (ka॰)||

357.

 ‘‘saṅkārakūṭā  āhitvā, susānā rathikāhi ca|

tatau saṅghāṭiṃ karitvā, dhārayiṃ lūkhacīvaraṃ||

358.

 ‘‘mahābhisak‍kau tasmiṃ mai, guṇai tuṭṭhau vināyakau|

lūkhacīvaradhārīnaṃ, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

359.

 ‘‘puñ‍ñapāpaparikkhīṇau, sabbaraugavivaj‍jitau|

sikhīva anupādānau, nibbāyissamanāsavau||

360.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

361.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

362.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā maugharājā thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

maugharājatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

kac‍cāyanavaggau catupañ‍ñāsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kac‍cānau vak‍kalī thairau, mahākappinasavhayau|

dabbau kumāranāmau ca, bāhiyau kauṭṭhikau vasī||

uruvaiḷakassapau rādhau, maugharājā ca paṇḍitau|

tīṇi gāthāsatānaittha, bāsaṭṭhi caiva piṇḍitā||


55. bhaddiyavaggau

1. lakuṇḍabhaddiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammaisu cakkhumā|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

2.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, saiṭṭhiputtau mahaddhanau|

jaṅghāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ, saṅghārāmaṃ agacchahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘tadā sau laukapaj‍jautau, dhammaṃ daisaisi nāyakau|

mañ‍jussarānaṃ pavaraṃ, sāvakaṃ abhikittayi||

4.

 ‘‘taṃ sutvā muditau hutvā, kāraṃ katvā mahaisinau|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, taṃ ṭhānamabhipatthayiṃ||

5.

 ‘‘tadā buddhau viyākāsi, saṅghamajjhai vināyakau|

‘anāgatamhi addhānai, lacchasai taṃ manaurathaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

7.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

bhaddiyau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

8.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, phussau uppaj‍ji nāyakau|

durāsadau duppasahau, sabbalaukuttamau jinau||

10.

 ‘‘caraṇaina ca sampan‍nau, brahā uju patāpavā|

hitaisī sabbasattānaṃ sabbapāṇīnaṃ (sī॰), bahuṃ maucaisi bandhanā||

11.

 ‘‘nandārāmavanai tassa, ahausiṃ phussakaukilau pussakaukilau (sī॰ syā॰)|

gandhakuṭisamāsan‍nai, ambarukkhai vasāmahaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘tadā  piṇḍāya gacchantaṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ jinuttamaṃ|

disvā cittaṃ pasādaitvā, mañ‍junābhinikūjahaṃ mañ‍junādaina kūjahaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)||

13.

 ‘‘rājuyyānaṃ  tadā gantvā, supak‍kaṃ kanakattacaṃ|

ambapiṇḍaṃ gahaitvāna, sambuddhassaupanāmayiṃ||

14.

 ‘‘tadā mai cittamañ‍ñāya, mahākāruṇikau jinau|

upaṭṭhākassa hatthatau, pattaṃ paggaṇhi nāyakau||

15.

 ‘‘adāsiṃ  haṭṭhacittauhaṃ tuṭṭhacittauhaṃ (sī॰), ambapiṇḍaṃ mahāmunai|

pattai pakkhippa pakkhaihi, pañ‍jaliṃ pakkhaihañ‍jaliṃ (sī॰) katvāna mañ‍junā||

16.

 ‘‘saraina rajanīyaina, savanīyaina vaggunā|

vassantau buddhapūjatthaṃ, nīḷaṃ niddaṃ (syā॰ pī॰) gantvā nipaj‍jahaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘tadā muditacittaṃ maṃ, buddhapaimagatāsayaṃ|

sakuṇagghi upāgantvā, ghātayī duṭṭhamānasau||

18.

 ‘‘tatau cutauhaṃ tusitai, anubhautvā mahāsukhaṃ|

manussayaunimāgacchiṃ, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

19.

 ‘‘imamhi  bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

20.

 ‘‘sāsanaṃ jautayitvā sau, abhibhuyya kutitthiyai|

vinayitvāna vainaiyyai, nibbutau sau sasāvakau||

21.

 ‘‘nibbutai tamhi laukaggai, pasan‍nā janatā bahū|

pūjanatthāya buddhassa, thūpaṃ kubbanti satthunau||

22.

 ‘‘‘sattayaujanikaṃ thūpaṃ, sattaratanabhūsitaṃ|

karissāma mahaisissa’, ic‍caivaṃ mantayanti tai||

23.

 ‘‘kikinau kāsirājassa, tadā saināya nāyakau|

hutvāhaṃ appamāṇassa, pamāṇaṃ caitiyai vadiṃ||

24.

 ‘‘tadā tai mama vākyaina, caitiyaṃ yaujanuggataṃ|

akaṃsu naravīrassa, nānāratanabhūsitaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘pacchimai  ca bhavai dāni, jātau saiṭṭhikulai ahaṃ|

sāvatthiyaṃ puravarai, iddhai phītai mahaddhanai||

27.

 ‘‘purappavaisai sugataṃ, disvā vimhitamānasau|

pabbajitvāna na ciraṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

28.

 ‘‘caitiyassa pamāṇaṃ yaṃ, akariṃ taina kammunā|

lakuṇḍakasarīrauhaṃ, jātau paribhavārahau||

29.

 ‘‘saraina  madhuraināhaṃ, pūjitvā isisattamaṃ|

mañ‍jussarānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, aggattamanupāpuṇiṃ||

30.

 ‘‘phaladānaina  buddhassa, guṇānussaraṇaina ca|

sāmañ‍ñaphalasampan‍nau, viharāmi anāsavau||

31.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

32.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, buddhasaiṭṭhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā lakuṇḍabhaddiyau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

lakuṇḍabhaddiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. kaṅkhāraivatatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammaisu cakkhumā|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

35.

 ‘‘sīhahanu brahmagirau, haṃsadundubhinissanau haṃsadundubhinissarau (sī॰) … nissavanau (pī॰) … sāvanau (syā॰)|

nāgavik‍kantagamanau, candasūrādikappabhau||

36.

 ‘‘mahāmatī  mahāvīrau, mahājhāyī mahābalau mahāgati (syā॰), mahāhitau (pī॰)|

mahākāruṇikau nāthau, mahātamapanūdanau mahātamavidhaṃsanau (syā॰), mahātamanisūdanau (pī॰)||

37.

 ‘‘sa  kadāci tilaukaggau, vainaiyyaṃ vinayaṃ bahuṃ vainaiyyai viniyaṃ bahū (sī॰)|

dhammaṃ daisaisi sambuddhau, sattāsayavidū muni||

38.

 ‘‘jhāyiṃ jhānarataṃ vīraṃ, upasantaṃ anāvilaṃ|

vaṇṇayantau parisatiṃ, tausaisi tausaiti (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) janataṃ jinau||

39.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, brāhmaṇau vaidapāragū|

dhammaṃ sutvāna muditau, taṃ ṭhānamabhipatthayiṃ||

40.

 ‘‘tadā jinau viyākāsi, saṅghamajjhai vināyakau|

‘muditau hauhi tvaṃ brahmai, lacchasai taṃ manaurathaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

42.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

raivatau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

43.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘pacchimai ca bhavai dāni, jātauhaṃ kauliyai purai|

khattiyai kulasampan‍nai, iddhai phītai mahaddhanai||

45.

 ‘‘yadā  kapilavatthusmiṃ, buddhau dhammamadaisayi|

tadā pasan‍nau sugatai, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘kaṅkhā mai bahulā āsi, kappākappai tahiṃ tahiṃ|

sabbaṃ taṃ vinayī buddhau, daisaitvā dhammamuttamaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘tatauhaṃ  tiṇṇasaṃsārau, sadā jhānasukhai ratau|

viharāmi tadā buddhau, maṃ disvā aitadabravi||

48.

 ‘‘‘yā  kāci kaṅkhā idha vā huraṃ vā, sakavaidiyā vā paravaidiyā vā|

yai jhāyinau tā pajahanti sabbā, ātāpinau brahmacariyaṃ carantā’||

49.

 ‘‘satasahassai kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

sumuttau saravaigauva kilaisai jhāpayiṃ mama||

50.

 ‘‘tatau jhānarataṃ disvā, buddhau laukantagū muni|

jhāyīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aggau, pañ‍ñāpaiti mahāmati||

51.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

52.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṅkhāraivatau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kaṅkhāraivatatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sīvalitthairaapadānaṃ

54.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammaisu cakkhumā|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

55.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ tassa asaṅkhaiyyaṃ, samādhi vajirūpamau|

asaṅkhaiyyaṃ ñāṇavaraṃ, vimutti ca anaupamā||

56.

 ‘‘manujāmaranāgānaṃ, brahmānañ‍ca samāgamai|

samaṇabrāhmaṇākiṇṇai, dhammaṃ daisaisi nāyakau||

57.

 ‘‘sasāvakaṃ mahālābhiṃ, puñ‍ñavantaṃ jutindharaṃ|

ṭhapaisi aitadaggamhi, parisāsu visāradau||

58.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ khattiyau āsiṃ, nagarai haṃsasavhayai|

sutvā jinassa taṃ vākyaṃ, sāvakassa guṇaṃ bahuṃ||

59.

 ‘‘nimantayitvā  sattāhaṃ, bhaujayitvā sasāvakaṃ|

mahādānaṃ daditvāna, taṃ ṭhānamabhipatthayiṃ||

60.

 ‘‘tadā maṃ vinataṃ pādai, disvāna purisāsabhau|

saraina mahatā vīrau sussaraina mahāvīrau (sī॰ pī॰), idaṃ vacanamabravi||

61.

 ‘‘‘tatau jinassa vacanaṃ, sautukāmā mahājanā|

daivadānavagandhabbā, brahmānau ca mahiddhikā’||

62.

 ‘‘samaṇabrāhmaṇā caiva, namassiṃsu katañ‍jalī|

‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama||

63.

 ‘‘‘khattiyaina  mahādānaṃ, din‍naṃ sattāhikampi vau sattahikaṃ mi vau (sī॰), sattahikādhikaṃ (syā॰), sattahikaṃ vibhau (pī॰)|

sautukāmā phalaṃ tassa, byākarauhi mahāmunai’||

64.

 ‘‘tatau avauca bhagavā, ‘suṇātha mama bhāsitaṃ|

appamaiyyamhi buddhamhi, sasaṅghamhi patiṭṭhitā saṃghamhi suppatiṭṭhitā (sī॰ pī॰)||

65.

 ‘‘‘dakkhiṇā tāya dakkhiṇādāya (syā॰ pī॰) kau vattā, appamaiyyaphalā hi sā|

api cai sa mahābhaugau, ṭhānaṃ patthaiti uttamaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘‘lābhī vipulalābhānaṃ, yathā bhikkhu sudassanau|

tathāhampi bhavaiyyanti, lacchasai taṃ anāgatai||

67.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

68.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sīvali nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

69.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsūpagau ahaṃ||

70.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, vipassī laukanāyakau|

uppaj‍ji cārudassanau, sabbadhammavipassakau||

71.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ  bandhumatiyaṃ, kulassañ‍ñatarassa ca|

dayitau passitau caiva, āsiṃ kammantavāvaṭau kammantabyāvaṭau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰)||

72.

 ‘‘tadā añ‍ñatarau pūgau, vipassissa mahaisinau|

parivaisaṃ akārayi, mahantamativissutaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘niṭṭhitai  ca mahādānai, daduṃ khaj‍jakasañhitaṃ|

navaṃ dadhiṃ madhuñ‍caiva, vicinaṃ naiva addasuṃ||

74.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ taṃ gahaitvāna, navaṃ dadhiṃ madhumpi ca|

kammassāmigharaṃ gacchiṃ, tamaisantā mamaddasuṃ||

75.

 ‘‘sahassamapi datvāna, nālabhiṃsu ca taṃ dvayaṃ|

tatauhaṃ aivaṃ cintaisiṃ, ‘naitaṃ haissati aurakaṃ||

76.

 ‘‘‘yathā imai janā sabbai, sak‍karaunti tathāgataṃ|

ahampi kāraṃ kassāmi, sasaṅghai laukanāyakai’||

77.

 ‘‘tadāhamaivaṃ  cintaitvā, dadhiṃ madhuñ‍ca aikatau|

madditvā laukanāthassa, sasaṅghassa adāsahaṃ||

78.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘punāhaṃ bārāṇasiyaṃ, rājā hutvā mahāyasau|

sattukassa tadā duṭṭhau, dvāraraudhamakārayiṃ||

80.

 ‘‘tadā tapassinau ruddhā, aikāhaṃ rakkhitā ahuṃ|

tatau tassa vipākaina, pāpatiṃ pāpiṭṭhaṃ (syā॰) pāpattaṃ (ka॰) nirayaṃ bhusaṃ||

81.

 ‘‘pacchimai ca bhavai dāni, jātauhaṃ kauliyai purai|

suppavāsā ca mai mātā, mahāli licchavī pitā||

82.

 ‘‘khattiyai puñ‍ñakammaina, dvāraraudhassa vāhasā|

satta vassāni nivasiṃ, mātukucchimhi dukkhitau||

83.

 ‘‘sattāhaṃ  dvāramūḷhauhaṃ, mahādukkhasamappitau|

mātā mai chandadānaina, aivaṃ āsi sudukkhitā||

84.

 ‘‘suvatthitauhaṃ  nikkhantau, buddhaina anukampitau|

nikkhantadivasaiyaiva, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

85.

 ‘‘upajjhā sāriputtau mai, mauggal‍lānau mahiddhikau|

kaisai auraupayantau mai, anusāsi mahāmati||

86.

 ‘‘kaisaisu  chij‍jamānaisu, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ|

daivā nāgā manussā ca, pac‍cayai upanainti mai||

87.

 ‘‘padumuttaranāthañ‍ca, vipassiñ‍ca vināyakaṃ|

yaṃ pūjayiṃ pamuditau, pac‍cayaihi visaisatau||

88.

 ‘‘tatau taisaṃ visaisaina, kammānaṃ vipuluttamaṃ|

lābhaṃ labhāmi sabbattha, vanai gāmai jalai thalai||

89.

 ‘‘raivataṃ dassanatthāya, yadā yāti vināyakau|

tiṃsabhikkhusahassaihi, saha laukagganāyakau||

90.

 ‘‘tadā daivaupaṇītaihi, mamatthāya mahāmati|

pac‍cayaihi mahāvīrau, sasaṅghau laukanāyakau||

91.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhitau  mayā buddhau, gantvā raivatamaddasa|

tatau jaitavanaṃ gantvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

92.

 ‘‘‘lābhīnaṃ sīvali aggau, mama sissaisu bhikkhavau’|

sabbalaukahitau satthā, kittayī parisāsu maṃ||

93.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

94.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīvalithairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

sīvalitthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. vaṅgīsatthairaapadānaṃ

96.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammaisu cakkhumā|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

97.

 ‘‘yathāpi  sāgarai ūmi, gaganai viya tārakā|

aivaṃ pāvacanaṃ tassa, arahantaihi cittitaṃ||

98.

 ‘‘sadaivāsuranāgaihi, manujaihi purakkhatau|

samaṇabrāhmaṇākiṇṇai, janamajjhai jinuttamau||

99.

 ‘‘pabhāhi anurañ‍jantau, laukai laukaṃ (sī॰) laukantagū jinau|

vacanaina vibaudhaintau, vainaiyyapadumāni sau||

100.

 ‘‘vaisāraj‍jaihi sampan‍nau, catūhi purisuttamau|

pahīnabhayasāraj‍jau, khaimappattau visāradau||

101.

 ‘‘āsabhaṃ  pavaraṃ ṭhānaṃ, buddhabhūmiñ‍ca kaivalaṃ|

paṭijānāti laukaggau, natthi sañ‍caudakau k‍vaci||

102.

 ‘‘sīhanādamasambhītaṃ, nadatau tassa tādinau|

daivau narau vā brahmā vā, paṭivattā na vij‍jati||

103.

 ‘‘daisaintau  pavaraṃ dhammaṃ, santāraintau sadaivakaṃ|

dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti, parisāsu visāradau||

104.

 ‘‘paṭibhānavataṃ aggaṃ, sāvakaṃ sādhusammataṃ|

guṇaṃ bahuṃ pakittaitvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi taṃ||

105.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, brāhmaṇau sādhusammatau|

sabbavaidavidū jātau, vāgīsau vādisūdanau||

106.

 ‘‘upaic‍ca taṃ mahāvīraṃ, sutvāhaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ|

pītivaraṃ paṭilabhiṃ, sāvakassa guṇai ratau||

107.

 ‘‘nimantaitvāva sugataṃ, sasaṅghaṃ laukanandanaṃ|

sattāhaṃ bhaujayitvāhaṃ, dussaihacchādayiṃ tadā||

108.

 ‘‘nipac‍ca sirasā pādai, kataukāsau katañ‍jalī|

aikamantaṃ ṭhitau haṭṭhau, santhaviṃ jinamuttamaṃ||

109.

 ‘‘‘namau tai vādimaddana vādisaddula (sī॰ pī॰), vādisūdana (syā॰), namau tai isisattama purisuttama (sī॰ pī॰)|

namau tai sabbalaukagga, namau tai abhayaṅkara||

110.

 ‘‘‘namau  tai māramathana māramasana (aṭṭha॰), namau tai diṭṭhisūdana|

namau tai santisukhada, namau tai saraṇaṅkara||

111.

 ‘‘‘anāthānaṃ  bhavaṃ nāthau, bhītānaṃ abhayappadau|

vissāmabhūmi vissāsaṃ bhūmi (syā॰), vissānabhūmi (pī॰) santānaṃ, saraṇaṃ saraṇaisinaṃ’||

112.

 ‘‘aivamādīhi sambuddhaṃ, santhavitvā mahāguṇaṃ|

avaucaṃ vādisūdassa vādisūrassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), gatiṃ pappaumi bhikkhunau||

113.

 ‘‘tadā avauca bhagavā, anantapaṭibhānavā|

‘yau sau buddhaṃ abhaujaisi, sattāhaṃ sahasāvakaṃ||

114.

 ‘‘‘guṇañ‍ca mai pakittaisi, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi|

aisau patthayatai ṭhānaṃ, vādisūdassa bhikkhunau||

115.

 ‘‘‘anāgatamhi  addhānai, lacchasai taṃ manaurathaṃ|

daivamānusasampattiṃ, anubhautvā anappakaṃ||

116.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

117.

 ‘‘‘tassa  dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

vaṅgīsau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

118.

 ‘‘taṃ sutvā muditau hutvā, yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ|

pac‍cayaihi upaṭṭhāsiṃ, maittacittau tathāgataṃ||

119.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tusitaṃ tāvatiṃsaṃ (syā॰) agamāsahaṃ||

120.

 ‘‘pacchimai ca bhavai dāni, jātau vippakulai paribbājakulai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ahaṃ|

pac‍cājātau sampattau ca (ka॰) yadā āsiṃ, jātiyā sattavassikau||

121.

 ‘‘sabbavaidavidū  jātau, vādasatthavisāradau|

vādissarau vaggussarau (syā॰ pī॰) cittakathī, paravādappamaddanau||

122.

 ‘‘vaṅgai jātauti vaṅgīsau, vacanai issarauti vā|

vaṅgīsau iti mai nāmaṃ, abhavī laukasammataṃ||

123.

 ‘‘yadāhaṃ  viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau, ṭhitau paṭhamayaubbanai|

tadā rājagahai rammai, sāriputtamahaddasaṃ mathaddasaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), ca addasaṃ (syā॰)||

pañ‍cavīsatimaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

124.

 ‘‘piṇḍāya vicarantaṃ taṃ, pattapāṇiṃ susaṃvutaṃ|

alaulakkhiṃ mitabhāṇiṃ, yugamattaṃ nidakkhitaṃ nirikkhataṃ (sī॰ pī॰), udikkhataṃ (syā॰)||

125.

 ‘‘taṃ disvā vimhitau hutvā, avaucaṃ mamanucchavaṃ mananucchavaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

kaṇikāraṃva nicitaṃ kaṇikāraparicitaṃ (pī॰), khaṇikaṃ ṭhānaracitaṃ (sī॰), cittaṃ gāthāpadaṃ ahaṃ||

126.

 ‘‘ācikkhi sau mai satthāraṃ, sambuddhaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

tadā sau paṇḍitau vīrau, uttariṃ uttaraṃ (sī॰ pī॰) samavauca mai||

127.

 ‘‘virāgasaṃhitaṃ vākyaṃ, katvā duddasamuttamaṃ|

vicittapaṭibhānaihi, tausitau taina tādinā||

128.

 ‘‘nipac‍ca sirasā pādai, ‘pabbājaihī’ti maṃ bravi|

tatau maṃ sa mahāpañ‍ñau, buddhasaiṭṭhamupānayi||

129.

 ‘‘nipac‍ca sirasā pādai, nisīdiṃ satthu santikai|

mamāha vadataṃ saiṭṭhau, kac‍ci vaṅgīsa jānāsi sac‍caṃ vaṅgīsa kac‍ci tai (syā॰)||

130.

 ‘‘kiñ‍ci  sippanti tassāhaṃ, ‘jānāmī’ti ca abraviṃ|

matasīsaṃ  vanacchuddhaṃ, api bārasavassikaṃ|

tava vij‍jāvisaisaina, sacai sak‍kausi vācaya bhāsaya (sī॰ pī॰)||

131.

 ‘‘āmauti mai paṭiñ‍ñātai, tīṇi sīsāni dassayi|

nirayanaradaivaisu, upapan‍nai avācayiṃ||

132.

 ‘‘tadā khīṇāsavassaiva pac‍caikabuddhassa (sī॰ pī॰), sīsaṃ dassaisi nāyakau|

tatauhaṃ vihatārabbhau, pabbaj‍jaṃ samayācisaṃ||

133.

 ‘‘pabbajitvāna sugataṃ, santhavāmi tahiṃ tahiṃ|

tatau maṃ kabbavittausi kavicittauti (syā॰ pī॰), ujjhāyantiha bhikkhavau||

134.

 ‘‘tatau  vīmaṃsanatthaṃ mai, āha buddhau vināyakau|

tak‍kikā panimā gāthā, ṭhānasau paṭibhanti taṃ||

135.

 ‘‘na kabbavittauhaṃ vīra, ṭhānasau paṭibhanti maṃ|

taina hi dāni vaṅgīsa, ṭhānasau santhavāhi maṃ||

136.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ santhaviṃ vīraṃ, gāthāhi isisattamaṃ|

ṭhānasau mai tadā tuṭṭhau, jinau aggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

137.

 ‘‘paṭibhānaina cittaina, añ‍ñaisamatimañ‍ñahaṃ|

paisalai taina saṃviggau, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

138.

 ‘‘‘paṭibhānavataṃ aggau, añ‍ñau kauci na vij‍jati|

yathāyaṃ bhikkhu vaṅgīsau, aivaṃ dhāraitha bhikkhavau’||

139.

 ‘‘satasahassai kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

sumuttau saravaigauva kilaisai jhāpayiṃ mama||

140.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

141.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

142.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaṅgīsau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

vaṅgīsatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. nandakatthairaapadānaṃ

143.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammaisu cakkhumā|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

144.

 ‘‘hitāya sabbasattānaṃ, sukhāya vadataṃ varau|

atthāya purisājañ‍ñau, paṭipan‍nau sadaivakai||

145.

 ‘‘yasaggapattau sirimā, kittivaṇṇabhatau kittivaṇṇa bhaṭau (syā॰ ka॰) jinau|

pūjitau sabbalaukassa, disā sabbāsu vissutau||

146.

 ‘‘uttiṇṇavicikicchau  sau, vītivattakathaṃkathau|

paripuṇṇamanasaṅkappau, pattau sambaudhimuttamaṃ||

147.

 ‘‘anuppan‍nassa maggassa, uppādaitā naruttamau|

anakkhātañ‍ca akkhāsi, asañ‍jātañ‍ca sañ‍janī||

148.

 ‘‘maggañ‍ñū  maggavidū sau maggavidū (sī॰ pī॰) ca, maggakkhāyī narāsabhau|

maggassa kusalau satthā, sārathīnaṃ varuttamau naruttamau (syā॰)||

149.

 ‘‘tadā mahākāruṇikau, dhammaṃ daisaisi nāyakau|

nimuggai kāmapaṅkamhi mauhapaṅkamhi (sī॰ syā॰), mauhamaggamhi (pī॰), samuddharati pāṇinai||

150.

 ‘‘bhikkhunīnaṃ auvadanai, sāvakaṃ saiṭṭhasammataṃ|

vaṇṇayaṃ aitadaggamhi, pañ‍ñapaisi mahāmuni||

151.

 ‘‘taṃ sutvāhaṃ pamuditau, nimantaitvā tathāgataṃ|

bhaujayitvā sasaṅghaṃ taṃ, patthayiṃ ṭhānamuttamaṃ||

152.

 ‘‘tadā pamuditau nāthau, maṃ avauca mahāisi|

‘sukhī bhavassu dīghāvu dīghāyu (sī॰ syā॰), lacchasai taṃ manaurathaṃ||

153.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

154.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

nandakau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

155.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsūpagau ahaṃ||

156.

 ‘‘pacchimai  ca bhavai dāni, jātau saiṭṭhikulai ahaṃ|

sāvatthiyaṃ purai varai, iddhai phītai mahaddhanai||

157.

 ‘‘purappavaisai sugataṃ, disvā vimhitamānasau|

jaitārāmapaṭiggāhai, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

158.

 ‘‘nacirainaiva  kālaina, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ|

tatauhaṃ tiṇṇasaṃsārau, sāsitau sabbadassinā||

159.

 ‘‘bhikkhunīnaṃ  dhammakathaṃ, paṭipucchākariṃ ahaṃ|

sāsitā tā mayā sabbā, abhaviṃsu anāsavā||

160.

 ‘‘satāni pañ‍canūnāni, tadā tuṭṭhau mahāhitau|

bhikkhunīnaṃ auvadataṃ, aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

161.

 ‘‘satasahassai kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

sumuttau saravaigauva, kilaisai jhāpayiṃ mama||

162.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

163.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

164.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nandakau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

nandakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kāḷudāyitthairaapadānaṃ

165.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammaisu cakkhumā|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

166.

 ‘‘nāyakānaṃ  varau satthā, guṇāguṇavidū jinau|

katañ‍ñū katavaidī ca, titthai yaujaiti pāṇinai pāṇinau (sī॰ syā pī॰)||

167.

 ‘‘sabbañ‍ñutaina ñāṇaina, tulayitvā dayāsayau|

daisaiti pavaraṃ dhammaṃ, anantaguṇasañ‍cayau||

168.

 ‘‘sa  kadāci mahāvīrau, anantajinasaṃsari anantajanasaṃsadi (sī॰), anantajanasaṃsudhi (syā॰), anantajanasaṃsarī (pī॰)|

daisaiti madhuraṃ dhammaṃ, catusac‍cūpasañhitaṃ||

169.

 ‘‘sutvāna taṃ dhammavaraṃ, ādimajjhantasaubhaṇaṃ|

pāṇasatasahassānaṃ, dhammābhisamayau ahu||

170.

 ‘‘nin‍nāditā tadā bhūmi, gaj‍jiṃsu ca payaudharā|

sādhukāraṃ pavattiṃsu, daivabrahmanarāsurā||

171.

 ‘‘‘ahau  kāruṇikau satthā, ahau saddhammadaisanā|

ahau bhavasamuddamhi, nimuggai uddharī jinau’||

172.

 ‘‘aivaṃ pavaidajātaisu, sanarāmarabrahmasu|

kulappasādakānaggaṃ, sāvakaṃ vaṇṇayī jinau||

173.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, jātaumac‍cakulai ahuṃ|

pāsādikau dassaniyau, pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñavā||

174.

 ‘‘haṃsārāmamupaic‍cāhaṃ, vanditvā taṃ tathāgataṃ|

suṇitvā madhuraṃ dhammaṃ, kāraṃ katvā ca tādinau||

175.

 ‘‘nipac‍ca pādamūlaihaṃ, imaṃ vacanamabraviṃ|

‘kulappasādakānaggau, yau tayā santhutau yau tava sāsanai (syā॰) munai||

176.

 ‘‘‘tādisau  haumahaṃ vīra tādisauhaṃ mahāvīra (syā॰ ka॰), buddhasaiṭṭhassa sāsanai’|

tadā mahākāruṇikau, siñ‍cantau vā mataina maṃ||

177.

 ‘‘āha maṃ ‘putta uttiṭṭha, lacchasai taṃ manaurathaṃ|

kathaṃ nāma jinai kāraṃ, katvāna viphalau siyā||

178.

 ‘‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

179.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

udāyi nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

180.

 ‘‘taṃ sutvā muditau hutvā, yāvajīvaṃ tadā jinaṃ|

maittacittau paricariṃ, pac‍cayaihi vināyakaṃ||

181.

 ‘‘taina kammavipākaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

182.

 ‘‘pacchimai  ca bhavai dāni, rammai kapilavatthavai|

jātau mahāmac‍cakulai, suddhaudanamahīpatai suddhaudanau mahīpati (syā॰)||

183.

 ‘‘tadā ajāyi siddhatthau, rammai lumbinikānanai|

hitāya sabbalaukassa, sukhāya ca narāsabhau||

184.

 ‘‘tadahaiva  ahaṃ jātau, saha tainaiva vaḍḍhitau|

piyau sahāyau dayitau, viyattau nītikauvidau||

185.

 ‘‘aikūnatiṃsau vayasā, nikkhamitvā agāratau nikkhantau pabbajitthasau (sī॰ syā॰)|

chabbassaṃ vītināmaitvā, āsi buddhau vināyakau||

186.

 ‘‘jaitvā  sasainakaṃ māraṃ, khaipayitvāna āsavai|

bhavaṇṇavaṃ taritvāna, buddhau āsi sadaivakai||

187.

 ‘‘isivhayaṃ gamitvāna isivhayaṃ patanaṃ gantvā (syā॰), vinaitvā pañ‍cavaggiyai|

tatau vinaisi bhagavā, gantvā gantvā tahiṃ tahiṃ||

188.

 ‘‘vainaiyyai vinayantau sau, saṅgaṇhantau sadaivakaṃ|

upaic‍ca magadhai giriṃ māgadagiriṃ (sī॰), maṅgalāgiriṃ (pī॰), viharittha tadā jinau||

189.

 ‘‘tadā  suddhaudanaināhaṃ, bhūmipālaina paisitau|

gantvā disvā dasabalaṃ, pabbajitvārahā ahuṃ||

190.

 ‘‘tadā mahaisiṃ yācitvā, pāpayiṃ kapilavhayaṃ|

tatau purāhaṃ gantvāna, pasādaisiṃ mahākulaṃ||

191.

 ‘‘jinau tasmiṃ guṇai tuṭṭhau, maṃ mahāparisāya sau mamāha purisāsabhau (syā॰ pī॰)|

kulappasādakānaggaṃ, pañ‍ñāpaisi vināyakau||

192.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

193.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

194.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kāḷudāyithairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

kāḷudāyitthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. abhayatthairaapadānaṃ

195.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammaisu cakkhumā|

itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau||

196.

 ‘‘saraṇagamanai  kiñ‍ci, nivaisaisi tathāgatau|

kiñ‍ci sīlai nivaisaisi, dasakammapathuttamai||

197.

 ‘‘daiti kassaci sau vīrau, sāmañ‍ñaphalamuttamaṃ|

samāpattī tathā aṭṭha, tissau vij‍jā pavacchati||

198.

 ‘‘chaḷabhiñ‍ñāsu yaujaisi, kiñ‍ci sattaṃ naruttamau|

daiti kassaci nāthau sau, catassau paṭisambhidā||

199.

 ‘‘baudhanaiyyaṃ pajaṃ disvā, asaṅkhaiyyampi yaujanaṃ asaṅkhaiyyaipi yaujanai sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

khaṇaina upagantvāna, vinaiti narasārathi||

200.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ, ahausiṃ brāhmaṇatrajau|

pāragū sabbavaidānaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇasammatau||

201.

 ‘‘niruttiyā ca kusalau, nighaṇḍumhi visāradau|

padakau kaiṭubhavidū, chandauvicitikauvidau||

202.

 ‘‘jaṅghāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ, haṃsārāmamupaic‍cahaṃ|

addasaṃ varadaṃ vadataṃ (sī॰ pī॰), pavaraṃ (syā॰) saiṭṭhaṃ, mahājanapurakkhataṃ||

203.

 ‘‘daisaintaṃ virajaṃ dhammaṃ, pac‍canīkamatī ahaṃ|

upaitvā tassa kalyāṇaṃ, sutvāna vimalaṃ ahaṃ vākyāni, sutvāna vimalānahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

204.

 ‘‘byāhataṃ  punaruttaṃ vā, apatthaṃ vā niratthakaṃ|

nāddasaṃ tassa muninau, tatau pabbajitau ahaṃ||

205.

 ‘‘nacirainaiva kālaina, sabbasattavisāradau|

nipuṇau buddhavacanai, ahausiṃ guṇisammatau||

206.

 ‘‘tadā catassau gāthāyau, ganthayitvā subyañ‍janā|

santhavitvā tilaukaggaṃ, daisayissaṃ dinai dinai||

207.

 ‘‘virattausi mahāvīrau, saṃsārai sabhayai vasaṃ|

karuṇāya na nibbāyi, tatau kāruṇikau muni||

208.

 ‘‘puthuj‍janau  vayau santau, na kilaisavasau ahu|

sampajānau satiyuttau, tasmā aisau acintiyau||

209.

 ‘‘dubbalāni  kilaisāni, yassāsayagatāni mai|

ñāṇaggiparidaḍḍhāni, na khīyiṃsu tamabbhutaṃ||

210.

 ‘‘yau sabbalaukassa garu, laukau laukai (syā॰ ka॰) yassa tathā garu|

tathāpi laukācariyau, laukau tassānuvattakau||

211.

 ‘‘aivamādīhi sambuddhaṃ, kittayaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ|

yāvajīvaṃ karitvāna, gatau saggaṃ tatau cutau||

212.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

213.

 ‘‘daivalaukai mahāraj‍jaṃ, pādaisiṃ kañ‍canagghiyaṃ dibbānubhaujahaṃ tadā (syā॰), raj‍jaṃ pādaisi kaṃcayaṃ (sī॰)|

cak‍kavattī mahāraj‍jaṃ, bahusaunubhaviṃ ahaṃ||

214.

 ‘‘duvai  bhavai pajāyāmi, daivattai atha mānusai|

añ‍ñaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

215.

 ‘‘duvai kulai pajāyāmi, khattiyai atha brāhmaṇai|

nīcai kulai na jāyāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

216.

 ‘‘pacchimai ca bhavai dāni, giribbajapuruttamai|

rañ‍ñauhaṃ bimbisārassa, puttau nāmaina cābhayau||

217.

 ‘‘pāpamittavasaṃ gantvā, nigaṇṭhaina vimauhitau|

paisitau nāṭaputtaina, buddhasaiṭṭhamupaic‍cahaṃ||

218.

 ‘‘pucchitvā  nipuṇaṃ pañhaṃ, sutvā byākaraṇuttamaṃ|

pabbajitvāna naciraṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

219.

 ‘‘kittayitvā jinavaraṃ, kittitau haumi sabbadā|

sugandhadaihavadanau, āsiṃ sukhasamappitau||

220.

 ‘‘tikkhahāsalahupañ‍ñau, mahāpañ‍ñau tathaivahaṃ|

vicittapaṭibhānau ca, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

221.

 ‘‘abhitthavitvā padumuttarāhaṃ, pasan‍nacittau asamaṃ sayambhuṃ|

na gacchi kappāni apāyabhūmiṃ, sataṃ sahassāni balaina tassa||

222.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

223.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

224.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā abhayau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

abhayatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. laumasakaṅgiyatthairaapadānaṃ

225.

 ‘‘imamhi bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

226.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ candanau caiva, pabbajitvāna sāsanai|

āpāṇakauṭikaṃ dhammaṃ, pūrayitvāna sāsanai||

227.

 ‘‘tatau cutā santusitaṃ, upapan‍nā ubhau mayaṃ|

tattha dibbaihi nac‍caihi, gītaihi vāditaihi ca||

228.

 ‘‘rūpādidasahaṅgaihi, abhibhautvāna saisakai|

yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna, anubhautvā mahāsukhaṃ||

229.

 ‘‘tatau cavitvā tidasaṃ, candanau upapaj‍jatha|

ahaṃ kapilavatthusmiṃ, ajāyiṃ sākiyatrajau||

230.

 ‘‘yadā udāyitthairaina, ajjhiṭṭhau laukanāyakau|

anukampiya sakyānaṃ, upaisi kapilavhayaṃ||

231.

 ‘‘tadātimāninau  sakyā, na buddhassa guṇañ‍ñunau|

paṇamanti na sambuddhaṃ, jātithaddhā anādarā||

232.

 ‘‘taisaṃ  saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, ākāsai caṅkamī jinau|

paj‍jun‍nau viya vassittha, paj‍jalittha yathā sikhī||

233.

 ‘‘dassaitvā rūpamatulaṃ, puna antaradhāyatha|

aikaupi hutvā bahudhā, ahausi punaraikakau||

234.

 ‘‘andhakāraṃ  pakāsañ‍ca, dassayitvā anaikadhā|

pāṭihairaṃ karitvāna, vinayī ñātakai muni||

235.

 ‘‘cātuddīpau mahāmaighau, tāvadaiva pavassatha|

tadā hi jātakaṃ buddhau, vaissantaramadaisayi||

236.

 ‘‘tadā tai khattiyā sabbai, nihantvā jātijaṃ madaṃ|

upaisuṃ saraṇaṃ buddhaṃ, āha suddhaudanau tadā||

237.

 ‘‘‘idaṃ tatiyaṃ tava bhūripañ‍ña, pādāni vandāmi samantacakkhu|

yadābhijātau pathavī pakampayī, yadā ca taṃ naj‍jahi jambuchāyā’||

238.

 ‘‘tadā buddhānubhāvaṃ taṃ, disvā vimhitamānasau|

pabbajitvāna tatthaiva, nivasiṃ mātupūjakau||

239.

 ‘‘candanau daivaputtau maṃ, upagantvānupucchatha|

bhaddaikarattassa tadā, saṅkhaipavitthāraṃ nayaṃ||

240.

 ‘‘cauditauhaṃ  tadā taina, upaic‍ca naranāyakaṃ|

bhaddaikarattaṃ sutvāna, saṃviggau vanamāmakau||

241.

 ‘‘tadā mātaramapucchiṃ, vanai vacchāmi aikakau|

sukhumālauti mai mātā, vārayī taṃ tai (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) tadā vacaṃ||

242.

 ‘‘kāsaṃ dabbaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kusaṃ pauṭakilaṃ, usīraṃ muñ‍japabbajaṃ muñ‍jababbajaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)|

urasā panudissāmi, vivaikamanubrūhayaṃ||

243.

 ‘‘tadā vanaṃ paviṭṭhauhaṃ, saritvā jinasāsanaṃ|

bhaddaikarattaauvādaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

244.

 ‘‘‘atītaṃ  nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

245.

 ‘‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

246.

 ‘‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ saṅkaraṃ (ka॰) taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

247.

 ‘‘‘aivaṃvihāriṃ  ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai bhaddaikarattauti, santau ācikkhatai muni’||

248.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

249.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

250.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā laumasakaṅgiyau laumasaṅkhiyau (syā॰ ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

laumasakaṅgiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vanavacchatthairaapadānaṃ

251.

 ‘‘imamhi bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

252.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ pabbajitvāna, tassa buddhassa sāsanai|

yāvajīvaṃ caritvāna, brahmacāraṃ tatau cutau||

253.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

254.

 ‘‘tatau cutau arañ‍ñamhi, kapautau āsahaṃ tahiṃ|

vasatai guṇasampan‍nau, bhikkhu jhānaratau sadā||

255.

 ‘‘maittacittau kāruṇikau, sadā pamuditānanau|

upaikkhakau mahāvīrau, appamañ‍ñāsu kauvidau||

256.

 ‘‘vinīvaraṇasaṅkappai, sabbasattahitāsayai|

visaṭṭhau naciraināsiṃ, tasmiṃ sugatasāvakai||

257.

 ‘‘upaic‍ca  pādamūlamhi, nisin‍nassa tadāssamai|

kadāci sāmisaṃ daiti, dhammaṃ daisaisi caikadā||

258.

 ‘‘tadā  vipulapaimaina, upāsitvā jinatrajaṃ|

tatau cutau gatau saggaṃ, pavāsau sagharaṃ yathā||

259.

 ‘‘saggā  cutau manussaisu, nibbattau puñ‍ñakammunā|

agāraṃ chaḍḍayitvāna, pabbajiṃ bahusau ahaṃ||

260.

 ‘‘samaṇau  tāpasau vippau, paribbajau tathaivahaṃ|

hutvā vasiṃ arañ‍ñamhi, anaikasatasau ahaṃ||

261.

 ‘‘pacchimai ca bhavai dāni, rammai kapilavatthavai|

vacchagauttau dijau tassa, jāyāya ahamauk‍kamiṃ||

262.

 ‘‘mātu mai dauhaḷau āsi, tiraukucchigatassa mai|

jāyamānasamīpamhi, vanavāsāya nicchayau||

263.

 ‘‘tatau mai ajanī mātā, ramaṇīyai vanantarai|

gabbhatau nikkhamantaṃ maṃ, kāsāyaina paṭiggahuṃ||

264.

 ‘‘tatau kumārau siddhatthau, jātau sakyakuladdhajau|

tassa mittau piyau āsiṃ, saṃvisaṭṭhau sumāniyau||

265.

 ‘‘sattasāraibhinikkhantai, auhāya vipulaṃ yasaṃ|

ahampi pabbajitvāna, himavantamupāgamiṃ||

266.

 ‘‘vanālayaṃ bhāvanīyaṃ, kassapaṃ dhutavādikaṃ|

disvā sutvā jinuppādaṃ, upaisiṃ narasārathiṃ||

267.

 ‘‘sau mai dhammamadaisaisi, sabbatthaṃ sampakāsayaṃ|

tatauhaṃ pabbajitvāna, vanamaiva punāgamaṃ punāgamiṃ (sī॰ pī॰), punauk‍kamaṃ (syā॰)||

268.

 ‘‘tatthāppamattau  viharaṃ, chaḷabhiñ‍ñā aphassayiṃ apassayiṃ (syā॰ ka॰)|

ahau suladdhalābhaumhi, sumittainānukampitau||

269.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

270.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

271.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vanavacchau thairau imā gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

vanavacchatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. cūḷasugandhatthairaapadānaṃ

272.

 ‘‘imamhi  bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

273.

 ‘‘anubyañ‍janasampan‍nau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau|

byāmappabhāparivutau, raṃsijālasamautthaṭau||

274.

 ‘‘assāsaitā yathā candau, sūriyauva pabhaṅkarau|

nibbāpaitā yathā maighau, sāgarauva guṇākarau||

275.

 ‘‘dharaṇīriva sīlaina, himavāva samādhinā|

ākāsau viya pañ‍ñāya, asaṅgau anilau yathā||

276.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ  bārāṇasiyaṃ, upapan‍nau mahākulai|

pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñasmiṃ, nānāratanasañ‍cayai||

277.

 ‘‘mahatā parivāraina, nisin‍naṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

upaic‍ca dhammamassausiṃ, amataṃva manauharaṃ||
